Machine-facing technical metadata uses canonical qualified names or typed references only.

Required rules:
  - Owners/Dependencies/Read/Write/Maps-to fields use canonical qualified names.

  anchor.

PARENT-FLOW RULE
Every detailed contract maps upward to one or more [S-*] system-flow nodes and downward to [A-*] canonical definitions;
theory/reference-architecture semantics may additionally resolve to [TH-*]/[RA-*] in the companion source.
No detailed owner may redefine the system architecture, create authority by local naming, or bypass Annexure-owned
invariants/proofs/registries.

CURRENT DETAILED-SPECIFICATION RULE

The sections below are current lower-level design detail. They are subordinate to the Human Sovereignty Core, GSL
type/epistemic discipline, universal algebras, AAP hard-gate selection, Proof result semantics and current canonical
registries. A lower-level profile may strengthen a constraint within its domain but cannot weaken an applicable
higher-level hard constraint.

DETAILED SPECIFICATION EXPLANATION CONTRACT

Every detailed specification below is interpreted as an explanation-backed technical contract.

For each specification:
  Role / Principle          answers WHY.
  State / Artifacts         answer WHAT is represented.
  Functions / Pipeline      answer HOW.
  Preconditions / Inputs    answer what must be true/available.
  Postconditions / Outputs  answer what is produced or changed.
  Invariants / Hard rules   answer what cannot be violated.
  Dependencies              answer what it relies on and affects.
  Proof/Evidence bindings   answer how applicable claims are supported.
  Failure / terminal states answer what happens under uncertainty, invalidity or resource/fault conditions.
  Update protocol           answers how the object changes over time.
  Explanation bindings      allow GSL-EXPLAIN to reconstruct the human-readable WHY/WHAT/HOW/WHY_NOT/WHAT_IF trace.

Where an older detailed block lacks one explicit field, the field is inherited from its typed dependencies and
cross-cutting contracts only when that inheritance is machine-resolvable; otherwise it is PENDING/UNKNOWN and becomes a
conformance finding. Silence is never interpreted as success.

Schema references: SCHEMA-BBDF39E4A1 — DetailedSpecificationExplanation..

SECTION III-A — GSL SEMANTIC FOUNDATION — DETAILED SPECIFICATION
SemanticAnchor: [T-GSL]

2.1 CANONICAL ONTOLOGY

Six Pillars
 Existence · Change · Agency · Law · Value · Frame

Ten Core Objects
 TIME = instant/interval, ordering, validity and actual/simulation branch.
 SPACE = coordinates/region/reference system/parent space.
 THING = universal semantic root with stable identity/name/type/version.
 EVENT = occurrence in the modeled world/state-transition history. An ObservationRecord or AttestedObservation is an
 ARTIFACT that may reference an EVENT; recording or observing an occurrence does not become the occurrence itself.
 ACTION = intentional state-changing operation with actor/preconditions/status.
 AGENCY = actor/authority/capability/delegation/accountability semantics.
 RULE = tiered condition → conclusion/constraint.
 VALUE = semantic Fact | Preference | Norm. Representation is orthogonal; VALUE is not universally a Decimal.
 CONTEXT = frame containing scope, risk class, active rules, environment and interpretation conditions.
 CLAIM = assertion with subject/predicate/object, evidence references, disposition, confidence?,
 provenance, validity_scope, and version. Claim is the primary unit of epistemic state.

Schema references: SCHEMA-CFD2F543E6 — Claim..
THING subtype registry
 ENTITY = Person | Organization | Community | Locality | Device | Infrastructure | Garden |
 extensible typed descendants.
 ARTIFACT = Document | Dataset | Model | Software | Media | Contract | Credential | Proof | WorkPackage |
 TheoryArtifact | LanguageArtifact | Observation | extensible typed descendants.

Invariant references: ONT-001..004.
HumanCore.DelegatedAgent means the AI agent acting for the user. GSL AGENCY is the semantic/ontological object; Agent ≠
Agency.

2.2 CURRENT 24 CORE RELATIONS

Foundational (10)
 identifies, causes, governs, values, frames, acts, obeys, assesses, contextualizes, controls

Practical / lineage (14)
 partOf, dependsOn, owns, delegates, references, derivedFrom, equivalentTo, contradicts,
 supports, blocks, hasHypothesis, supersedes, conflictsWith, originatesFrom

Key algebraic rules
 equivalentTo symmetric + transitive; cycles permitted.
 partOf transitive + antisymmetric; cycles forbidden.
 dependsOn is kind-qualified: build/order dependencies are transitive over the admitted build DAG and cycles are forbidden; runtime/semantic dependencies may contain an explicitly declared strongly-connected feedback/fixed-point group only under registered semantics, admissible initialization, and required termination/service-liveness evidence. Antisymmetry applies to the acyclic/condensed dependency order, not raw nodes inside an admitted feedback SCC.
 derivedFrom directed lineage; cycles forbidden.
 controls authority/control edge; cycles forbidden.
 supersedes directional/version aware; cycles forbidden; predecessor remains immutable.
 originatesFrom directed provenance; cycles forbidden.
 contradicts symmetric semantic opposition; cycles permitted.
 conflictsWith symmetric declared conflict; cycles permitted, explicit resolution required.
 causes feedback cycles permitted.
 references cycles permitted.
 delegates capability(delegate) ⊆ capability(principal).
 supports does not by itself promote epistemic status.

Canonical composition candidates include:
 delegates ∘ acts → acts
 owns ∘ controls → derivedControl
 supports ∘ supports → candidate indirect support; it becomes supports only through an explicit valid warrant.
 causes ∘ causes → causalPath; it becomes a causal Claim only under a valid causal model, compatible
 context, and declared assumptions.
 hasHypothesis ∘ assesses → evaluatedHypothesis
All undeclared normative compositions raise CompositionError or require an explicit domain rule. Non-core
lineage/domain vocabulary does not silently become one of the 24 Core Relations.

Invariant references: REL-ALG-001..002.
2.3 TWELVE GSL CORE SEMANTIC PRIMITIVES + TWO GARDEN EXECUTION-CONTROL PRIMITIVES

STATUS: NORMATIVE — CROSS-CUTTING SEMANTIC / EXECUTION-CONTROL REGISTRY

GSL v45.1 defines twelve core semantic primitive families. Garden uses COMPARE and REASON as universal
execution-control primitives expressed using existing GSL semantics. The Garden-level total is therefore fourteen,
without changing the ten Core Objects, 24 Core Relations, §12.4 grammar or GSL v45.1 release identity. These
primitives govern how typed payloads are observed, transformed, authorized, compared, reasoned about, stored and
executed; they create no competing top-level object.

1. STATE
 S(t) + Event + Action → S(t+1).

Schema references: SCHEMA-3583B93887 — DeterministicResolutionContext..
 Determinism is conditional on the declared context and inputs. Missing nondeterministic physical or
 external inputs are modeled explicitly; they are not silently replaced by a seed.

2. OBSERVATION
 Observation ≠ Evidence ≠ Claim ≠ Knowledge.

 ObservationSource = SensorAttested(SensorAttestationRef) | Human | Document | System | Model.
 A sensor attestation binds device identity, calibration, time, configuration, integrity, and
 provenance.

3. AUTHORITY
 Entity → Identity → Credential → Role → Capability → Authority → Action.

 Capability does not self-create authority. Delegation is scoped, expiring, revocable, auditable,
 and no broader than the principal’s legitimate authority.

4. RESOURCE
 Request → Reserve → Allocate → Consume → Release → Reconcile.

 Forecast Resource ≠ Receivable Resource ≠ Liquid Resource ≠ Allocated Resource ≠
 Committed Resource ≠ Spent Resource ≠ Recovered Resource.

5. ADDRESS
 garden://{namespace}/{type}/{instance}/{version} + content hash.

 Human-readable address, semantic version, validity scope, and immutable content identity are
 separately represented.

6. PROVENANCE
 Source + DirectedLineageGraph + cryptographic roots/anchors.

 Provenance is a typed graph, not a single source string. It supports backward explanation,
 forward impact analysis, privacy-aware disclosure, and cryptographic/institutional attestation.

7. CAUSALITY
 C0 Correlation | C1 Temporal | C2 Mechanistic | C3 Intervention | C4 Counterfactual.

 No higher level is inferred from a lower one without the declared causal model, assumptions,
 evidence, identification conditions, and uncertainty.

8. DEPENDENCY
 Build DAG | Runtime dependency | Semantic dependency.

 Cycles require an explicit permitted semantics, fixed-point/feedback interpretation where
 applicable, and termination/liveness proof. Undeclared cycles are rejected.

9. CONFLICT
 Argument | Authority | Governance | CRDT | HardcodedFallback.

 Conflict is typed and auditable. SAME, REFINEMENT, COMPATIBLE, CONTEXT_DIFFERENT, APPROXIMATION,
 TRUE_CONTRADICTION, and UNRESOLVED are distinct results. UNRESOLVED is valid.

10. UNCERTAINTY
 Epistemic (potentially reducible) versus aleatoric (model-irreducible within the declared model).

 Neither uncertainty type weakens K0P or Constitution. Representation follows the Mathematical
 Representation Policy; interval, distribution, vector, tensor, symbolic, and domain-specific
 forms are not silently collapsed to one confidence number.

11. DECISION
 Recommendation | Authorization | Commitment | Execution.

 The stages are not interchangeable. Recommendation ≠ Authorization. Authorization without a
 valid commitment/execution path is not execution. Execution does not prove the recommendation
 was epistemically correct.

12. VALIDITY
 Version = semantic revision identity. Validity = time/context/scope.

 Version and validity are orthogonal. The same version may have different validity scopes; a new
 version does not automatically invalidate all predecessor uses.

13. COMPARE — GARDEN EXECUTION-CONTROL PRIMITIVE
 COMPARE(A, B, dimensions, context) → ComparisonResult.

 Compare performs typed difference, similarity, dominance, conflict, materiality and uncertainty analysis. It is used
 before consequential selection where alternatives/targets exist and after effectful transitions to compare expected
 and observed state. Comparison never creates truth, evidence, authority or permission merely by ranking candidates.

 Comparison may evaluate actual vs expected/desired state; candidate vs candidate; claim vs evidence; prediction vs
 observation; action vs constraints; cost vs benefit; risk vs permissible risk; current vs alternative design; new vs
 existing knowledge; and result vs success condition.

 Schema reference: SCHEMA-2D736A85E2 — ComparisonResult.

14. REASON — GARDEN EXECUTION-CONTROL PRIMITIVE
 REASON(problem, context, candidates, tier) → ReasoningTrace | InferenceResult | UNKNOWN.

 Reason determines what follows, what alternatives exist, what should be selected within legitimate constraints, and
 what further evidence or verification is required. It is logically present at every meaningful transition, while AAP
 selects R0..R4 depth. Reasoning remains distinct from Proof, Evidence, Authorization and Execution.

RELATION TO THE 10 CORE OBJECTS
The 10 Core Objects (TIME, SPACE, THING, EVENT, ACTION, AGENCY, RULE, VALUE, CONTEXT, CLAIM) are the atomic payload
families. The twelve GSL v45.1 primitives plus the two Garden execution-control primitives govern how those payloads are
created, observed, transformed, stored, authorized, allocated, compared and reasoned about. Every Garden operation uses
one or more primitive families through UPA/GTF profiles.

Invariant references: PRIM-001..003.

Canonical definitions: Annexure A.

2.7 EFFECT / TYPE / EPISTEMIC SYSTEM
Closed effect dimensions:
 Computational, Safety, Auth/Authority, Privacy, Temporal, Resource, Uncertainty, Continuity, Optimization.
Conflicting effects produce INFEASIBLE rather than arbitrary compromise. Effect ordering is compile-time enforced.

Epistemic basis:
 Formal | Empirical | Statistical | Testimonial | Inference | Hypothesis.
Disposition:
 PROVEN | VERIFIED | SUPPORTED | UNVERIFIED | RETRACTED | CONTRADICTED | UNKNOWN.
UNKNOWN subtypes:
 CONFLICT_UNKNOWN | EVIDENCE_UNKNOWN | UNDECIDABLE | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | STALE_UNKNOWN |
 MODEL_INVALID_UNKNOWN | SCOPE_UNDEFINED_UNKNOWN.
UNKNOWN ≠ NOT_APPLICABLE ≠ FALSE ≠ SAFE.

Evidence merge considers disposition, strength, independence, method, scope, freshness and provenance. Correlated
agreement is not independent confirmation. SCC/circular-support analysis prevents a mutually supporting cycle with no
independent grounding from self-promoting.

2.7A FORMAL TYPE, EFFECT, SCOPE, AUTHORITY AND REFERENCE SYSTEM

Judgment form:
  Γ ; Δ ; A ; C ⊢ e : T ! ε

where Γ is the value/type environment, Δ the dependency/schema environment, A the authority/capability environment,
C the context/DesignEpoch binding, T the result type and ε the closed effect vector.

Core rules:
- Every canonical field has a declared type or a TypeResolutionEntry; unresolved names are compile errors.
- Ref<T> is a typed content-addressable reference carrying target kind, immutable identity/hash, version, validity,
  DesignEpoch and provenance. EvidenceRef, ProofRef, TheoryRef, ActionRef and similar names are aliases of Ref<T>.
- Subtyping is explicit nominal/refinement subtyping. Scope widening, authority widening and epistemic promotion are not
  ordinary subtyping operations.
- Effect dimensions are Computational, Safety, Authority, Privacy, Temporal, Resource, Uncertainty, Continuity and
  Optimization. Composition joins effects and preserves the most restrictive applicable hard requirement.
- Authority is capability- and scope-based. Capability possession does not prove legitimacy; execution still requires
  Constitution/Rights, K0P, authenticity, authorization, consent and policy gates where applicable.
- Bool is used only for total deterministic predicates with complete true/false semantics. Epistemic, proof, safety,
  policy, compliance, migration and distributed outcomes use declared multi-status algebras.
- Money, rates, physical quantities, probability, risk, intervals/distributions and calibrated measurements use semantic
  types. Raw machine floating point is an implementation representation under Represented<T>, never a universal type.
- Generated aliases, enums and owner-qualified domain types resolve through the Artifact Catalogue, machine-projection
  generation rules and the generated TypeResolutionRegistry.
- Type/effect preservation, progress or explicit terminal status, reference integrity, authority non-amplification and
  dependency closure are proof obligations for the admitted compiler profile.

Schema references: SCHEMA-2AFAC82F5D — GSLTypeSystemProfile.; SCHEMA-E1FBF0D84D — TypeResolutionEntry.;
                   SCHEMA-5FE2DD0C69 — TypeResolutionRegistry.

2.8 CORE ALGEBRAS
Schema references: SCHEMA-EE8B487BB1 — Transition.; SCHEMA-4A4A42095B — Contract..
Outcome = Success | Rejected | Infeasible | Timeout | Unknown | Conflict | Failure | Quarantined | Escalated
Schema references: SCHEMA-F89319FBC1 — Epistemic.; SCHEMA-D28FD57947 — Temporal..
Formal semantics are represented through three mutually checked views:
 Op : operational semantics (state, expression) → (state', value)
 Den : denotational mapping Type → mathematical object (set/probability/Hilbert/etc.)
 Ax : axiomatic/Hoare-style discrete, probabilistic and continuous contracts.
Target D1: operational, denotational and axiomatic semantics agree on the declared fragment; proof discharge remains
pending where not established.

2.9 MATHEMATICAL REPRESENTATION POLICY — CURRENT NORMALIZED FORM
Core rule: representation ≠ semantic meaning.

Schema references: SCHEMA-C222A09D1A — Represented<T>..
LogOdds = semantic probability representation only.
LogEvidence = evidence-strength representation; it does not become probability unless a valid model maps it.
LogRisk = domain risk representation with declared scale/model.

Invariant references: GSL-MATH-001..005.
RECURSIVE TYPING PRECEDENCE RULE
Canonical typed semantics override shorthand in detailed prose/profile examples. A raw Float, raw Bool, untyped vector,
or generic Bytes occurrence in a human-readable detailed schema is not permission to use that representation as the
machine semantic type. The machine projection MUST resolve it to the applicable MoneyAmount, ExactDecimal, Rational,
Quantity<Unit,Frame>, Probability, Rate<Unit>, RiskBound, Interval/Distribution/Tensor/Symbolic representation,
multi-status result algebra, typed payload, or other declared domain type before certification. Epistemic, proof,
safety, authority, policy and compliance states MUST NOT collapse to Bool where UNKNOWN/INCONCLUSIVE/BLOCKED/STALE or
other declared outcomes are semantically possible. Any unresolved shorthand is a machine-package conformance failure,
not a competing definition of GSL.

2.10 GSL COMPILATION / GRAMMAR
Human / External Source
 → GSL semantic parsing
 → AST
 → Type / Effect / Dependency analysis
 → CIR
 → Backend / code generation
 → Binary / runtime representation
 → Proof / Safety / Policy gates
 → Execution

Compiler triad: independently implemented Haskell / Rust / OCaml frontends/backends MUST agree on CIR for the declared
consensus profile; divergence → HALT/INVESTIGATE.

Normative grammar authority:
 §12.4 contains the sole current compiler-facing GSL grammar and lexical/precedence contract for this master.
 This section defines the compilation pipeline only. No second local program production is normative.
 Obsolete or external syntax requires explicit offline migration before current compilation/execution.

2.11 CUBE × RING — EXPANDED CURRENT MODEL

GardenState(t) ∈ Cube × Ring.

The construction is a semantic/state representation and not an additional architecture object, physical claim, or claim
that all
reality is literally a cube or algebraic ring.

Cube — simultaneous structured dimensions
 C1 Existence/Thing : entities, artifacts, identity, composition, state.
 C2 Space/Location : coordinate frame, region, topology, containment, adjacency.
 C3 Agency/Authority : actors, roles, capability, delegation, accountability.
 C4 Rule/Constraint : Constitution, law, policy, contract, invariant, proof obligation.
 C5 Value/Epistemic : fact/preference/norm, objective, evidence, uncertainty, status.
 C6 Context/Scope : domain, locale, environment, risk, branch, DesignEpoch, validity.

Ring — ordered continuity and change
 R1 Time : instants, intervals, clocks, logical/causal order, expiry.
 R3 Action : authorized operation and declared effects.
 R4 Transition : pre-state, delta, post-state, obligations, rollback/compensation.
 R5 Recurrence : schedules, periodic review, bounded loops, fixed points.
 R6 Causal progression : mechanisms, interventions, counterfactual branches, descendant effects.

Core operators
 project(Cube, dimensions, scope) → scoped state view
 join(view_a, view_b, conflict_policy) → merged view | CONFLICT | UNRESOLVED
 advance(state, transition, DRC) → next state + receipts
 replay(genesis, commits, environment) → reconstructed state | mismatch
 branch(state, delta) → immutable alternative history
 compare(trajectories, objectives) → dominance | incomparability | unresolved
 fold(events, transition_fn) → bounded accumulated state
 trace(effect, lineage_graph) → causes, dependencies, authority, provenance

Temporal/causal relationship
 S[n+1] = T[n](S[n]) is the discrete ledger view.
 For continuous domains, a declared flow Φ over an interval may update state, but its sampled events,
 assumptions, solver/calibration configuration, and approximation error are bound into the Ring.
Temporal precedence is not causality; causal promotion follows the C0–C4 ladder.

PROJECTION CORRESPONDENCE CONTRACT
Cube × Ring is a typed view over the authoritative Core Object graph, not a second ontology and not automatically a
global isomorphism. A declared fragment may claim lossless correspondence only when a machine-verifiable proof records:
 source graph and registry roots;
 fragment/scope and DesignEpoch;
 Core Object and relation coverage;
 forward and reverse mappings;
 round-trip preservation laws;
 alias/duplication and omission checks;
 information-loss/approximation bounds;
 unresolved or intentionally non-representable content.

Schema references: SCHEMA-723F655FA6 — ProjectionCorrespondenceProof.
Failure of correspondence blocks representation substitution for the affected scope; the authoritative typed graph
remains controlling. A lossy projection may still be used when its loss is explicit and admissible for the requested
purpose.
Invariant references: CXR-001..012.
2.12 Φ BRIDGE — PERCEPTION → GSL
Normalized operational profile:
Raw sensory input
 → AttestedObservation(sensor/device/human identity, calibration, time, environment, integrity, provenance)
 → immutable raw or declared loss-bounded observation retention
 → deadline-class routing
 FAST_SAFETY: bounded local features feed K0P without waiting for rich multimodal fusion
 EPISTEMIC: dependency-aware multimodal fusion under backpressure/resource/deadline budgets
 → feature and hypothesis extraction
 → candidate subject/predicate/object bindings
 → alternative hypotheses + unmodeled remainder
 → localization/synchronization where spatial
 → uncertainty propagation + calibration check
 → provenance/attestation binding
 → ClaimCandidate
 → theory/model applicability routing
 → VALIDATE | UNKNOWN | REJECT/QUARANTINE
 → Knowledge ingress only under K-INT rules.

Phi(Sensory_Input) → ClaimCandidate {
 subject, predicate, object,
 confidence ∈ [0,1]?,
 alternatives: [{hypothesis, confidence}],
 unmodeled_mass,
 calibration_refs, timestamps, localization?, uncertainty,
 provenance
}
Σ alternative confidence ≤ 1; remainder is explicit unmodeled alternative space. A perception model may propose a Claim;
it may not manufacture validated Evidence. Fusion deadline expiry returns PARTIAL/UNKNOWN with a receipt; it never
delays the independent L0 safety path or presents a partial fused frame as complete.

2.13 METACOGNITION
Observation → Pattern → Hypothesis → Principle → Theory → Framework. Promotion requires explicit validation at each
boundary. Survival under falsification ≠ proof.

2.14 BIOMIMETIC & NATURAL DESIGN PATTERNS

STATUS: NORMATIVE — ARCHITECTURAL DESIGN-REVIEW PRINCIPLES
PURPOSE: Record the natural-system patterns that motivate and test Garden mechanisms.

Garden is engineered, but many of its architectural choices deliberately reuse patterns that have demonstrated
persistence, adaptation, coordination, and repair in natural systems. The mapping is analogical: biology inspires design
obligations but does not prove that a Garden implementation is safe, optimal, alive, or empirically valid.

NATURAL-PATTERN REVIEW CARDS

  HOMEOSTASIS
    Maps to    : Capability.Trust.Safety, Capability.Compute.Runtime, Domain.Society.Economics.
    Requires   : K0P boundaries, feedback regulation, resource/liquidity equilibrium, bounded degradation and recovery.

  IMMUNE SYSTEM
    Maps to    : Capability.Trust.Security, Engine.Compliance, Engine.Evolve.
    Requires   : Anomaly detection, quarantine, incident response, memory, repair and adaptive defense without
                 autoimmune or self-confirming behavior.

  REDUNDANCY
    Maps to    : Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric, Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Proof.
    Requires   : Replication, independent verification, applicable consensus, Byzantine/common-mode analysis and
                 immutable logs.

  STIGMERGY
    Maps to    : Engine.Search, Capability.State.EventService, Engine.Evolve.
    Requires   : Indirect coordination through typed environmental/event traces, caches, reinforcement/decay and
                 provenance.

  SWARM INTELLIGENCE
    Maps to    : HumanCore.DelegatedAgent, Capability.Authority.Governance, Engine.Reason.
    Requires   : Distributed proposals and authorized quorum/argumentation; collective agreement cannot manufacture
                 truth or rights.

  EPIGENETICS
    Maps to    : Engine.Evolve, Context, Engine.Reason.
    Requires   : Versioned context-dependent expression/configuration and inheritance without silent semantic change.

  NEURAL PLASTICITY
    Maps to    : Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Reason, Engine.Research.
    Requires   : Continuous revalidation, calibrated learning, dependency-aware adaptation and reversible/reviewable
                 reconfiguration.

  AUTOPOIESIS
    Maps to    : Engine.Evolve, Capability.Resilience.Continuity, System Integrity.
    Requires   : Bounded self-maintenance, repair and recursive regulation while trust roots remain externally
                 protected.

Invariant references: BIO-001..004.
These principles are not optional as review questions. Their concrete implementation remains domain-specific and must
pass ordinary applicability, proof, evidence, safety and conformance gates.

2.15 THE DOUBLE HELIX PATTERN — INTERTWINED DUALITY AND SELF-CORRECTION

STATUS: NORMATIVE — CROSS-CUTTING ARCHITECTURAL PRINCIPLE

ARCHITECTURAL PRINCIPLE
PURPOSE: Provide a unifying conceptual pattern for complementary, mutually checking processes.

The Double Helix is a structural pattern present throughout Garden. It is a metaphor/formal-design heuristic, not a
claim that Garden literally has DNA or that every pair is symmetric.

DOUBLE-HELIX PAIR CARDS

  USER-FACING SOVEREIGN LAYER  ↔  GARDEN SYSTEM
    Relation : They interact and co-evolve while the person remains a rights-holder, not a system component.

  KNOWLEDGE  ↔  REASON
    Relation : Knowledge stores and versions epistemic state; Reason transforms/tests it without manufacturing Evidence.

  EVOLVE  ↔  PROOF
    Relation : Evolution proposes/repairs; Proof verifies declared formal obligations; neither self-certifies.

  CONSTITUTION  ↔  LAW
    Relation : Constitutional rights constrain legal interpretation and evolution.

  SAFETY  ↔  EMERGENCY
    Relation : Safety enforces hard boundaries; Emergency uses separately authorized bounded paths and never bypasses
               Constitution/K0P.

  TIME SERVICE  ↔  EVENT SERVICE
    Relation : Time orders and scopes; Events record occurrences with clock/provenance bindings.

  INTENT  ↔  POLICY
    Relation : Intent expresses desired outcomes; Policy constrains legitimate action without becoming truth.

Formalization candidates include dual objects/pairs, adjunctions where mappings genuinely satisfy the required laws,
product structures, and the Cube × Ring recurrence model. Dual numbers may represent local sensitivity in applicable
mathematical profiles. These are candidate formalisms, not automatically the formal underpinning of every pair; each use
requires an explicit mapping and proof/validation.

Invariant references: DHX-001..004.
The Double Helix pattern explains why Garden components are interwoven, complementary and self-correcting while
preserving typed boundaries and independent verification.

SECTION III-B — PROCESS, TRANSITION AND STATE ARCHITECTURE — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

SemanticAnchor: [T-AAP]

AAP TECHNICAL BINDING
OperationProfile -> ActivationPlan + AssurancePlan -> Engine.Orchestrate -> ExecutionAdmissionToken.
The detailed owner contracts that AAP activates are defined in [T-CAPABILITIES]; assurance/proof contracts are in
[T-PROOF]. System semantics and tier selection are defined in [S-AAP].

SemanticAnchor: [T-PROCESS]

3.1 UNIVERSAL PROCESS ALGEBRA (UPA)

Purpose: replace many duplicated, hard-coded linear lifecycle engines with one typed compositional process semantics.
Incident, research, prediction, verification, conformance, deployment, transition, evolution, legal CI/CD, epistemic
revalidation and multi-agent review are UPA profiles—not separate universal process architectures.

Schema references: SCHEMA-FBF99D1B6F — UniversalProcess..
Operators:
 SEQUENCE(A,B) ordered composition.
 BRANCH(c,A,B) typed conditional path.
 PARALLEL({P_i}) concurrent work with declared join semantics.
 COMPOSE(A,B) contract/effect/authority-aware composition.
 RETRY(P,bound) bounded retry; never unbounded hidden recursion.
 ROLLBACK(P) revert reversible state using certified pre-state.
 COMPENSATE(P) recovery action where literal rollback is physically impossible.
 RECONSTRUCT(x) rebuild a causal/provenance path only from content-addressed events whose original
 signatures, hardware attestations, membership epoch and environment bindings are
 re-verified. Unverified records remain SIMULATED/UNTRUSTED and cannot recreate authority.
 ITERATE(P,k) bounded iteration.
 RECURSE(P,contract) recursively invoke only under TerminationContract.
 TERMINATE(reason) close with explicit reason/status.
 DO_NOTHING first-class candidate/action.

Current normalized P0–P11 lifecycle used by this master:
 P0 RECEIVE / PROPOSE
 P1 UNDERSTAND / SEMANTIC-NORMALIZE
 P2 DISCOVER / APPLICABILITY
 P3 IMPACT / DEPENDENCIES
 P4 EPISTEMICS / EVIDENCE
 P5 PREDICT CONSEQUENCES
 P6 RESOURCE PLAN / ADMISSION
 P7 ASSURE / VERIFY / TEST
 P8 AUTHORITY / GOVERNANCE / CONSENT
 P9 PREPARE / EXECUTE / COMMIT
 P10 OBSERVE / VALIDATE / RECONSTRUCT
 P11 LEARN / RECURSE / TERMINATE
Conditional paths may skip inapplicable stages only through declared contracts; a skipped stage is not silently
considered passed.

Invariant references: UPA-001..015.
Schema references: SCHEMA-42B00D89E1 — ProcessProfile..
UNIVERSAL STEP REFINEMENT
Every UPA process node that crosses the meaningful-transition boundary refines to one or more TransitionStep records.
Hierarchical refinement preserves the parent process contract. The child process may increase assurance when new risk,
uncertainty or dependencies appear, but may not silently weaken the parent's hard obligations. Compare and Reason are
therefore universal control semantics inside UPA rather than optional whole-process decorations. AAP determines the
actual tier and architecture closure for each step.

Schema references: SCHEMA-2D736A85E2 — ComparisonResult.; SCHEMA-B23C042B9C — TransitionStep.
Invariant references: PROC-001..021.

3.2 GARDEN TRANSITION FABRIC (GTF)
GTF is the high-assurance UPA profile for consequential Garden state changes.

Schema references: SCHEMA-FC27C1153A — TransitionContract..
Formal change-management human projection:
 Propose → Understand → Impact → Verify → Approve → Commit → Monitor → Rollback/Compensate.
UNDERSTAND is mandatory because an ill-specified change cannot have a trustworthy impact closure.

3.3 CANONICAL GARDEN STATE LEDGER
S[n+1] = T[n](S[n]).
Every canonical state transition has a stable transition identity, inputs, bindings, evidence/proofs, authority and
provenance sufficient for deterministic reconstruction within declared external/environmental assumptions. The ledger is
a causal semantic state lineage—not merely a blockchain or append-only text log.

Schema references: SCHEMA-AE91B553CC — CanonicalStateRecord..
3.4 PROOF-CARRYING CHANGE DELTA (PCCD)
A change carries the obligations created/invalidated by its own semantic delta instead of forcing full-context
reconstruction every time.
Schema references: SCHEMA-9B459E8745 — PCCD..
PCCD is not itself proof; it is the typed carrier telling Proof/conformance what must be proved/rechecked.

3.5 OBLIGATION GRAPH
ObligationGraph nodes/edges cover affected:
 invariants · proof obligations · evidence requirements · dependencies · predictions · resources · safety checks ·
 security checks · governance/authority · human consent · post-commit validation.
Edges encode dependsOn / invalidates / requires / supports / blocks / scope propagation. Impact closure traverses this
graph; Dependency-Local Reverification operates on the affected closure.

3.6 PROOF-CARRYING STATE
Schema references: SCHEMA-B389F66624 — ProofCarryingState..
State ≠ CertifiedState. A stored/deployed state does not become valid merely because it exists.

3.7 TRANSACTIONAL / REVERSIBLE EVOLUTION
Normal high-impact pattern: PREPARE → ISOLATE → VALIDATE → COMMIT → MONITOR. Failure before commit → ABORT. Failure
after reversible commit → ROLLBACK. Irreversible physical effects require a pre-authorized Compensation/Recovery plan;
“rollback” must not falsely promise physical reversibility.

3.8 GARDEN CHANGE CARD — HUMAN VIEW OF A TRANSITION CONTRACT
What changes? · Why? · Affected architecture objects? · Affected invariants? · Expected benefit? · Risks? · Uncertainty?
· Evidence? · Predictions? · Verification? · Authority/consent? · Dissent? · Rollback/compensation? · Current status?
GardenChangeCard is a non-authoritative view and DERIVED_VIEW candidate (current origin SOURCE; qualification
PENDING), not a separate truth source; it is generated from the canonical
TransitionContract/ObligationGraph.

SECTION III-C — EPISTEMIC REVALIDATION AND KNOWLEDGE MAINTENANCE — DETAILED SPECIFICATION
SemanticAnchor: [T-KNOWLEDGE]

4.1 PRINCIPLE
Stored knowledge is never permanently trusted solely because it passed once. Revalidation is triggered by time, new
evidence, changed dependencies, changed environment/model, contradiction, high-impact use or explicit audit.

RevalidationPriority(k,t) is domain-policy controlled and must increase with relevant staleness/exposure rather than
rewarding stale unreviewed knowledge with less scrutiny. A normalized monotone age factor used by this consolidation is:
 AgeFactor_d(a) = 1 + age_weight[d] · ln(1 + a / τ_age[d])
for a≥0, age_weight[d]≥0, τ_age[d]>0. The formula records the current direction: older un-revalidated knowledge rises,
not falls, in review pressure. Domain criticality, change rate, uncertainty and dependency impact may dominate age.

A priority implementation may combine normalized factors such as:
 Priority ∝ Uncertainty × Importance × DependencyImpact × ChangeExposure × AgeFactor × Criticality
subject to bounded, transparent normalization; zeroing one factor must not accidentally suppress a mandatory critical
review.

4.2 OUTCOMES
CONFIRMED | STRENGTHENED | WEAKENED | REFINED | SUPERSEDED | CONTRADICTED | STALE | UNKNOWN | INCONCLUSIVE.
Revalidation changes interpretation/status while preserving the original historical artifact.

4.3 EPI-007…EPI-012 / KNW-016…018
Canonical definitions: Annexure A.
Invariant references: EPI-007..012, KNW-016..018.
4.4 DAILY EPISTEMIC SWEEP + SATURATION MODEL
Cheap Sweep: scan metadata/dependency/status/freshness across the declared knowledge universe. Deep Revalidation: spend
bounded compute on the highest-priority eligible subset. Adaptive cadence: daily / weekly / monthly or domain-specific;
safety-critical domains have a declared minimum cadence even when estimated change rate is low. Budgets: compute,
memory, storage, bandwidth, time and money are explicit; exhaustion yields DEFERRED/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, not “validated”.
Scheduling uses protected critical lanes, per-domain quotas, aging/round-robin fairness, deduplication and retry
backoff. A repeatedly deferred high-cost item retains importance but cannot occupy every next slot; its next eligibility
time and required resource class are explicit. Critical lower-cost work in other domains therefore cannot be starved by
one permanently infeasible item.

Schema references: SCHEMA-218EDDB2D8 — SweepRecord.; SCHEMA-C740DD8953 — SaturationProfile..
Invariant references: EPI-013..018, SWP-001..004.
4.5 EPISTEMIC STABILITY CACHE (ESC)
ESC caches validated epistemic assessments only while KnowledgeSnapshot, EnvironmentCommitment, assumptions,
source/dependency validity and DesignEpoch binding remain valid. Any relevant invalidation evicts/downgrades the entry
before operational reuse.

4.6 THEORY STATUS + THEORY DEPENDENCY GRAPH
Normalized status vocabulary:
 ESTABLISHED | DERIVED | PROPOSED | EXPERIMENTAL | SPECULATIVE | REJECTED.
This status is separate from formal proof status and empirical validation status; mappings may be declared per theory.
TheoryDependencyGraph edges identify assumptions/derivations/reuse. Upstream change → affected downstream theories
become REVALIDATION_REQUIRED; they are not automatically false.

5.4 CONSTITUTIONAL RIGHTS
DEFINED RIGHTS REGISTRY

Registry reference: REG-RIGHTS-001.

5.5 TRUST ROOT / SAFETY SUMMARY
Genesis / immutable physical root
 → OTP/TPM/qualified hardware trust
 → K0P physical safety veto
 → Constitution / active rights registry
 → Proof / proof boundary
 → Compiler / Native / Runtime
 → governed execution.
K0P is necessary, not sufficient: a physically safe action may still fail Constitution, Security, Policy, proof or
consent.

5.6 CANONICAL INVARIANT REGISTRY

only invariant references.

PROOF / ASSURANCE FORMALIZATION CLOSURE

- P6 composition uses CompositionProof, which binds component proofs, interface proof, shared invariants, resource,
  temporal, failure-composition and assumption-compatibility obligations.
- P20 admission-critical requirements use AdmissionCriticalInvariantBinding. BootstrapIntegrityManifest identifies the
  exact invariant/proof set that must discharge before the admitted scope can boot.
- ProofBudgetBinding maps each proof obligation/rung to ProofResourceBudget allocations and terminal behavior; resource
  exhaustion yields an explicit status and never a fabricated proof.
- SMTLegalState explicitly binds LEGAL-SMT-P25. TRAIN profiles bind TRAIN-P21..TRAIN-P24 through the same proof registry
  without redefining bare P identifiers.
- Formal VLP conformance cases are represented by VLPConformanceSuite; executable compiler receipts remain pending in
  the companion machine package.

Schema references: SCHEMA-D723CBC0A6 — CompositionProof.; SCHEMA-65B4DDE09D — AdmissionCriticalInvariantBinding.;
                   SCHEMA-20B90AA979 — ProofBudgetBinding.; SCHEMA-61A2F5DD8C — VLPConformanceSuite.

SECTION III-E — CAPABILITY / ENGINE / DOMAIN CONTRACTS — DETAILED SPECIFICATION
SemanticAnchor: [T-CAPABILITIES]

HUMANCORE.IDENTITY — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Universal Architecture Object Contract
Purpose : Persistent entity identity, authentication, credential history, pseudonyms and
reputation linkage. Inputs : Genesis proof, credentials, signatures, identity events, reputation evidence. Outputs :
EntityRef, authenticated identity, credential status, pseudonym bindings, reputation profile reference. Rules : Entity ≠
Identity; credentials are revocable/versioned; reputation ≠ human worth; identity does not create authority. Failure :
Invalid/revoked credential → DENY + Security event; uncertain binding → UNKNOWN/REVIEW. Dependencies :
Capability.Knowledge, Capability.Trust.Security, HumanCore.Privacy, Domain.Society.Economics,
Capability.State.EventService, Capability.State.TimeService. Why : Every authority, consent, provenance and
accountability path needs a stable, auditable subject. Status : ARCHITECTURALLY SPECIFIED; implementation/proof pending
unless separately established.

Internal State (GSL-typed):
Schema references: SCHEMA-744C789D90 — IdentityState..
EntityKind = Person | Organization | Community | Locality | Device | Infrastructure | Garden.

Schema references: SCHEMA-7FCCE02F14 — Credential.; SCHEMA-2877F9A631 — Pseudonym.; SCHEMA-23EC5B84BA —
                   ReputationProfile..
A single global reputation scalar is non-normative. Historical Karma/Bayesian scalar machinery may be used only as a
declared per-domain implementation profile; it may not represent human worth or override rights.

Functions:
Register(genesis_proof) → EntityRef
 Requires a valid genesis/identity proof; creates immutable entity genesis.
Authenticate(credential, challenge) → GenericResult<Bool>
 Requires ACTIVE, unrevoked, scope/time-valid credential; failed/revoked attempts emit Security events.
Rotate(new_public_key, proof) → Credential
 Previous credential becomes SUPERSEDED; entity history/reputation/provenance survive rotation.
Revoke(entity, reason, authority) → Event
 Only the entity itself or valid delegated/governed authority may revoke within scope; active credentials become
 invalid.
UpdateReputation(evidence_event, domain) → ReputationProfile
 Updates only evidence-supported dimensions; all changes preserve evidence, uncertainty, provenance and appealability.
StasisRequest(bond) → State
 Freezes the applicable active and passive reputation-update profile, including ordinary time decay,
 under the declared policy. Safety revocations, factual corrections and immutable history still apply;
 counterfactual decay may be recorded separately without changing the frozen public value.
ReleaseStasis(authority) → State
 Returns to ACTIVE under the declared policy.

Invariant references: ID-001..006.
Math: M4.CRYPTO signatures/PQC; M3.PR calibrated reputation evidence; M1.GR credential DAG; M3.PV provenance. Proofs:
P2, P3, P4a, P6, P11, P14.

HUMANCORE.PRIVACY — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: User / Epistemic Role:
Enforces consent, data tainting, erasure, and VSS (Verifiable Secret Sharing) escrow.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type PrivacyState = { consent_records:
Map[Entity, List[ConsentRecord]], taint_graph: Map[DataRef, TaintLabel], erasure_receipts: List[ErasureReceipt],
vss_shares: Map[DataRef,
List[VSSShare]], revocation_queue: List[ConsentRevocation], ttl_policy:
TTLPolicy, last_sweep: Time }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-7EE3B3366F — ConsentRecord..

Schema references: SCHEMA-7B5EA2BA07 — TaintLabel.; SCHEMA-5FF7191B1C — ErasureReceipt.; SCHEMA-9BB60DE1C2 — VSSShare..
Functions: GrantConsent(entity: Entity, data: DataRef, purpose: Purpose, scope: Scope) → ConsentRecord Precondition:
Entity must be authenticated (HumanCore.Identity). Postcondition: ConsentRecord appended to state. Data taint set to
PRIVATE. Contract condition: No PII processing without explicit ConsentRecord.

RevokeConsent(consent_id: Hash) → ErasureReceipt Precondition: Entity must be owner or Governance order. Postcondition:
Consent marked revoked. Taint propagation triggers cascade. Action: Schedule key destruction via
Capability.Knowledge.Curation + Capability.Trust.Security.

TaintPropagate(source: DataRef, derived: DataRef) → TaintLabel Rule:
Taint(derived) = MAX(Taint(source_i)) ∪ Taint(transformation). Algebra:
PRIVATE ⊓ PUBLIC = PRIVATE. SENSITIVE ⊕ PUBLIC = SENSITIVE. Contract condition:
Derived data never has lower taint than its source.

Declassify(source, policy, authority) → ReclassificationReceipt
 A source downgrade never silently downgrades a derivative. It invalidates affected derived views,
 models, caches and proofs and schedules dependency-aware re-evaluation against every remaining source,
 purpose, consent and transformation taint.

EmergencyAccess(data_ref: DataRef, quorum_proof: Set[VSSShare]) → Plaintext Precondition: Requires the current versioned
threshold policy, current share epoch, unrevoked guardian credentials and a valid revocation root. Postcondition: Data
decrypted; event logged to Capability.State.EventService as EMERGENCY. Contract condition: Emergency access NEVER
overrides K0P/Constitution (only Privacy).

Update Protocol: ConsentState: REQUESTED → GRANTED → ACTIVE → REVOKED → ERASED. Taint: Input → Propagate → MAX(Source) →
Final. Erasure: Request
→ KeyDestroy → Tombstone → Audit.

Invariant references: PRIV-001..006.
Dependencies & Math: Math: M3.IF (Information Flow), M4.CRYPTO (VSS), M1.A (Capability). Proofs: P2 (Non-Amp), P11
(Info-Flow), P14 (Provenance).

DOMAIN.SOCIETY.ECONOMICS — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: User / Operational Role:
Garden-wide value: wallets, atomic transactions, UBI distribution, exchange, lending.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type EconomyState = { wallets: Map[Entity,
Wallet], assets: Map[AssetID, Asset], transaction_log:
List[Transaction], ubi_ledger: Map[Entity, UBIReceipt], exchange_order_book: List[Order], lending_pool: Map[Entity,
Loan], gas_price: PriceQuote, total_supply: Map[AssetID, MoneyAmount], last_ubi_distribution: Time,
reserve_fund: MoneyAmount, inflation_rate: RateSpec
}

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-3458D45D3D — Wallet..

Schema references: SCHEMA-1E9F5A2157 — Transaction.; SCHEMA-A499D5E338 — Asset.; SCHEMA-233F457F27 — UBIReceipt.;
                   SCHEMA-8D9C4D980D — Loan..
Functions: Transfer(from: Entity, to: Entity, asset: AssetID, amount:
MoneyAmount) → Transaction Precondition: balance(from) ≥ amount. UBI lock not active. Postcondition: Debit from, credit
to.
Atomic (all or nothing).
Operational condition: Sum(debits) = Sum(credits). No negative balance.

DistributeUBI(epoch: UInt) → DistributionManifest Precondition: Called by Capability.State.TimeService scheduler on the
UBI interval with a funded, authorized manifest. Postcondition: idempotent bounded batches produce Merkle- committed UBI
receipts. A failed batch is retried or compensated without retaining the entire population transaction in memory.
Contract condition: cumulative committed distribution ≤ liquid authorized reserve.

Exchange(buy_asset: AssetID, sell_asset: AssetID, amount: MoneyAmount) → Transaction Precondition: Order book has
matching
orders; cryptographic
auction. Postcondition: Atomic swap with price discovery. Contract condition:
Oracle price feed (M3.PR) used for fair valuation.

Lend(borrower: Entity, lender: Entity, amount: MoneyAmount, terms: CreditCertificateTerms)
→ Loan Precondition: terms pass the current risk, affordability, disclosure, authority and applicable
collateral policy. No universal 1.5 collateral constant exists. Postcondition: the obligation and loss waterfall are
registered before funding. Default enters bounded workout, restructuring or resolution; it does not trigger an unbounded
simultaneous liquidation cascade.

Update Protocol: Wallet: Balance_Before → Balance_After (Atomic). UBI:
Scheduled → Distributed → Locked → Unlocked. Loan: Request → Approval → Active → Repaid | Defaulted → Seized.

Invariant references: ECON-001..005, SAF-005.
Dependencies & Math: Deps: HumanCore.Identity, Capability.State.TimeService, Engine.Policy, Domain.Earth.CarbonSystems,
Capability.Compute.Runtime. Math: M4.OPT (Optimization), M3.Game (Game Theory), M1.R (Resource). Proofs: P1 (State
Invariants), P4 (Hard Safety).

PUBLIC BENEFICIAL-OWNERSHIP / PRIVATE-PROFILE BOUNDARY
- Person-specific asset or wealth limits are private Policy/Profile data and never appear in the public
 architecture-object specification. Economy loads them only through an authorized private overlay where applicable.
- The valuation unit/currency and valuation method for any applied policy MUST be explicit in its private
 execution context without being promoted into a public default.
- Ultimate beneficial ownership resolves to a Person or Community. A Company/Organization may hold,
 manage or administer assets legally but is not treated as a sovereign human-equivalent beneficiary.
- Private limits do not redefine the GSL VALUE object, public economic policy or universal human rights.

HUMANCORE.INTENT — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: User / Semantic Role: Declares
what an Entity wants, its relative priorities, and the hard constraints it must not violate.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type IntentState = { entity: EntityRef,
active_goals: List[Goal], preferences: List[Preference], hard_constraints: List[Constraint], soft_constraints:
List[Constraint],
version: SemVer, last_update: Time, provenance: Provenance }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-598EEC22B9 — Goal..

Schema references: SCHEMA-28E8BF2A68 — Constraint.; SCHEMA-921DD3FC31 — Preference..
Functions: DeclareIntent(entity: Entity, goals: List[Goal], constraints:
List[Constraint]) → IntentState Precondition: Entity authenticated (HumanCore.Identity). Postcondition: IntentState
updated; previous version superseded. Contract condition: Intent ≠ Authority (declaring a goal does not grant
permission).

ResolveIntent(entity: Entity, action: Action) → Feasibility Precondition: Action must be expressible in GSL.
Postcondition: Returns FEASIBLE | INFEASIBLE | CONDITIONAL. Logic: ALL hard constraints must pass. Soft constraints
reduce utility score.

ConstraintCheck(action: Action) → GenericResult<Bool> Precondition: Action must be
GSL-typed. Postcondition: If any FATAL constraint violated → INFEASIBLE.
Operational condition: Constraints never override K0P/Constitution (lexical priority).

Update Protocol: Intent: DRAFT → DECLARED → ACTIVE → SUPERSEDED. Goal:
ACTIVE → ACHIEVED (if metrics met) or ABANDONED (if deadline passes).

Invariant references: INT-001, K-INT-010, INT-002..004.
Dependencies & Math: Deps: Engine.Reason, Engine.Policy, Capability.Knowledge. Math: M3.Decision (Decision Theory),
M4.OPT (Optimization). Proofs: P2 (Non-Amp), P3 (Scope), P4 (Hard Safety).

HUMANCORE.DELEGATED-AGENT — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: User / Operational Role:
Manages delegated authority. Principal (Entity) → Delegate (Entity or System). Bounded by CapabilityToken.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type AgentState = { agency_graph: DAG,
active_sessions: Map[SessionID, AgentSession], capability_tokens:
Map[TokenID, CapabilityToken], swarm_policies: Map[SwarmID, SwarmPolicy], revocation_ledger: List[RevocationEvent],
max_depth: UInt
= 5, last_revocation_check: Time, pending_requests:
Queue[DelegationRequest], active_swarms: Map[SwarmID, Set[AgentRef]] }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-37902F6CA2 — Agency..

                   SwarmPolicy..
Functions: Delegate(principal: Entity, delegate: Entity, caps:
Set[Capability]) → Agency Precondition: principal authenticated,
delegate exists, depth(principal)+1 ≤ max_depth, ∀ cap ∈ caps: cap ⊆
Capability(principal). Postcondition: Agency created with status=ACTIVE.
CapabilityTokens generated with bounded resource limits. Contract condition:
Capability(delegate) ⊆ Capability(principal). No amplification.

CapabilityToken) → Outcome Precondition: Session ACTIVE, token.expiry >

Caller must be Principal OR Capability.Authority.Governance with valid proof.
Postcondition: agency.status = REVOKED. All child tokens invalidated

Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric.

SwarmCoord(swarm_id: SwarmID, action: Action) → Outcome Precondition:
Active swarm exists, ≥ quorum agents approve, principal-wide cumulative and rate budgets pass, Σ(resources) ≤
swarm.max_resource, and each action is authorized by the intersection of its sub-agent token, swarm token and principal
token. Horizontal splitting cannot expand capability or total resources.

Update Protocol: Agency: DRAFT → SUBMITTED → ACTIVE → SUSPENDED →
REVOKED. Session: REQUESTED → CHALLENGED → ACTIVE → EXPIRED | REVOKED.
Token: GRANTED → ACTIVE → REVOKED | EXPIRED. Swarm: FORMING → ACTIVE →
VOTING → EXECUTING → DISSOLVED.

GARDENCORE.SEMANTICS.GSL — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Foundation / Semantic Role:
The definitive semantic compiler. Translates Human intent → GSL AST and GSL AST → Human narrative. Enforces type/effect
soundness. Ambiguity is first-class.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type LanguageState = { ast_cache: Map[Hash,
GSL_AST], artifact_registry: Map[LanguageArtifactID, LanguageArtifact],
ambiguity_registry: Map[Hash, AmbiguityTrace], type_environment:
TypeEnv, effect_context: EffectContext, version: SemVer,
last_compilation: Time, translation_memory: Map[GSL_Expr, HumanExpr] }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-8788F27355 — LanguageArtifact..

Schema references: SCHEMA-603078AC96 — GSL_AST.; SCHEMA-64DE5C47CF — AmbiguityTrace..
GRAMMAR BINDING
GardenCore.Semantics.GSL uses the sole normative compiler-facing grammar in §12.4. Embedded/local grammar copies are not
authoritative and are omitted from this canonical master. Language-specific parser implementations must agree on the
canonical CIR for the declared consensus profile.

Functions: Parse(human_input: String, context: Context) → Set[GSL_AST] Precondition: Input must be UTF-8 encoded.
Postcondition: Returns one or more GSL_AST candidates. Contract condition: If multiple interpretations exist → AMBIGUOUS
(not silently resolved).

TypeCheck(ast: GSL_AST) → GenericResult<Bool> Precondition: AST must be syntactically valid. Postcondition: Verifies
  type consistency
against GSL type system (M0). Contract condition: WellTyped(e) ⇒ Meaning(e) defined (Type Soundness).

Render(ast: GSL_AST, locale: Locale, access: AccessProfile) → HumanText Precondition: AST must be semantically grounded.
Postcondition: Returns
human-readable narrative via Engine.Explanation + HumanCore.Accessibility. Contract condition:
Explanation does not amplify epistemic status (P2).

Disambiguate(candidates: Set[GSL_AST], context: Context) → GSL_AST Precondition: Candidates must be type-compatible.
Postcondition: If
context resolves → RESOLVED. Else → INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT. Contract condition:
Ambiguity is never collapsed without explicit evidence (K-INT-012).

If independent parser frontends produce disjoint AST sets, the deterministic result is NO_CONSENSUS. The input enters a
non-executing SemanticAmbiguitySandbox for evidence or competent human/domain clarification. Governance may choose a
lawful policy under explicit ambiguity; it cannot manufacture semantic truth or force one AST to become epistemically
correct.

Update Protocol: LanguageArtifact: DRAFT → REGISTERED → ACTIVE →
SUPERSEDED. AST: Parsed → TypeChecked → Grounded → Rendered. Ambiguity:
CANDIDATE → AMBIGUOUS → RESOLVED (with evidence) | INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT.

Invariant references: LANG-001..009.
Dependencies & Math: Read: Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Search, Engine.Reason, Context.LocaleCulture,
HumanCore.Accessibility. Write: Capability.Knowledge, Capability.State.EventService. Math: M0 (Type Theory, Logic),
M1.ALG (Category Theory), M1.RF (Refinement Algebra), M4.COMP (Compiler Theory). Proofs: P2, P3, P6, P8.

ENGINE.PROOF — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Foundation / Epistemic / Trust
Anchor Role: The formal proof engine. Discharges P1–P24. Proves structural soundness, NOT empirical truth. Part of the
Trust Anchor.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type ProofState = { proof_obligations:
Map[ObligationID, ProofObligation], discharged_certificates:
Map[ObligationID, ProofCertificate], counterexample_registry:
Map[ObligationID, CounterExample], validation_profiles: Map[Entity,
ValidationProfile], theorem_dependency_graph: DAG, prover_config:
ProverConfig, last_proof_attempt: Time, trust_root_measurement: Hash }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-37C4E41CD5 — ProofObligation..

Schema references: SCHEMA-6565D197D9 — ProofCertificate.; SCHEMA-9877FAD128 — CounterExample.; SCHEMA-BFC4A71850 —
                   ValidationProfile..
Functions:
Verify(obligation: ProofObligation) → ProofAttemptEnvelope
 Precondition: obligation is well-formed and its declared dependencies/assumptions are resolvable.
 Postcondition: returns exactly one ProofAttemptResult plus any ProofCertificate, CounterExample or checker diagnostic.
 Contract condition: Proof proves formal properties relative to declared assumptions; it does NOT manufacture empirical
 truth.

Discharge(obligation: ProofObligation) → ProofAttemptEnvelope
 Precondition: every required proof dependency has an accepted status compatible with this obligation.
 Postcondition: attempts proof through the selected qualified checker/prover profile.
 Contract condition: TIMEOUT, RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, UNDECIDABLE, UNSUPPORTED, MALFORMED, STALE and CHECKER_FAILURE remain
 explicit and can never be encoded as FALSE or success.

Falsify(obligation: ProofObligation, budget: ProofResourceBudget) → FalsificationResult
 Postcondition: FOUND_COUNTEREXAMPLE | NO_COUNTEREXAMPLE_WITHIN_BOUND | INCOMPLETE | TIMEOUT | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN |
 UNSUPPORTED | UNKNOWN.
 Contract condition: absence of a counterexample within a bound is never a proof.

ValidateFormalArtifact(artifact: Artifact, profile: ValidationProfile) → GenericResult<ValidationProfile>
 Contract condition: validates only the formal property/profile actually checked; empirical truth remains outside Proof.

Trust-root rule:
 The minimal checker is outside ordinary EvolutionWritableSet. It is not replaceable by ordinary Evolution, but a
 defective frozen checker may be replaced only through the externally authorized trust-root replacement ceremony,
 independent verification and a successor DesignEpoch.

Update Protocol:
 Obligation: PENDING → ATTEMPTED → DISCHARGED | COUNTEREXAMPLE | NOT_PROVED | TIMEOUT | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN |
 UNDECIDABLE | UNSUPPORTED | MALFORMED | STALE | CHECKER_FAILURE.
 ProofCertificate is immutable once verified. New evidence may invalidate an AssumptionBinding or
 TheoryApplicabilityResult; it does not retroactively falsify a valid derivation under its original assumptions.

Invariant references: VER-001..005.

verification), M4.COMP (Complexity bounds). Proofs: P2, P6, P8.

Classification: Foundation / Epistemic / Epistemic Trust Substrate Role:

                   AuditEvidence.; SCHEMA-7F9007D63D — UncertaintyPropagationProfile.; SCHEMA-64E54B04A4 —
                   EpistemicDebt..
EpistemicDebt Semantics: total_debt_score is a bounded aggregate of stale evidence, unresolved conflicts, correlated
evidence, expired assumptions, and unverified transformations. The metric is an optimization-pressure signal, not an
epistemic promotion signal. If total_debt_score > configured threshold, Knowledge MUST expose the debt to Evolution and
a mandatory ImprovementCycle MUST be admitted through Engine.Research and/or Engine.Reason, subject to the normal
authority, safety, proof, budget, and rollback gates. Low debt does NOT by itself justify a change.

Functions:
Ingest(artifact: StoredArtifact, authority: Authorization) → ArtifactID
 Precondition: authority is valid and provenance is attached.
 Postcondition: an immutable lineage/commitment record is appended.
 Contract condition: append-only applies to canonical lineage, commitments and required accountability records;
   protected
 payloads remain subject to lawful/consented cryptographic erasure and DataLifecyclePolicy.

ValidateEvidence(artifact: ArtifactID, profile: EvidenceValidationProfile) → EvidenceValidationResult
 Precondition: artifact exists; source/method/provenance/applicability and required independence checks are available.
 Postcondition: records the evidence validation result without changing the artifact kind into a Claim.
 Contract condition: Engine.Proof may verify formal calculations or transformations used by the profile, but cannot by
 itself establish empirical truth.

ValidateFormalClaim(claim: ClaimRef, obligation: ProofObligation) → ProofAttemptEnvelope
 Postcondition: binds a formal proof result to the Claim's declared formal proposition and assumptions.
 Contract condition: formal PROVEN status is scoped to the proposition/assumptions; it does not create empirical
   Evidence.

UpdateClaimDisposition(claim: ClaimRef, evidence: Set<EvidenceRef>, reason: ReasoningTrace) → ClaimDispositionResult
 Contract condition: Claim, Evidence and Knowledge remain distinct artifact/stage kinds; promotion occurs only through
 the owner-qualified validation path required by claim type.

UpdateInterpretation(artifact: ArtifactID, new_status: Status, reason:
Reason) → Interpretation Precondition: Artifact must exist. Reason must be evidenced. Postcondition: Interpretation
layer updated with new status; history appended. Contract condition: Record monotonicity ≠ belief monotonicity. The
artifact remains; its status changes.

Snapshot(epoch: Time, event_boundary: EventID) → KnowledgeSnapshot Precondition: Epoch must be > last snapshot.
Postcondition: Frozen view of interpretation layer created. Contract condition: Snapshot is immutable and consistent.

ConsistencySweep() → List[Anomaly] Precondition: Called periodically (K-INT-003). Postcondition: Detects contradictions,
staleness, and unresolved conflicts. Contract condition: Anomalies escalate to Governance/Research (K-INT-003).

Update Protocol: ObservationRecord, Evidence, Claim and KnowledgeRecord remain distinct typed artifacts. Applicable
validation creates a new Evidence artifact supported by ObservationRecord refs; reasoning creates a new Claim supported
or defeated by Evidence refs; justified derivation creates a new KnowledgeRecord linked to Claim/Evidence/proof refs.
EpistemicDerivationReceipt records each relation; no artifact mutates its ontological kind in place.
Retraction: ACTIVE → RETRACTED (tombstone, key destruction).
Supersession: ACTIVE → SUPERSEDED (new version linked). Contradiction:
ACTIVE → CONFLICTED (resolution via Proof/Research). Unknown: UNKNOWN remains UNKNOWN (never collapses).

LATE EPISTEMIC MAINTENANCE BINDING
Capability.Knowledge is the primary state carrier for Continuous Epistemic Revalidation and the Epistemic
Sweep/Saturation profile defined in [T-KNOWLEDGE]. Its implementation therefore also maintains revalidation metadata,
invalidation/dependency triggers, SweepRecord history and SaturationProfile references. Acceptance once is not permanent
trust.

HUMANCORE.CONSTITUTION — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Universal Architecture Object Contract
Purpose : Highest-order human-rights and constitutional rule registry; hardware-rooted veto
boundary. Inputs : GSL-typed proposed actions/transitions, RightRecords, amendment proposals, proofs, valid governance
records. Outputs : PASS | VETO | INCONCLUSIVE, constitutional version, registry root, amendment history. Rules :
Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision; Emergency never overrides Constitution; only substantively ratified ACTIVE
rights participate in constitutional veto evaluation. Explicit RESERVED or ON_HOLD records may exist without substantive
right semantics. Failure : Any active-right violation → VETO; unresolved constitutional applicability → low-authority
safe state/escalation. Dependencies : Capability.Trust.Safety, Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware, Engine.Proof,
Capability.Authority.Governance, Capability.Authority.Law, Engine.Policy, Capability.Knowledge,
Capability.State.TimeService. Why : Optimization, voting, model capability or emergency must never silently become
authority over fundamental rights. Status : Defined substantive rights registry current; RIGHT-026 is RESERVED and
RIGHT-027 is ON_HOLD; all other undefined human-right identifiers remain absent.

RightStatus = ACTIVE | RESERVED | ON_HOLD | SUPERSEDED.
RightID = stable versioned identifier. The registry is extensible; identifiers need not be contiguous, and absent
identifiers carry no semantics.
RESERVED and ON_HOLD RightRecords are non-active placeholders only: predicate = NONE and enforcement = NONE. They
create no right, veto, authority, obligation or permission until separately ratified as ACTIVE.

Schema references: SCHEMA-0FD4EE24BF — RightRecord.; SCHEMA-6F24051659 — ConstitutionState.; SCHEMA-94105927A3 —
                   ConstitutionalGate.; SCHEMA-9B3E9B1A1A — AmendmentRecord..
Functions:
Check(action_or_transition) → PASS | VETO | INCONCLUSIVE
 Evaluates every applicable ACTIVE right. RESERVED and ON_HOLD records are ignored for substantive right evaluation; an
 absent/unratified identifier has no right semantics and cannot be guessed.
Veto(action_or_transition) → GenericResult<Bool>
 Any applicable hard-right violation blocks execution before ordinary optimization.
Amend(proposal, votes, proof) → AmendmentRecord
 Historical profile: ≥90% quorum and ≥6 months deliberation for constitutional amendment, subject to valid
 constitutional process and proof. Superseded versions remain immutable.
VerifyRegistry() → GenericResult<Bool>
 Hardware/Proof check of registry root, version lineage and active-right set.

Invariant references: CON-RT-001, RIGHT-017, CON-RT-002..007.
Math: M0 formal logic/Hoare contracts; M1.GR version/precedence DAG; M4.CRYPTO registry roots/attestation. Proofs: P4a,
P6, P14, P16, P19.

CAPABILITY.AUTHORITY.LAW — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Constitutional / Normative
Role: Evolvable normative rules. Jurisdiction resolution. Conflict-of-laws lexicographic precedence. Law ≤ Constitution.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type LawState = { active_rules: Map[RuleID,
LawRule], jurisdiction_map: Map[Jurisdiction, Set[RuleID]],
precedence_graph: DAG, conflict_resolution_log:
List[ConflictResolution], source_authenticity_profiles: Map[LegalSourceID, LegalSourceAuthenticityProfile],
comparative_rights_syntheses: List[ComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile], version: SemVer, last_amendment: Time }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-61AB069FB7 — LawRule..

Schema references: SCHEMA-86B695DFF8 — JurisdictionMap.; SCHEMA-715C1D1A5F — ComplianceMapping.;
                   SCHEMA-446F627416 — LegalSourceAuthenticityProfile.;
                   SCHEMA-B5EFE9F7A5 — ComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile..
Functions: Resolve(context: Context, action: Action) → RuleSet Precondition: Context must include jurisdiction.
Postcondition: Returns
applicable set of laws (lexicographic precedence). Contract condition:
Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision.

ConflictResolution(rule1: LawRule, rule2: LawRule, context: Context) → LegalConflictResolutionResult
 Precondition: both candidate rules and the jurisdiction/context bindings are available.
 Postcondition: RESOLVED(rule) | LEGAL_CONFLICT(conflict_set) | UNRESOLVED | INCONCLUSIVE | BLOCKED.
 Higher-precedence resolution is returned only when precedence is actually determined. Equal-rank contradictory
 MANDATORY/PROHIBITED rules remain explicit and follow the competent legal escalation path. Governance may choose a
 lawful operational policy within authority; it cannot declare one rule epistemically true.

VerifyLegalSource(source: LegalSource) → LegalSourceAuthenticityProfile
Checks official publication/issuer, jurisdiction, authority hierarchy, instrument type, version/effective period,
amendment/supersession history, authenticity/signature/hash, translation status, retrieval provenance and known
completeness limits. Authenticity establishes what the source is; it does not make that source applicable outside its
jurisdiction or superior to another legal order.

SynthesizeComparativeRights(scope: ComparativeLegalScope) → ComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile
Produces a research/governance candidate that preserves source-specific rules, contradictions, exceptions, minority
protections, conflicts among rights and unresolved interpretations. Garden's active constitutional rights are
non-regression floors for Garden action. The method may identify common, stronger, incomparable or context-specific
protections, but it cannot manufacture world law or legal authority.

Jurisdiction population, citation-network influence and a scalar external human-rights score are not legal-precedence
weights. Such data may appear as contextual evidence with declared source/method/uncertainty, never as automatic
authority or a reason to erase minority protections. "Intersection of protections" is not treated as "maximum rights";
rights may conflict and require explicit contextual/legal/human resolution.

Amend(proposal: LawAmendment) → LawRule Precondition: Must not violate Constitution/K0P (K-INT-011). Postcondition: New
law added or existing law updated only after the applicable LawAmendmentProfile quorum/authority/deliberation
requirements are satisfied. No universal 67% threshold is implied. Contract condition: Law ≤ Constitution and cannot
degrade Reason/Proof (K-INT-008).

Update Protocol: LawRule: DRAFT → PROPOSED → VOTED → ACTIVE → REPEALED |
SUPERSEDED.

Invariant references: LAW-001..002, K-INT-008, LAW-003..004, LEG-SRC-001..007, RIGHTS-SYN-001..007.
Dependencies & Math: Read: HumanCore.Constitution, Capability.Authority.Governance, Engine.Policy,
Context.LocaleCulture. Write: Capability.Knowledge, Capability.State.EventService. Math: M1.GR (Graph Theory), M0 (Rule
Algebra, Deontic Logic), M3.LE (Learning). Proofs: P4,
P6, P16.

CAPABILITY.COMPUTE.EXECUTION — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Operational / Infrastructure
Role: Dispatches authorized WorkPackages. Enforces idempotency (AT_LEAST_ONCE), atomic transactions (ALL or NOTHING),
and outcome tracking. Final gate before K0P.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type ExecuteState = { active_executions:
Map[ExecutionID, ExecutionContext], transaction_log:
List[ExecutionReceipt], pending_commits: Map[TransactionID, TxState], idempotency_cache: Map[ActionHash,
ExecutionReceipt],
compensation_stack: Map[TransactionID, CompensationPlan], last_commit:
Time, active_rollbacks: Set[TransactionID] }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-DC2673F2B8 — ExecutionContext..

Schema references: SCHEMA-27491A2F8A — CP1_Certificate..
invalid → escalate to CP2.

Schema references: SCHEMA-9E88E41AA4 — ExecutionReceipt.; SCHEMA-B53C610C82 — CompensationPlan..
Functions: Prepare(workpackage: WorkPackage) → ExecutionContext Precondition: WorkPackage must be authorized, carry a
locally verifiable ExecutionAdmissionToken, pass the applicable proof/evidence gate and pass K0P. Postcondition:
ExecutionContext created with status=PREPARED. Emergency uses only pre-authorized emergency contracts and local K0P; it
is not a proof-free bypass. Irreversible actions require an irreversibility and recovery plan.

Execute(execution: ExecutionContext) → Outcome Precondition: Execution must have status=PREPARED or STARTED.
Postcondition: Performs actions atomically only while effects remain logically reversible. External and irreversible
effects use prepare/commit barriers, idempotency, bounded partial-failure states and compensation. Contract condition:
K0P choke point mandatory for all ControlledActuation (SAF-002).

Commit(execution: ExecutionContext) → ExecutionReceipt Precondition: All actions must be COMPLETED. Postcondition: State
changes finalized; receipt appended to Capability.Knowledge. Contract condition: Irreversible actions require explicit
audit trail.

Rollback(execution: ExecutionContext) → GenericResult<Bool> Precondition: Execution must be FAILED or ABORTED.
  Postcondition:
CompensationPlan executed; reversible software state is restored where possible and committed external effects are
addressed. The receipt states RESTORED, COMPENSATED, REMEDIATION_REQUIRED or IRREVERSIBLE_RESIDUAL. Compensation is
never mislabeled as restoration of the physical past.

Audit(receipt: ExecutionReceipt) → GenericResult<Bool> Precondition: Receipt must exist. Postcondition: Verifies proof
  against
Engine.Proof and logs to Capability.State.EventService. Contract condition: Audit logs are immutable and tamper-evident.

Update Protocol: Execution: PREPARED → STARTED → PARTIAL → COMPLETED | FAILED → COMPENSATED (rollback) | ABORTED.
Transaction: PENDING → COMMITTED (irreversible) | ROLLED_BACK.

Invariant references: EXE-001..006.

M4.CRYPTO (Signatures), M3.PR (Probabilistic outcome tracking). Proofs: P1, P4, P9.

declared stable thermal envelope. Active thermal excursions defer baseline replacement and may update only an incident
profile. Postcondition: Runs diagnostics; updates thermal_profile. Contract condition: If failure detected → trigger
Capability.Resilience.Emergency.

HARDWARE GENESIS / BOOT BINDING
HardwareGenesisReceipt specializes the system-level TrustRootCeremonyReceipt for a declared device. It binds device
identity/public key or certificate chain, immutable-root commitment, initial platform measurements, manufacturer and
supply-chain evidence where available, anti-rollback state, accepted bootstrap manifest, applicable external witnesses,
and the authorization that admits the device into an initial DesignEpoch. The device genesis may predate a Garden
DesignEpoch; an explicit acceptance binding connects them without rewriting either history.

BootAttestationReceipt is run-specific. It binds a verifier challenge/nonce or other valid freshness evidence, measured
boot/PCR state, firmware and configuration roots, monotonic/anti-rollback state, device identity, time evidence when
available, bootstrap IntegrityHealth status and provenance. External time or multi-party witnesses are assurance-profile
requirements rather than universal assumptions, so qualified offline boot remains possible.
Engine.Proof/Safety verification of a K0P-controlled physical path requires a fresh, scope-compatible verified boot
receipt and its accepted genesis/ceremony lineage; software self-report alone is insufficient.
Invariant references: HW-GEN-001..007.

Update Protocol: Hardware: BOOTING → READY → RUNNING → FAULTED (escalate). DigitalTwin: SYNC → UPDATED → CALIBRATED.
Firmware: LOADED → VERIFIED → EXECUTING → ROLLBACK (if corrupt).

Invariant references: HWR-001..006.
Dependencies & Math: Read: Domain.Mathematics, Capability.Trust.Safety, Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric,
Capability.Trust.Security, Capability.State.TimeService, Capability.Compute.NativeInterface. Write:
Capability.State.EventService, Capability.Knowledge. Math: M2 (Thermal Dynamics), M3.PR (Measurement uncertainty),
M4.CRYPTO (OTP, TPM, PQC). Proofs: P1, P4, P19.

CAPABILITY.INFRASTRUCTURE.NETWORK — DETAILED SPECIFICATION

Classification: Infrastructure / Operational
Role: Provides secure, authenticated, and timely communication between Garden nodes and external systems. Enforces PQC,
QoS, and revocation propagation.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type NetworkState = { active_sessions:
Map[SessionID, NetworkSession], route_table: RouteTable,
pending_packets: Queue[NetworkPacket], qkd_session_pool:
Map[QKDSessionID, QKDSession], revocation_cache: CuckooFilter, connection_health: Map[PeerID, HealthStatus], last_sync:
Time,
partition_status: CONNECTED | PARTITIONED | OFFLINE }

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-61741FAB1F — NetworkPacket..

Schema references: SCHEMA-C9CCE1AFE2 — QKDSession.; SCHEMA-945D59620F — RouteTable..
Functions:
Send(packet: NetworkPacket, destination: EntityRef) → GenericResult<NetworkDeliveryReceipt>
 Precondition: packet is signed, typed, authorized and destination resolution is valid.
 Postcondition: packet is routed under the active CryptographicSuite and QoS/priority profile.
 Operational condition: protected communication is authenticated and encrypted under the selected suite; plaintext PII
 is prohibited outside an explicitly authorized protected boundary.

Receive(packet: NetworkPacket) → Result<DecodedEnvelope<T>, DecodeFailure>
 Precondition: packet passes integrity, authentication, schema/version, taint and authorization checks.
 Postcondition: a typed decoded envelope is delivered to the architecture boundary; raw Bytes remain transport
 serialization only. Contract condition: A Cuckoo filter is only a fast hint.
Saturation or a positive result invokes a signed exact revocation structure before consequential rejection; revoked
senders remain blocked.

SyncTime(peer: EntityRef) → Time Precondition: Peer must be trusted (Capability.Trust.Security). Postcondition:
Synchronizes HLC (Hybrid Logical Clock) via PTP/GNSS. Contract condition: Drift <1ms for R4/AUTO domains.

Revoke(entity: EntityRef) → GenericResult<Bool> Precondition: Caller must be Capability.Trust.Security or
Capability.Authority.Governance. Postcondition: Revocation propagated to all nodes through signed epoch roots plus
dynamically resized/rotated filters. Load monitoring, exact fallback and re-indexing prevent saturation from becoming
permanent node isolation.

Update Protocol: Session: HANDSHAKE → ESTABLISHED → EXPIRED | REVOKED. Packet: QUEUED → ROUTED → DELIVERED →
ACKNOWLEDGED. Partition: CONNECTED
→ PARTITIONED → RECONNECTING → RECONCILED.

Invariant references: NET-001..006.
Dependencies & Math: Read: Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware, Capability.Trust.Security, Capability.State.TimeService,
Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric, Capability.Resilience.Offline. Write: Capability.State.EventService,
Capability.Knowledge. Math: M1.GR (Graph Theory), M3.PR (Probability), M4.CRYPTO (PQC, QKD, Signatures). Proofs: P1, P6,
P7, P11.

  Postcondition:
Deltas propagated to peers. Contract condition: CRDT convergent.

Merge(state1: CRDT_State, state2: CRDT_State) → CRDT_State Precondition:
States must be compatible. Postcondition: Returns merged state via CRDT merge function. Contract condition: Merge is
associative, commutative, idempotent.

Commit(state: CRDT_State, evidence: ConsistencyFinalityResult) → GenericResult<HEMT_Root>
 Precondition: the active ConsistencyProfile's required finality/visibility condition is established by typed evidence.
 `UNKNOWN_PARTITION` or insufficient finality cannot be relabeled as converged.
 Postcondition: state is committed only at the visibility/finality level authorized by the profile; HEMT asynchronous
 merging remains available without claiming unknowable global convergence.

CommitProofCarryingState(manifest, state_chunks, obligation_chunks) → HEMT_Root
 No state becomes visible until every content-addressed state/proof chunk, environment commitment and
 ObligationGraph root is present and verified. Commit is an atomic manifest-pointer publication;
 incomplete serialization remains PENDING and cannot race ahead of its proof payload.

ResolveConflict(state1: CRDT_State, state2: CRDT_State) → CRDT_State Precondition: Conflict detected. Postcondition:
Conflict resolved via DRC conflict_policy. Contract condition: Conflict resolution deterministic.

Update Protocol: CRDT: LOCAL → PENDING → MERGED → COMMITTED. HEMT:
APPEND → REHASH → ROOT_UPDATE.

 Precondition: time source/binding is available.

Functions: Monitor() → GridState Precondition: Sensors active. Postcondition: Returns current grid state. Contract
