CP1 gains: environment_commitment: Hash

Full binding: code_hash, param_schema, param_range, effect_set, resource_bound, safety_env, deadline, env_assumptions,
environment_commitment.

If environment_commitment changes: CP1 INVALID ->
re-verification/escalation required.

Incident-derived deployment: Sandbox -> Simulation -> Historical Replay
-> Verification -> Adversarial Test -> Regression -> Canary ->
Controlled Deployment -> Monitoring -> Validation -> Commit.

No AI proposal -> direct production path exists.

G.13 Capability.Resilience.Offline ESCROW

Schema references: SCHEMA-8965175621 — OfflineAuditEscrow..
On reconnection: 1. Verify escrow integrity. 2. Verify hardware root-of-trust signature. 3. Verify reconnection proof.

4. Reconcile events. 5. Resolve applicable conflicts. 6. Convert verified escrow into full quorum multi-signature seal.

7. Bind seal to Emergency audit record.

Offline escrow is not equivalent to online quorum seal until step 6 succeeds.

G.14 GardenCore.Semantics.GSL PATCH GRAMMAR

PatchDecl ::= “patch” ID “{” PatchItem* “}”

PatchItem ::= “applies_to” ID “;” | “before” Expr “;” | “after” Expr “;” |
              “proof” ProofBody “;” | “rollback” Expr “;”

First-class GSL types: IncidentRecord, CausalGraph, FailurePattern, CorrectiveChange, RegressionArtifact, AttackReplay,
UncertaintyPropagationProfile, IncidentClassification, RootCauseHypothesis, MitigationCandidate, VerificationRecord,
ContainmentAction, CausalReconstructionTrace.

G.15 META-EVOLUTION

Invariant reference: EVO-009.

The evolving component cannot be its own sole judge. Evolution may improve its algorithms, but the modified mechanism
cannot certify its own modification. TerminationContract bounds recursive self-improvement. OscillationDetection detects
repeated improvement/reversal cycles. Constitutional, hardware-trust, and safety roots remain outside the Evolution
writable set.

G.16 PROACTIVE DEFENSIVE SCANNING

Verified FailurePattern -> LearnedIndex -> Existing WorkPackages ->
Pending WorkPackages -> Runtime-relevant configurations -> Future
patches.

ProactiveScan identifies: exact recurrence; structural variants; causal variants; equivalent state transitions;
weakened/precondition-shifted versions; composition attacks.

Positive matches trigger the applicable Security/Reason containment or verification pathway.

G.17 REGRESSION PERMANENCE

Every verified significant incident generates: original replay + variant replays + legitimate counterexamples +
regression tests + expected safety outcome.

Future versions are tested against the preserved regression set. Retirement requires governed review, explicit
rationale, evidence the failure condition is no longer applicable, preserved provenance and a retirement audit.

G.18 HUMAN/GOVERNANCE BOUNDARY

Automatic incident learning may detect, contain, reconstruct, classify, hypothesize, generate technical fixes, formally
verify, replay, test, deploy within authorized technical boundaries, and learn generalized defensive patterns.

It SHALL NOT autonomously weaken constitutional rights, modify immutable constitutional roots, redefine human rights,
alter fundamental governance authority, remove mandatory human review, or modify hardware trust roots outside authorized
mechanisms.

Such changes escalate to Governance/Constitution.

G.19 EMERGENCY OVERRIDE

Active Threat -> Immediate Containment -> Evidence Preservation ->
Safe-State / Emergency Mechanism -> Incident Reconstruction -> Root
Cause -> Corrective Research -> Verification -> Replay -> Deployment.

Emergency containment may precede full verification only where an already- authorized emergency mechanism exists. The
mechanism remains auditable and immutable within its authority boundary.

G.20 FINAL LEARNING PROPERTY

FAILURE -> DETECTION -> CONTAINMENT -> FORENSIC RECONSTRUCTION -> CAUSAL
ANALYSIS -> ROOT-CAUSE HYPOTHESIS -> GENERALIZED FAILURE PATTERN ->
CANDIDATE REMEDIATION -> DO_NOTHING COMPARISON -> INDEPENDENT
VERIFICATION -> HISTORICAL REPLAY -> ADVERSARIAL VARIANT TESTING ->
PERMANENT REGRESSION -> CONTROLLED DEPLOYMENT -> POST-DEPLOYMENT
VALIDATION -> DURABLE KNOWLEDGE -> PROACTIVE FUTURE DETECTION ->
RECURSIVE AUDIT

Core invariant: Garden SHALL learn from verified technical failures without allowing the failure, the diagnosis, the
learning mechanism, or the remediation mechanism to become self-authenticating authority.

8.11 DESIGN EPOCH / BINDING / DRIFT / CONFORMANCE
VII-H — DESIGN EPOCH, BINDING, DRIFT AND CONFORMANCE

H.1 DESIGN EPOCH

Schema references: SCHEMA-C4CCD0ED87 — DesignEpoch.; SCHEMA-32C6E214AB — DesignBinding.;
                   SCHEMA-146B6B8992 — EnvironmentCommitment.; SCHEMA-F3D094725B — KnowledgeSnapshot.;
                   SCHEMA-1A2A6D825F — DesignEpochReadinessReport.

DesignEpoch is Garden’s cryptographic constitutional time capsule: an immutable versioned snapshot of the complete
canonical design for a declared scope. It binds at least:
  - every schema definition and schema-alias/migration record;
  - every invariant and proof obligation;
  - every registry entry and architecture-object identity/binding;
  - the ten Core Objects and 24 Core Relations;
  - Constitution and active Rights registry;
  - compiler/CIR contracts and the normative GSL grammar/semantic version;
  - capability/domain/profile definitions and their dependencies;
  - acceptance evidence, verification receipts, authority and provenance roots.

An epoch becomes ACCEPTED only after all applicable gates succeed:
  1. Integrity proof / registry-root consistency.
  2. Constitutional and active-rights check.
  3. Global invariant/proof-obligation analysis for the declared scope.
  4. Interface/schema compatibility and required migration evidence.
  5. Dependency closure/cycle/SCC analysis.
  6. Independent frozen-verifier or equivalent independent verification.
  7. Competent human/governance approval.

Describing these requirements does not self-certify the document. A human-readable master is only a DesignEpoch
candidate until machine receipts and independent acceptance exist.

H.2 DESIGN BINDING

Every production artifact is permanently bound to the specific DesignEpoch it implements.

DesignBinding contains: code hash, epoch reference, architecture_object_id, requirement/invariant/proof references,
safety/security constraints, environment commitment and verification status.

CodeStatus lattice: COMPLIANT | STALE | INCONSISTENT | UNKNOWN |
VERIFICATION_REQUIRED | BLOCKED

H.3 DRIFT DETECTION — Engine.Search + Capability.Compute.Runtime

Runtime periodically invokes Search to compare live code bindings with the latest accepted DesignEpoch.

Drift Index: - inverted map: Epoch -> affected artifacts - delta index:
Blueprint Hash <-> Live Binding Hash - priority queue ranked by blast
radius, epistemic debt, safety/security criticality

Detected STALE, INCONSISTENT or VERIFICATION_REQUIRED states emit auditable Events. Safety-critical drift escalates
immediately.

H.4 IMPACT ANALYSIS / PROOF INVALIDATION — Engine.Proof

When a new epoch is accepted, compute a Design Diff against the previous epoch. Impact Closure determines affected
requirements, invariants, architecture objects, interfaces, schemas, state machines, dependencies, assumptions, proof
obligations,
safety/security constraints and deployed bindings.

Proofs whose assumptions, dependencies or interfaces changed become STALE and cannot be used until re-discharged against
the new epoch.

H.5 RECURSIVE CONFORMANCE ENGINE — Engine.Evolve

Mandatory enforce_conformance loop: 1. Load latest accepted Epoch. 2. Scan live system for drift. 3. If drift empty,
emit ConformanceCertificate and exit. 4. Run impact analysis. 5. Generate candidate patches, explicitly including
DO_NOTHING. 6. For each candidate ranked by risk/reversibility: - adversarial falsification; - independent verification;
- projected-state simulation; - Sandbox execution and global-conformance verification; - Canary execution and
global-conformance verification; - atomic commit and post-commit verification. 7. On success, emit patch certificate and
re-scan. 8. If all candidates fail or DO_NOTHING is justified, stop. 9. Oscillation/depth/resource-limit exceedance
escalates to governance.

Recursion is bounded by TerminationContract: depth, attempts and oscillation. Non-convergence blocks deployment and
requires human review.

H.6 PATCH DEPENDENCY ORDER

Strict order: 1. Trust roots & Constitution. 2. Schemas & types. 3. Interfaces & core dependencies. 4. Safety/security
kernels. 5. Dependent implementations. 6. Integration & deployment artifacts.

A dependent patch cannot be certified until prerequisite patch verification and commit are complete.

H.7 EIGHT-LEVEL CONFORMANCE VERIFICATION

All levels must pass for global conformance: 1. Structural — architecture objects, interfaces, dependencies, schemas. 2.
Semantic
— state transitions, invariants, scope non-widening. 3. Safety — K0P barriers, fail-safe reachability. 4. Security —
authorization, trust roots, PQC, privilege boundaries. 5. Proof — discharged obligations, assumption freshness. 6.
Operational — resources, latency, determinism, auditability. 7. Provenance — valid DesignBinding, full lineage trace. 8.
Global Composition — cross-component blast radius, shared invariants.

check_conformance(epoch, snapshot) -> PASS/FAIL + detailed violations.

H.8 DEPLOYMENT GATE / EMERGENCY OVERRIDE — Capability.Compute.Execution

Unrestricted deployment is rejected if status is BLOCKED, INCONSISTENT,
UNKNOWN or VERIFICATION_REQUIRED.

Emergency path is allowed only through Capability.Resilience.Emergency with explicit authorization, bounded
scope/duration, immutable audit trail, Constitution/K0P preservation, mandatory post-emergency reconciliation and
re-verification.

An override cannot permanently mark an artifact COMPLIANT.

H.9 CONFORMANCE CERTIFICATE — Capability.Knowledge

Certificate captures epoch, system snapshot, artifact-set hash and PASS/FAIL for all eight verification levels. It
references adversarial review, independent verification, invalidated proofs and active exceptions.

It becomes invalid if: - DesignEpoch is superseded; - bound assumption/dependency/environment/source/compiler input
changes; - post-deployment verification fails.

Statuses: FULL | CONDITIONAL | EXCEPTION | FAILED | PENDING

H.10 CONFORMANCE EXCEPTION

ConformanceException is an explicit bounded waiver for specific artifacts or requirements. It requires justification,
risk assessment, compensating controls, expiry, independent review and explicit authority.

It cannot widen design scope, weaken constitutional roots, erase provenance or bypass independent verification
permanently.

H.11 PROVENANCE CHAIN

Every production artifact has an immutable trace: Requirement -> Design
-> Epoch -> ArchitectureObject -> Implementation -> Dependency Set -> Proofs ->
Adversarial Review -> Independent Verification -> Deployment -> Runtime
State -> Conformance Certificate.

Broken link -> UNKNOWN or VERIFICATION_REQUIRED -> unrestricted
deployment blocked until restored.

H.12 SELF-CONFORMANCE OF CONFORMANCE MACHINERY

The following mechanisms themselves require valid DesignBindings and ConformanceCertificates: DesignEpoch registry;
Binding registry; Drift detector; Impact analyzer; Patch generator; Adversarial verifier; Independent verifier;
Conformance checker; Deployment gate; Rollback mechanism; Certificate issuer.

Meta-Evolution requires independent verification using a frozen/trusted baseline. The evolving mechanism cannot be its
own sole judge.

H.13 DCC FAMILY — GLOBAL CONFORMANCE INVARIANT REFERENCES

Canonical definitions: Annexure A.
Invariant references: DCC-001..036.
H.14 TEN SEALING PRINCIPLES

1. The latest accepted DesignEpoch is authoritative.

2. No code is trusted merely because it was generated by Garden.

3. The conformance machinery is itself subject to conformance.

4. A design change propagates through impact analysis and proof
 invalidation.

5. Local correctness never substitutes for global correctness.

6. Every repair is adversarially reviewed and independently verified.

7. Recursive repair is bounded and cannot recursively modify its own
 trust roots.

8. DO_NOTHING remains a legitimate outcome.

9. Exceptions are temporary, explicit, auditable and cannot silently
 weaken constitutional roots.

10. Deployment is continuously rechecked after commitment.

H.15 DESIGNEPOCH PROTECTION — “CONSTITUTIONAL TIME CAPSULE” VIEW

No silent drift
 Every production artifact binds to a specific accepted DesignEpoch. A relevant design/environment/knowledge change
 invalidates or re-evaluates the affected binding rather than silently changing meaning.

No self-certification
 The system/ mechanism proposing an epoch cannot be its sole independent verifier or certifier.

No retroactive rewriting
 Accepted DesignEpochs are immutable historical states. A change creates a successor epoch and explicit migration.

Governed evolution
 Epoch transition uses impact analysis, proof/evidence, migration, rollback/compensation and legitimate authority.

Trust-root preservation
 Physical trust roots, K0P and constitutional roots remain outside ordinary Evolution’s writable authority.

H.16 CURRENT DESIGNEPOCH VALIDATION CHECKLIST — HUMAN-SOURCE STATUS

CURRENT MACHINE-CLOSURE GATES
The following are no longer hand-authored document tasks. They are mandatory generated/verification stages:

  1. Generate the complete schema/type package from the accepted current H/S/T/THA/A semantic-source roots and the
     current Artifact Catalogue.
  2. Resolve every name through GSL primitives, Core Objects, generic constructors, generated artifact types or explicit
     owner-qualified imports.
  3. Parse/type/effect-check the generated package and produce the canonical CIR.
  4. Compute dependency closure and permitted SCC/fixed-point contracts; unresolved/illegal cycles block acceptance.
  5. Generate and discharge applicable proof obligations, including P1..P24, PROC-001..021 and VLP obligations.
  6. Generate executable TEST-VLP-001..012 and other conformance tests from their canonical test contracts.
  7. Run independent/frozen-verifier review of generator, compiler outputs, registry roots and self-audit machinery.
  8. Issue SelfAuditCertificate only after all required document→machine projection and assurance checks pass.
  9. Accept a DesignEpoch only through the defined constitutional/governance process.

The human document MUST NOT fabricate these receipts. Until implementations exist, the applicable status is PENDING.

Current document-level status is intentionally separated from formal certification:

  Registry consistency       : TEXTUAL PASS — canonical REG identifiers resolve; machine registry-root receipt PENDING.
  Schema-ID completeness     : TEXTUAL PASS — every referenced SchemaID resolves in ANNEXURES; parser receipt PENDING.
  Named-type closure         : TEXTUAL PASS for the ratified defect-report set; full compiler type/effect receipt
  PENDING.
  VLP schema/test closure    : TEXTUAL PASS — four VLP schemas + TEST-VLP-001..012 are present; execution PENDING.
  Invariant registry         : TEXTUAL PASS/retained; formal discharge remains PENDING unless separately evidenced.
  Constitutional consistency : NO KNOWN DOCUMENT-LEVEL CONTRADICTION from this audit; formal/independent check PENDING.
  Dependency closure         : schema/reference closure PASS; implemented graph/SCC dependency verification PENDING.
  Compiler compatibility     : PENDING — §12.4 grammar exists, but no accepted parser/type/effect receipt is claimed.
  Independent verification  : PENDING.
  SelfAuditCertificate       : PENDING.
  DesignEpoch state          : SPECIFIED_DESIGNEPOCH_CANDIDATE, not ACCEPTED.

H.17 GSL-COMPARE — CURRENT CANDIDATE VS ACCEPTED DESIGNEPOCH TARGET

  Dimension                  current human-source candidate          Accepted DesignEpoch target
  Registry/schema identity   Closed at document level                       Cryptographically rooted + machine verified
  Type resolution            Reported gaps resolved                         Zero compiler/type/effect unresolved
  identifiers
  Invariant coverage         Registry retained                              Applicable obligations discharged/receipted
  Dependency closure         Static references resolve                      Engine.Dependency closure/SCC receipt
  accepted
  Constitutional status      Design constraints present                     Independent constitutional/rights gate
  accepted
  Compiler status            PENDING                                        Parser/CIR/type/effect consensus PASS
  Independent verifier       PENDING                                        Accepted independent/frozen-verifier receipt
  Self-audit certificate     PENDING                                        Accepted, scope-bound certificate

The remaining gap is machine/formal/independent certification. Document-level consistency does not substitute for
generated-package compilation, proof discharge or independent DesignEpoch acceptance.

8.13 USER / EXTERNAL SHELL

B.1 — EXTERNAL UI SHELL / HUMAN INTERFACE — COMPACT FULL CONTRACT

Status: external shell; NOT new fixed top-level object. Gateway=GardenCore.Semantics.GSL;
adaptation=HumanCore.Accessibility+Context.LocaleCulture. Philosophy: one persistent input; minimal navigation;
progressive disclosure; every important answer inspectable; user remains principal within constitutional bounds.
Navigation: Home(summary/actions); Explore(search/compare/learn/plan/capabilities);
Activity=Capability.State.EventService+Capability.Knowledge timeline;
Me=identity/preferences/permissions/memory/projects. Epistemic badges: VERIFIED(formal proof discharged),
SUPPORTED(strong evidence), UNCERTAIN(insufficient evidence), CONFLICTED(credible disagreement),
HYPOTHETICAL(prediction/simulation), UNKNOWN(not known), REJECTED(blocked by Safety/Constitution/Policy), NEEDS_YOU(user
decision unless authorized Emergency). UI badge never promotes underlying epistemic status. Interactions: ASK:
User→UI→GardenCore.Semantics.GSL→Core→Engine.Explanation→Language→UI. WHY opens Evidence
Inspector(reasoning,evidence,assumptions,uncertainty). Recommendations expose Approve/Change/Reject. Corrections enter
epistemic processing; they are not automatically truth. Autonomy modes: Ask me first | Assist | Automatic. Critical
action needs user approval unless constitutionally authorized Capability.Resilience.Emergency. UI approval cannot
override Constitution, Trust Anchor, K0P, Engine.Policy, Security, proof/applicability gates or hardware vetoes. Data
flow:
User→UI→GardenCore.Semantics.GSL→Engine.Reason/Engine.Search/Capability.Knowledge→Engine.Explanation→
Language+Access+Locale→UI.
Boundary: UI may express intent, expose evidence/status/uncertainty, request decisions and render authorized results; it
cannot create authority, weaken safety, promote UNKNOWN/prediction to fact/knowledge, bypass governance/proofs/K0P, or
authorize Policy-rejected action.

8.15 SECURITY AND PRIVACY-PRESERVING EXECUTION PROFILE

Purpose: protect agentic, distributed and privacy-sensitive execution without binding Garden to one model family,
cryptographic primitive or vendor. Primary architecture objects: HumanCore.Privacy, HumanCore.DelegatedAgent,
GardenCore.Semantics.GSL, Capability.Compute.Runtime, Capability.Compute.Execution, Capability.Compute.NativeInterface,
Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware, Capability.Infrastructure.Network, Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric,
Capability.Trust.Security, Engine.Policy, Engine.Reason, with Engine.Proof and Engine.Compliance as independent
checkers.

Trust-channel separation

InstructionChannel = SYSTEM_CONSTITUTION | AUTHORIZED_POLICY | USER_INTENT | EXTERNAL_CONTENT |
 TOOL_OUTPUT | RETRIEVED_MEMORY | MODEL_PLAN

Schema references: SCHEMA-FD69F25CE7 — UntrustedContentEnvelope..
External content, tool output, retrieved documents and model-generated plans are data. They cannot become executable
instructions merely because natural language inside them asks Garden to act. GardenCore.Semantics.GSL preserves channel
labels; HumanCore.DelegatedAgent and Capability.Compute.Execution reject channel confusion. This is the primary
prompt/instruction-injection boundary; adversarial content remains tainted through parsing, retrieval, memory, planning
and tool selection.

Plan/action separation

Schema references: SCHEMA-215DDB0F5D — PlanActionContract..
A planner proposes. A separately constrained action selector verifies the requested operation, tool, arguments, effects,
capability, policy, safety and current context. Using two models, code generation, map-reduce or other agent patterns is
optional defense-in-depth; separation of untrusted interpretation from authorization is mandatory.

Agent containment and credential isolation

Schema references: SCHEMA-E1504F99EE — AgentEnvelope.; SCHEMA-8E3F7995E7 — CapabilityFragment..
CapabilityCompositionProof demonstrates that composition, delegation, swarming and migration preserve the intersection
of principal authority, token scope, AgentEnvelope and current policy. An agent cannot enlarge its own envelope.

Schema references: SCHEMA-A85AF32DF1 — ScopedCredentialLease..
Broad reusable credentials are not given to an agent where a narrower expiring capability can perform the task.
Issuance, use, result, revocation and expiry remain linked in Capability.State.EventService.

Security contracts

Schema references: SCHEMA-1DFC3464A9 — SecurityContract..
GardenCore.Semantics.GSL types the contract; Engine.Compile and Engine.Proof check static obligations;
Capability.Compute.Runtime, Capability.Infrastructure.Network and Capability.Trust.Security enforce monitorable clauses;
Engine.Compliance consumes resulting evidence.

Privacy-preserving execution ladder

Schema references: SCHEMA-3C39E15FBB — PrivacyComputationPlan..
Cheapest sufficient ladder:
 data minimization
 → local/on-device execution
 → isolated process
 → attested confidential-computing environment
 → secure multi-party computation (SMPC)
 → homomorphic encryption
 → another independently justified method.

The ladder is not monotonic in universal superiority. Garden selects a Pareto-feasible method satisfying the privacy and
correctness contract. SMPC, FHE and trusted execution environments remain assumption-bound; their use does not by itself
prove privacy, correctness or freedom from side channels.

Network and implementation hardening

- Every workload has an authenticated workload identity; service communication is mutually authenticated,
 encrypted and authorized at the narrowest practical segment.
- Micro-segmentation limits lateral movement; Fabric consensus never assumes network location implies trust.
- Security-critical cryptographic and secret-dependent paths declare a constant-time/cache/power/fault
 profile. Compilation and Development scan for violations; Hardware adds shielding where required.
- Formal rate limits cover requests, transactions, allocations, SOS/event ingress, tool calls, storage,
 memory allocation and capability issuance. Priority traffic receives bounded admission and backpressure.
- Extension sandboxes have hard memory, allocation-rate, stack, CPU/gas, I/O and process limits.
- Hardware provenance uses signed component identities, content-addressed manifests and independently
 witnessed transparency logs. A blockchain is an optional anchoring mechanism, never a requirement or
 substitute for physical inspection and attestation.
- Imported models, weights, tools and datasets enter an isolated admission pipeline with provenance,
 poisoning/backdoor tests, capability limits and continuous monitoring.
- Representation/neuron/anomaly monitors are experimental signals only. A detector output routes evidence
 to Proof and may trigger containment; it is never proof of safety or sentience.

Invariant references: AGT-008..014, PRIV-007..012, SEC-006..014.
8.16 UNIVERSAL AUDIT, COMPLIANCE AND DATA-LIFECYCLE PROFILE

Principle: every material Garden state, decision, authority use, proof, transformation, deployment and physical effect
is auditable to the extent appropriate to consequence, irreversibility, novelty, uncertainty and legal/constitutional
need. Auditable does not mean retain every byte forever.

Schema references: SCHEMA-AD02E1E919 — AuditPolicy.; SCHEMA-FE6D8FD05B — AuditReceipt.; SCHEMA-B731EFD747 —
                   ComplianceManifest.; SCHEMA-50F3A06C26 — DataLifecyclePolicy.; SCHEMA-6A60A1B11F — PurgeReceipt..
Audit flow

DesignBinding
 → generated ComplianceManifest
 → build/static audit
 → proof and evidence audit
 → deployment safety case
 → runtime/behavior/effect audit
 → drift or violation detection
 → block, quarantine, bounded continuity or remediation
 → independent re-audit
 → certificate renewal, restriction or invalidation.

Risk-proportionate coverage

- Full audit is mandatory for constitutional, safety-critical, authority-changing, irreversible,
 high-privacy, high-value and self-modifying operations.
- Deterministic low-risk repetitive operations may use verified controls, sampling and aggregate evidence.
- UNKNOWN applicability, missing evidence or audit timeout cannot be reported as PASS.
- Audit machinery, auditors, retention rules and purge machinery are themselves audited.

Retention and purge

- Hot: operationally necessary and actively queried.
- Warm: recent accountability, debugging and revalidation evidence.
- Cold: rarely used but still required for rights, safety, law, proof or historical reconstruction.
- Archive: immutable or content-addressed evidence retained under an explicit continuing purpose.
- Purge: payload and recoverable keys removed after purpose expiry and hold checks; caches, replicas,
 backups and derivatives are included.

Canonical constitutions, accepted DesignEpochs, rights-affecting decisions, unresolved liabilities, active legal/safety
holds and the minimum evidence needed to prove accountability cannot be silently
purged. Conversely, privacy-sensitive raw data is not made permanently visible merely to simplify audit:
Garden may retain a minimal commitment, redacted receipt or independently verifiable aggregate.

Schema references: SCHEMA-8859E963A2 — DeploymentSafetyCase..
Post-deployment observations flow through Events → Knowledge → Proof → Reasoning → Evolution. Distribution, capability,
security, privacy, resource or environment drift invalidates affected certificates automatically and triggers the
applicable response.

Invariant references: AUD-001..015, DLC-001..012.

SemanticAnchor: [T-EVOLVE]

SECTION III-H — EVOLUTION, PREDICTION, EFFICIENCY, HISTORY AND MULTI-AGENT IMPROVEMENT

9.1 CURRENT INTEGRATED MECHANISMS
This Part contains the current Branch/Merge, prediction-safety, ownership, verification-efficiency, resource-efficiency,
construction-distrust, provenance and historical-optimization mechanisms. Prior release labels have no normative role in
this canonical specification.

9.2 BRANCH & MERGE — CURRENT FULL MODEL
Schema references: SCHEMA-3BC2005533 — EvolutionBranch..
Branch status: CREATED → ISOLATED → REPLAYING → VERIFIED | REJECTED | DEFERRED → MERGE_READY → MERGED | PRUNED |
ARCHIVED.

Schema references: SCHEMA-4D907A214A — DeterministicReplay.; SCHEMA-AC334D0277 — EvolutionTrace.; SCHEMA-1D846E3452 —
                   MergeCertificate..
Rules:
- Branches are isolated from canonical state until governed commit.
- Current_System and DO_NOTHING are mandatory candidates.
- Replay is deterministic relative to declared inputs/seed/environment; undeclared external nondeterminism is recorded.
- Pareto selection preserves incomparable candidates; one scalar score does not erase hard constraints.
- Recombination is allowed only after compatibility/assumption/dependency analysis and creates a new candidate, never
  history rewriting.
- Pruning removes active search cost but preserves sufficient provenance/rejection memory.
- Merge requires proof/conformance/authority/rollback and atomic/global consistency where required.
- Rollback creates a governed transition; original commits remain immutable.

9.3 PREDICTION SAFEGUARDS
PredictionTier = FAST | STANDARD | DEEP.
Each tier declares compute/time/model/ensemble/sample budget; tier choice is controlled by risk, information value,
uncertainty and consequences.
Schema references: SCHEMA-218892276D — PredictionContext.; SCHEMA-8BB3882714 — PredictionArtifact..
Deterministic seed procedure: seed is derived from declared request/context/epoch/snapshot/environment plus explicit
nonce where exploration is intended; rerun equivalence is checked within declared stochastic semantics. Staleness: FRESH
→ CONTEXT_CHANGED → STALE → INVALID_FOR_OPERATION; rebind/recompute returns to FRESH only after validation.
PredictionSafetyGate: predicted catastrophic hard-constraint violation blocks/pre-empts the candidate path and
escalates; predicted safety is not proof of safety. Prediction ≠ Evidence. Future ≠ Knowledge. Governance escalation
cannot convert a forecast into fact.

9.4 OWNERSHIP / MAPPING CLOSURE
Prediction → Engine.Reason primary. Active Evidence Acquisition → Engine.Reason+Engine.Search+Engine.Research.
Evolutionary Landscape → Engine.Search+Capability.Knowledge. Intervention →
Engine.Reason+Capability.Compute.Execution+Engine.Proof. ConfidenceDebt →
Capability.Knowledge+Engine.Policy+Capability.Authority.Governance. Attention →
Capability.Authority.Governance+Engine.Explanation+HumanCore.Accessibility. No competing top-level architecture object
is created.

9.5 CURRENT TEN-STAGE VERIFICATION OPTIMIZATION CHAIN
DLR — Dependency-Local Reverification: reverify only affected dependency/obligation closure. VRR — Verification Result
Reuse: reuse prior result only if DesignEpoch, inputs, assumptions, dependencies, epistemic bindings and environment
remain valid. RCS — Redundant Check Suppression: identify logically equivalent/subsumed obligations; suppression carries
equivalence justification and invalidation linkage. ACD — Adaptive Conformance Depth: FAST/STANDARD/DEEP verification
according to risk, affected invariants, dependencies, uncertainty and prediction consequences; mandatory deep floors
cannot be optimized away. PCCD — Proof-Carrying Change Delta: change carries affected/invalidated proof obligations and
reusable proof context. PBG — Prediction Budget Governor: allocate predictive computation by expected information value
and risk; dangerous/uncertain predictions escalate rather than being starved. VWS — Verification Workload Scheduler:
schedule nonurgent work by risk, deadline, dependency criticality and resources; critical floors remain protected. EST —
Early-Stability Termination: terminate recursive verification/evolution when additional iterations produce no meaningful
validated delta and mandatory invariants remain satisfied; never use superficial convergence to skip unresolved hard
obligations. ESC — Epistemic Stability Cache: reuse stable epistemic assessments only while
snapshot/environment/dependency bindings remain valid. ECRG — Evolutionary Change ROI Gate: estimate expected validated
improvement vs expected cost/risk; reject/defer low-return evolution, subject to critical mandatory work.

Canonical dependency/order profile:
 DLR → VRR → RCS → ACD → PCCD → PBG → VWS → EST → ESC → ECRG
This is an optimization pipeline/profile, not a claim that every request literally executes all ten in serial order.

9.6 CURRENT TEN-STAGE RESOURCE / DATA-CENTER SET
SSC — Semantic State Compression: canonical references + deltas instead of duplicated semantic state. HCPT — Hot/Cold
Provenance Tiering: frequently accessed provenance on fast storage; cold immutable lineage on cheaper archival tiers
without loss of reconstructibility. CDM — Context Deduplication Mesh: share identical/near-identical
reasoning/verification contexts after semantic compatibility check. PAD — Proof Artifact Deduplication:
content-address/share identical proof artifacts/subgraphs with invalidation dependencies. EACP — Energy-Aware Compute
Placement: route permissible nonurgent work toward lower-cost/lower-energy-intensity compute subject to privacy,
locality, deadline, safety and authority. MPG — Memory Pressure Governor: retain intermediate state by expected
reuse/criticality under memory pressure; critical evidence/provenance cannot be evicted merely for convenience. BMV —
Bandwidth-Minimized Verification: move verification toward data/proof locality and avoid repeated transfer of large
artifacts while preserving verifier independence. CDS — Checkpoint Delta Storage: deltas against canonical checkpoints
plus strategic full checkpoints; reconstructibility is mandatory. WCE — Workload Coalescing Engine: combine compatible
work so shared computation is performed once; coalescing never creates false evidential/verifier independence. RAEG —
Resource-Aware Evolution Gate: resource-admit expensive evolution based on expected validated improvement and
compute/memory/energy/bandwidth/latency/money constraints.

Canonical operational profile:
 Incoming Work → SSC → CDM+PAD → RAEG → WCE → EACP+BMV → MPG → HCPT+CDS
Do not add GB + kWh + seconds + dollars into one meaningless scalar. Monetary ROI may be modeled as expected validated
value / monetary cost; compute, memory, energy, bandwidth, latency, privacy, safety and proof requirements remain
explicit constraints. ECRG asks “is the evolutionary change worth pursuing?”; RAEG asks “is this admitted work
resource-feasible/worth allocating now?” They are related but not collapsed.

Additional compatible efficiency mechanisms retained as implementation profiles where not semantically subsumed:
ProofCache; prediction-delta gating; tournament pruning; solver escalation ladder; bounded caches; adaptive proof
sampling where allowed; CRDT compaction; prediction Bloom filters; branch throttling; proof parallelization; idle-aware
scheduling; agent micro-budgeting. Context dedup belongs under CDM; proof dedup under PAD; duplicate names do not create
duplicate engines.

9.7 CONSTRUCTION DISTRUST / EPISTEMIC INDEPENDENCE — NORMALIZED FAMILIES
Role separation: Generator ≠ Reviewer ≠ Verifier ≠ Certifier ≠ Deployer for high-impact changes; one entity/model may
hold multiple roles only under explicitly justified low-risk policy, never by default for authority-changing work.

Invariant references: CND-001..008, EPI-001..006.
Contradiction classes:
 SAME | REFINEMENT | COMPATIBLE | CONTEXT_DIFFERENT | APPROXIMATION | TRUE_CONTRADICTION | UNRESOLVED.
ResolutionVector includes: provenance_integrity, source_independence, methodological_independence, context_alignment,
temporal_alignment, scope_alignment, evidence_strength, consistency.

9.8 HISTORICAL INTEGRITY / REPLAY / RECOVERY — CANONICAL LINEAGE SECTION 6D
Schema references: SCHEMA-14F6E848D0 — CommitContract.; SCHEMA-7419BCDD52 — RecoveryCertificate..
Invariant references: HIS-001..004.
Audits may be periodic, event-triggered or on-demand. Genesis reconstruction samples/fully replays according to
risk/budget. Timeout/incomplete replay is INCONCLUSIVE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, not “clean”.

SECTION 6E — CANONICAL EARTH BRANCH & RECURSIVE CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE ARCHITECTURE

STATUS: NORMATIVE — FIRST-CLASS ARCHITECTURAL LAYER
PLACEMENT: Canonical identifier 6E, immediately after Historical Integrity & Commit Replay. TOPOLOGY IMPACT: none; all
mechanisms map into the current typed architecture. PRIMARY ARCHITECTURE OBJECTS: Engine.Evolve, Capability.Knowledge,
Engine.Search,
Engine.Reason, Capability.State.EventService, Engine.Dependency,
 with Capability.Authority.Governance, HumanCore.Privacy, HumanCore.Constitution, Engine.Proof, Engine.Transition and
 Capability.Knowledge.Curation as required.

PRINCIPLE
Garden evolution is no longer only recursive within one system; it is recursive across the entire branch civilization
tree.

6E.1 BRANCH HIERARCHY

GARDEN
├── EARTH CANONICAL BRANCH — globally accepted Garden state at Earth scope
│ ├── 1. UNIVERSAL CORE
│ │ universally applicable accepted structures
│ ├── 2. CONTEXTUAL REGISTRY
│ │ nation / culture / environment / domain variants
│ └── 3. FRONTIER
│ unresolved alternatives, experimental branches, dissent,
│ hypotheses, rejected-but-preserved designs
├── Nation branches
├── City / Institution / Community branches
├── Personal Garden branches
└── Research / Experimental branches

Earth Canonical Branch is the sole canonical reference for Garden's accepted Earth-scope state. It is NOT the source
that makes propositions true.

 Reality → Evidence → Garden
 never Garden → Reality.

UNRESOLVED is a legitimate Earth-canonical state.

6E.2 BranchBinding — FIRST-CLASS ARTIFACT

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-3A08E38975 — BranchBinding..

UPSTREAM_VISIBLE:
 full permitted manifest.

UPSTREAM_ANONYMIZED:
 generalized/de-identified manifest.

UPSTREAM_BLOCKED:
 minimum existence + compatibility/security metadata only,
 unless a stronger sovereignty/privacy rule prohibits even that disclosure.

The root Earth branch is the declared canonical root and therefore has no ordinary parent binding.

6E.3 BranchChangeManifest — UPSTREAM-VISIBLE DESCRIPTION

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-753D72B589 — BranchChangeManifest..

Manifest disclosure is filtered by BranchBinding privacy_class, upstream_policy, HumanCore.Privacy,
HumanCore.Constitution and any stronger lawful sovereignty restriction.

6E.4 UPSTREAM AWARENESS RULE

Every legitimate Garden branch change MUST become discoverable by its ancestors, subject to privacy_class and
sovereignty constraints.

Upstream may know, as permitted:
 - that a change occurred;
 - affected invariants/components;
 - compatibility requirements;
 - that performance/evidence artifacts exist;
 - that a GeneralizationCandidate exists, if one was generated.

Upstream does NOT automatically know:
 - raw private data;
 - personal conversations;
 - sensitive personal records;
 - content prohibited by a stronger sovereignty/privacy rule.

Rule:
 upstream semantic awareness ≠ universal data surveillance.

6E.5 PROACTIVE MERGE / GENERALIZATION PIPELINE

Any descendant branch improvement can flow upward automatically:

 Descendant improves
 ↓
 Capability.State.EventService emits BranchChangeEvent
 ↓
 Engine.Dependency calculates relevance / dependency closure
 ↓
 Engine.Search finds analogous branches / structures
 ↓
 Engine.Reason asks: "Is this improvement general?"
 ↓
 Engine.Evolve creates GeneralizationCandidate
 ↓
 Independent verification / conformance
 ↓
 Earth-branch comparison
 ↓
 Governance where required
 ↓
 MERGE | REJECT | REMAIN_LOCAL | UNRESOLVED

"Proactive" means Garden does not require a human author to remember to submit every generalizable improvement manually.
It does not bypass privacy, authority, proof, resource, Safety or Constitution constraints.

6E.6 GeneralizationCandidate

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-0CC3FE52D1 — GeneralizationCandidate..

The candidate explicitly separates potentially universal structure from local/contextual structure. Generalization is a
candidate operation; it does not itself widen scope or create truth.

6E.7 DownstreamPropagationPlan

When Earth canonical changes, affected descendant branches are NOTIFIED and REVALIDATED:

 Earth change
 ↓
 Impact analysis
 ↓
 Applicability check
 ↓
 Compatible / potentially affected descendants notified
 ↓
 Local revalidation
 ↓
 Local branch adopts / adapts / remains divergent as appropriate and authorized.

Rule:
 Local innovation may flow upward; global standard may flow downward.
 Neither direction may silently erase context.

Downstream propagation is notification + applicability + revalidation, not blind overwrite.

6E.8 EARTH FRONTIER — UNRESOLVED / EXPERIMENTAL / DISSENT

Earth Canonical Branch Frontier contains:
 - unresolved alternatives;
 - experimental branches;
 - dissenting designs;
 - hypotheses;
 - rejected-but-preserved designs;
 - discriminating evidence requirements;
 - historical alternatives retained for replay/counterfactual analysis.

UNRESOLVED is a valid canonical answer. Example:

 STATUS: UNRESOLVED
 Theory A: 0.55 support
 Theory B: 0.45 support
 Critical discriminating evidence: ...

The numerical representation in such an example is illustrative only; actual support must use the appropriate
Mathematical Representation Policy and epistemic semantics.

6E.9 MINIMUM COMMON CORE → GROWING CORE

 local discoveries
 → cross-branch comparison
 → generalization candidate
 → verification / contextual analysis
 → Earth Universal Core where justified.

Genuine contextual differences remain in the Contextual Registry. They are not forcibly homogenized.

General method:
 C0 = minimum independently supported common structure.
 For each meaningful divergence/exception:
 classify contextual difference / incompatibility / approximation /
 missing abstraction / missing evidence / error.
 If a justified broader abstraction exists, propose Δn.
 C[n+1] = C[n] + validated Δn.
 Otherwise preserve divergence.
 Stop at TerminationContract/resource bound or diminishing validated improvement.

Common ≠ universal. Exception ≠ error.

6E.10 INDEPENDENT 100 × 100 CONVERGENT TEAMS

Independent recursive convergence experiments feed the FRONTIER, not the Universal Core directly.

Experimental profile:
 - approximately 100 independent teams;
 - approximately 100 heterogeneous LLM/model roles/families per team where feasible;
 - teams begin from the same declared Garden baseline;
 - no inter-team dependency/copying during the independent generation phase;
 - compare exact, functional, architectural, theoretical, invariant and performance convergence,
 residual divergence, Pareto equivalence, path dependence and time-to-convergence.

Convergence, votes or popularity:
 - MAY nominate/generalize candidates;
 - CANNOT bypass Evidence, Proof, Constitution, Safety, Conformance,
 contextual applicability, authority or dissent handling.

A critical unresolved safety counterexample remains independently investigable regardless of majority.

6E.11 RECURSIVE CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE ARCHITECTURE

 EARTH CANONICAL GARDEN
 ▲
 │
 proactive generalization
 │
 ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐
 │ │ │
 Nation Nation Research
 │ │ │
 Cities Institutions Labs
 │ │ │
 Communities People Agents
 │ │ │
 └────── new ideas / evidence ───┘

Information flows BOTH upward and downward:
 Upward = local innovations → generalization → Earth Frontier / Universal Core.
 Downward = global standards → contextual applicability → local adaptation/revalidation.

This creates a recursive civilization-wide knowledge/evolution tree while retaining local context, privacy, sovereignty,
dissent and uncertainty.
