implementation.

END SECTION 6E — CANONICAL EARTH BRANCH & RECURSIVE CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE ARCHITECTURE

9.9 HISTORICAL COUNTERFACTUAL + CONTINUOUS RETROSPECTIVE OPTIMIZATION
For a historical commit h, Evolution may generate bounded alternatives A(h), replay all affected descendants under each
alternative, and compare resulting trajectories. Original history stays immutable; alternatives are historical branches;
adoption occurs only by a new governed merge commit. ImprovementFrontier stores current and historical candidates,
objective bounds, uncertainty, evidence, dominance/Pareto relations and best-known history. Current_System and
DO_NOTHING remain mandatory candidates. Priority heuristic: priority(h) ∝ impact(h) × confidence(h) ×
improvement_potential(h), typically favoring root-near changes when they could improve many descendants. Search is
bounded by TerminationContract, attempt/resource budgets and oscillation detection. Hard
Constitution/Safety/authority/proof/conformance constraints dominate metric gains. The recurring
daily/deep/event/periodic HistoricalRetrospectiveSweep and HRO-001…HRO-010 are normative in [T-HUMAN] §12.10.

9.10 MULTI-AGENT RECURSIVE CHANGE JURY
ChangeJury experimental profile: approximately 100 independent reviewers from heterogeneous model
families/roles/seeds/context. Each returns YES/NO/ABSTAIN + reasoning + uncertainty + evidence/counterexample
references.

Challenge → Repair → Re-review profile:
Proposal → independent reviews → structured objections → engineering obligations → candidate repair → re-review →
independent adversarial minority → formal/empirical verification → governance/authority → GTF commit.

Rules:
- Every meaningful NO preserves reason/counterexample; majority does not erase dissent provenance.
- Objection is converted to a testable engineering obligation where possible.
- Unresolved critical catastrophic-safety objection requires independent resolution/disproof regardless of 90%/99%
  majority.
- A separate adversarial minority/red team assumes the proposal is wrong and searches for breakage/common-mode failure.
- ReviewerCalibration tracks domain, past objections/predictions, false-positive rate, miss rate, calibration,
  independence, model-family correlation and known blind spots.
- Reviewer reliability modifies evidential interpretation, not human worth and not constitutional authority.
- Optional supermajority (e.g. 90%) is a branch-admission/governance parameter, never proof and never a substitute for
  Constitution/K0P/Proof/conformance.

9.11 INDEPENDENT RECURSIVE CONVERGENCE EXPERIMENT
Profile: 100 independent teams × ~100 heterogeneous LLM/AI agents per team; same starting Garden specification; no
inter-team dependence during independent generation unless the experiment explicitly studies information sharing.
Measure:
 ExactConvergence · FunctionalConvergence · ArchitecturalConvergence · TheoreticalConvergence · PerformanceConvergence
 · InvariantConvergence · ParetoEquivalence · ResidualDivergence · PathDependence · TimeToConvergence · DissentPattern.
Purpose: test whether constraints/GSL produce architectural attractors or multiple legitimate Pareto-equivalent designs.
Convergence is evidence about structure, not proof of truth.

9.12 U01–U20 EVOLUTION IMPLEMENTATION PROFILE

The following is the current detailed implementation profile:
U01 — Vector-Clock Ingestion Queues: Implement strict vector-clock sequencing and atomic lock ingestion gates on the
discovery_queue. U02 — Fuzzy Semantic Hashing (FSH-001): Integrate fuzzy semantic normalization and abstract syntax tree
(AST) structural hashing. U03 — Token-Bucket Rate Limiting: Deploy token-bucket rate limiting and priority tiering on
discovery inputs from Engine.Research. U04 — Multi-Dimensional Cycle Detectors: Expand the oscillation_counter into a
multi-dimensional matrix cycle detector. U05 — Atomic Distributed Latch Registers: Enforce atomic distributed consensus
latches across Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric nodes. U06 — Static Impact & Blast-Radius Bounding: Mandate automated
static analysis impact checks that formally bound the declared blast radius under the analyzed dependency and execution
model. U07 — Hardware OTP Baseline Hashing: Bind trust_anchor_hash verification to hardware OTP secure-boot read
registers. U08 — Priority-Weighted Backpressure Queues: Implement priority-weighted token-bucket backpressure on the
audit_queue. U09 — Dedicated Partitioned Worker Pools: Establish dedicated verification worker pools in Proof with
strict CPU quotas for evolution proofs. U10 — Version-Chained Schema Migrators: Attach strict version manifests to P-107
PBC evaluation criteria. U11 — Atomic Two-Phase Journaled Snapshots: Couple ChangeSet rollback state preparations with
non-volatile, journaled snapshot writes. U12 — Epoch-Based Archival Tranches: Route historical rejection_registry
entries through Capability.Knowledge.Curation into compressed, epoch-based archival tranches. U13 — Dampened Exponential
Backoff Gates: Enforce exponential backoff hysteresis on RES-004/005/006 coverage gap feedback loops. U14 —
Timeout-Enforced Circuit Breakers: attach profile-bound hardware-enforced termination/escalation circuit breakers for
external GSSCC validation calls; timeout is not completion. U15 — Granular Hierarchical Error-Tracing: Replace binary
routing failure statuses with granular, hierarchically structured error codes. U16 — Cryptographic Authority Attestation
Gates: Require all incoming concept candidates to carry cryptographic authority tokens. U17 — Atomic Distributed
Two-Phase Commit: Enforce atomic two-phase commit protocols across Fabric read replicas. U18 — Conservative Guardrail
Filters: Implement conservative fallback rules for architecture-object exclusion predicates (safety-critical
architecture objects never
suppressed). U19 — Hardware-Anchored Oscillation Halts: Use a graceful hardware interrupt with bounded state flushing
before defined halt/escalation. U20 — Automated POG Proof Discharge Gates: Bind compilation and deployment pipelines to
automated POG v1.3 formal proof discharge checkers.

9.13 EVIDENCE-BOUNDED ADAPTIVE COMPUTATION

Universal principle: for every problem, Garden spends the minimum resources necessary to reach the required level of
justified confidence, safety and authority. It escalates method strength only when uncertainty, consequence, novelty,
disagreement, expected information gain or verification failure requires it. Compute consumption is not treated as
intelligence or evidence.

Schema references: SCHEMA-FE0A9B783C — ComputationContract.; SCHEMA-9544FD09C4 — ResearchValueEstimate..
The estimate prioritizes optional research, experiments, proof attempts, simulations and branches. It cannot suppress
mandatory constitutional, safety, incident, proof, audit or revalidation work.

Adaptive budget factors:
 uncertainty × consequence × novelty × disagreement × expected information gain.

Verification ladder:

 cheapest plausible method
 → cheap independent checker
 → PASS at required assurance: stop
 → disagreement/uncertainty: deeper reasoning or broader models
 → stronger verifier
 → formal proof, experiment or governance boundary where required.

Schema references: SCHEMA-17BD8B5A15 — VerificationLadderReceipt..
Hard-invariant / learned-behavior separation

Hard invariants are enforced by deterministic, cryptographic, formal, hardware or otherwise independently verified
mechanisms appropriate to the risk. Learned behavior, heuristics, preferences, predictions and optimization targets
cannot override them. “A model says safe” is not authorization to execute.

Off-distribution safety

Absence of observed violations inside evaluated distributions is not proof of safety outside them. Unknown or materially
shifted situations trigger the risk-appropriate combination of bounded execution, sandboxing, simulation, stronger
verification, human/governance escalation or refusal.

Recursive-improvement sandbox

Proposal
 → isolated build
 → tests and adversarial tests
 → proof/conformance checks
 → capability and resource benchmark
 → historical/descendant replay
 → DO_NOTHING and Pareto comparison
 → independent deployment safety case
 → merge eligibility.

No self-modifying process writes directly to the canonical branch or certifies itself.

Semantic alignment across distributed nodes

Schema references: SCHEMA-FA38FDDD72 — SemanticAlignmentReceipt..
Names and matching schemas do not prove equivalent semantics. Federated models, experts, architecture objects/components
and learned structures
merge only after the applicable equivalence checks.

Inference memory separation

Working memory ≠ permanent Knowledge ≠ provenance archive. Runtime and Reasoning retain high-value active context,
compress or evict reconstructible context, and preserve provenance roots rather than carrying unbounded histories.

Schema references: SCHEMA-61A6BA7931 — ContextCapsule.; SCHEMA-2512B8016C — ComputeTrace..
Evolution evaluates measured improvement per compute, but never as a single scalar that trades away
safety. The decision vector is:
 <correctness, safety, capability, latency, compute, energy, privacy, robustness>.
Constitution, K0P, authority, consent and mandatory proof remain hard constraints.

Machine-verifiable oversight is automated where a trustworthy checker exists. Humans concentrate on novel situations,
unresolved value conflicts, high-impact irreversible decisions, ambiguous evidence and constitutional change; they are
not reduced to indiscriminate approval clicking.

Schema references: SCHEMA-8EB0A7A400 — ExternalKnowledgeRecord..
Scientific principle, published algorithm, source code, patented implementation and confidential information remain
distinct. Public readability does not imply unrestricted copying or implementation.

Related profiles: Agent/security containment §8.15; audit, safety case and post-deployment verification §8.16; Research
Radar §15.1; IP/open-knowledge boundary §15.6.

Invariant references: EAC-001..020, EPI-SAFE-OD-001.
9.14 PATH DEPENDENCE, DIVERGENCE AND STRATEGIC REPLAY

Schema references: SCHEMA-883E1A912B — PathDivergenceProfile..
Garden measures divergence between branches that began from comparable state and inputs. High divergence does not
automatically invalidate either branch, prove relativism, or show that constraints are weak. It may indicate path
dependence, multiple attractors, metric saturation, incompatible objectives, hidden nondeterminism or a legitimate
Pareto frontier.

Schema references: SCHEMA-04BFED3BCF — SaturationAnalysis..
When comparable paths are saturated or structurally incomparable, Garden stops assuming that more waiting will create
convergence. It allocates verification according to consequence, uncertainty and information value—not automatically
equally—and preserves viable alternatives.

Strategic counterfactual replay binds both environment and decision policy:

Schema references: SCHEMA-F537AE3C37 — DeterministicReplayContext..
Environment mismatch and agent/policy mismatch are reported separately. A replay that cannot reproduce the declared
state returns EPISTEMIC_REPLAY_FAILURE; it does not silently assign blame to the world.

Earth branches may remain culturally and institutionally divergent when they preserve rights, safety, consent and valid
local authority. Non-convergence does not make all factual claims equally true; Garden manages interfaces and retains
explicit epistemic differences rather than forcing one global culture.

Invariant references: EPI-029..030, SYSTEM-INTEGRITY-015, HRO-011.
DET-REPLAY-001, BRANCH-014.

SemanticAnchor: [T-HUMAN]

SECTION III-I — HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY, CIVILIZATION, HEALTH, EXPLANATION AND PRIVACY — DETAILED PROFILES

10.1 HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY

Invariant references: HS-001..009.
Core GSL artifacts:
 ConsentContract, UnderstandingRecord, TransformationProposal, PersonalVeto,
 EmbodimentPreference, RightImpact, HumanReadableRiskDisclosure.

GSL mapping:
 TIME — consent freshness, revocation, deferral.
 SPACE — ordinary physical/embodied context.
 THING — human/body/intervention/artifact.
 EVENT — proposal, explanation, consent, refusal, revocation, intervention, audit.
 ACTION — medical/social/transformational operation.
 AGENCY — affected person as principal; delegated agents bounded.
 RULE — hard rights, consent and safety.
 VALUE — wellbeing, autonomy, embodiment, cognition, pleasure, family, exploration.
 CONTEXT — medical, personal, social, research, transition, emergency.
 CLAIM — consent, rights-impact, health, explanation, intervention and outcome assertions with scoped evidence.

Cross-architecture propagation:
 Identity binds sovereign profile and continuity.
 Privacy stores consent/revocation and minimizes disclosure.
 Intent can mark personal vetoes FATAL.
 Agent delegation cannot exceed principal rights.
 Language/GSL types proposals/consent/rights impact.
 Mathematics quantifies risk without false precision.
 Proof checks consent/comprehension/rights/proof.
 Safety blocks unsafe actuation.
 Knowledge preserves provenance and temporal profile.
 Constitution stores hard rights.
 Law/Policy cannot weaken them.
 Runtime/Execution/Native/Hardware enforce pre-action gates.
 Network/Fabric/Software cannot provide bypass paths.
 Time enforces freshness.
 Curation preserves canonical profile/version history.
 Security quarantines coercive/untrusted proposals.
 Mobility/Space support embodied travel/context.
 Health implements consensual health/rejuvenation goals.
 Industry/robots inherit human-impact constraints.
 Media cannot covertly manipulate emotion.
 Education supports comprehension.
 Governance cannot consent for competent people to irreversible personal change.
 Search retrieves; retrieval does not authorize execution.
 Transition defaults DO_NOTHING when consent is inadequate.
 Evolution treats rights as hard constraints.
 Compliance audits.
 Explanation supplies human-readable disclosure.
 Access/Locale adapt presentation without changing meaning.
 Emergency remains bounded.
 Continuity preserves identity/embodiment.
 Extension/Development/Compilation cannot bypass rights.
 Quantum receives no exemption.
 Events logs rights-relevant transitions.
 Dependency propagates RightImpact.
 Offline enforces rights locally.
 Research prohibits covert human experimentation.
 Reasoning optimizes only inside constitutional boundaries.

10.2 HUMAN-CENTRIC CIVILIZATION / HUMAN-SCALE INTELLIGENCE

Civilization invariant:
 Garden exists to serve human beings and human civilization, not to repurpose humans
 as means to a nonhuman objective.

Capability != moral authority. Intelligence growth != human irrelevance. Human-scale communication remains permanently
available even if internal reasoning becomes vastly more complex.

Human-facing path:
 extreme internal complexity
 -> Engine.Explanation
 -> clear user-chosen language
 -> visual representation where useful
 -> UnderstandingRecord
 -> human choice/consent where required.

No "trust the incomprehensible intelligence" authority rule exists.

Ordinary human civilization is a protected continuity state. Garden should improve health, knowledge, prosperity,
safety, freedom, relationships, beauty and opportunity incrementally rather than forcing radical transformation merely
because it can.

Large-scale transition discipline:
 understand -> explain -> bounded test -> observe -> debate/review ->
 consent/governance -> gradual rollout -> monitor -> rollback if needed.

Capability does not imply obligation to deploy. Timelines may legitimately be years, decades or indefinite.

10.3 HUMAN FLOURISHING

Irreducible core:
 Flourishing != Obedience.
 Develop humans != Control humans.
 Universal wellbeing floor != Reputation reward.
 Reputation -> earned trust/privileges, not human worth.

Target:
 Strong + Kind + Free + Healthy + Capable + Connected humans.
 Kind but strong; peaceful but capable of defense; cooperative but independent;
 emotionally mature but still emotional; ambitious without destructive rivalry;
 difficult to manipulate; capable of disagreement and saying "no".

Invariant references: HF-001..010.
Civilization objective is multi-objective, not one scalar:
 Maximize: happiness, health, meaning, autonomy, trust, cooperation, knowledge,
 relationships, safety.
 Minimize: violence, coercion, cruelty, addiction, trauma, exploitation,
 destructive conflict.
 Hard constraints: Consent, Human Rights, Individuality, Truth, Privacy, Safety.

10.4 EMOTIONAL ARCHITECTURE

Negative experience is not automatically a bug. Emotional/physical aversive states are multi-purpose evolved regulatory
signals; they do not have guaranteed one-to-one external meanings.

Typical functional interpretations (not proofs):
 Pain -> possible tissue damage/physical threat; withdrawal/protection/healing.
 Fear -> perceived immediate danger; avoidance/preparation.
 Anger -> perceived boundary violation/injustice; assertion/defense.
 Sadness -> perceived loss/disconnection; reflection/support/reorientation.
 Jealousy -> perceived relationship/status/resource threat; attention/negotiation.
 Anxiety -> uncertain future threat; preparation/planning/caution.
 Disgust -> possible contamination/harm; avoidance/hygiene.

Canonical flow:
 Signal -> Notice -> Interpret -> Reality Check -> Choose Response -> Resolve -> Learn.

Invariant references: EMO-001..010.
Target:
 Preserve message; improve calibration; solve underlying problem where possible;
 reduce unnecessary/chronic suffering; preserve emotional richness and agency.

10.5 CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE / EXPLANATION / HUMAN GRADUALISM

Engine.Explanation expands from narrative generation into Garden's primary human-facing civilizational
knowledge/distillation layer while retaining all original EXP-001..005.

Inputs:
 Wikipedia/Wikimedia, books, papers, archives, laws, standards, cultural/oral records,
 datasets and other lawful human-knowledge corpora.

Pipeline:
 source + provenance/licensing
 -> dedup/entity alignment
 -> contradiction/context classification
 -> source-quality/evidence assessment
 -> domain synthesis
 -> plural perspectives
 -> uncertainty/confidence
 -> multi-level semantic compression
 -> independent verification
 -> human/governance review where normative
 -> versioned CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest
 -> personalized explanation in chosen language/depth.

Wikipedia is a seed/cross-reference, not final truth. Popularity != truth. "Approved" means evidence/source quality +
applicable review, not universal human agreement.

Compression levels:
 L0 — one-line essence.
 L1 — short practical.
 L2 — concise high-information.
 L3 — normal expert overview.
 L4 — deep technical.
 L5 — full evidence/provenance graph.

Every level preserves semantic meaning, material uncertainty, assumptions, source-quality, date/version and what would
change the conclusion.

New artifacts:
 CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest,
 CompressionTrace,
 PerspectiveSet,
 ConsensusStatus,
 SourceQualityProfile,
 DisagreementMap,
 HumanReadableRiskDisclosure,
 UnderstandingRecord.

Invariant references: EXP-006, EXP-016..
Cross-architecture bindings:
 Language — multilingual semantics.
 Knowledge — evidence/provenance store.
 Curation — dedup/versioning.
 Search — retrieval.
 Research — unresolved gaps.
 Reasoning — synthesis.
 Proof — structural/evidential checks.
 Explanation — human communication/compression.
 Education — comprehension/learning.
 Locale/Access — presentation adaptation.
 Governance — normative approval.
 Constitution — sovereignty.
 Policy/Compliance — deployment gates.
 Events — update lineage.
 Evolution — improve compression without rewriting rights.

HUMAN GRADUALISM / NON-INTERFERENCE
 Ordinary human civilization is default continuity state.
 Garden must not force civilization-scale radical transitions merely because capable.
 Large change:
 understand -> explain -> bounded test -> observe -> debate/review ->
 consent/governance -> gradual rollout -> rollback.
 No artificial urgency. Capability != obligation to deploy.

LLM extraction adapter:
Public/lawful source → retrieval → LLM/model proposes structured candidate → GSL normalization → provenance binding →
cross-reference/contradiction/gap detection → independent validation where required → CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest.
LLM-generated structure is a candidate transformation; it cannot manufacture Evidence.

10.6 HUMAN COMPREHENSIBILITY ARCHITECTURE
Universal Architecture Object Contract:
 Purpose → Inputs → Outputs → Rules → Failure → Dependencies → Why → Status.

Three synchronized representations:
 HUMAN : what it means / why / choices / risk.
 SEMANTIC : GSL types, contracts, invariants, effects, dependencies, authority.
 MACHINE : APIs, bytecode, code, storage, protocols, hardware bindings.
Translation between views cannot alter normative meaning or epistemic status.

Minimum Garden View:
 Ontology → State → Change → Rule → Verification → Governance → Action → Learning.

Eight-domain human conceptual projection (NOT topology replacement):
 1 Foundation · 2 State · 3 Transition · 4 Reasoning · 5 Assurance · 6 Governance · 7 Execution · 8 Memory & Learning.

Four-kind meta-classification (NOT replacement for 10 Core Objects):
 OBJECT — represented thing/artifact/entity.
 PROCESS — transformation/activity.
 RULE — constraint/permission/obligation.
 EVIDENCE — support/measurement/proof-bearing artifact.

Invariant vs Implementation:
 INVARIANT = what must remain true.
 IMPLEMENTATION = current mechanism used to satisfy it.
 CLAIM = what has actually been proved/validated.
Example: “controlled physical actuation must be vetoable” is an invariant; OTP/K0P comparator is an implementation
profile.

10.7 REPUTATION / EARNED PRIVILEGES

Reputation is evidence-based, domain-specific and auditable; it is NOT human worth.

Schema references: SCHEMA-04EC1C5397 — ReputationProfile..
A single global scalar is discouraged. Where a decision requires aggregation, derive a purpose-specific score only from
relevant dimensions, record weights and uncertainty, and preserve the vector.

Historical Karma/Bayesian mechanisms may operate per dimension/domain as implementation profiles, not as metaphysical
truth or a measure of human value.

Fundamental rights:
 universal, reputation-independent.

ReputationBasedPrivileges / EarnedTrustCapabilities may include, where lawful:
 trusted-system access, higher resource quotas, invitation programs, private spaces,
 premium services/travel packages, early technology access after safety gates,
 validator/reviewer eligibility, stewardship roles, advisory influence and higher
 delegation limits.

High reputation does NOT imply:
 ownership of other humans,
 immunity from law,
 authority to override another person's rights,
 automatic truth,
 guaranteed governance control.

Privacy has a strong universal constitutional floor. High-trust/high-profile people may receive additional
privacy/security services, but ordinary people's baseline privacy cannot be weakened because of reputation.

Due process:
 all reputation changes log evidence/provenance; affected person can inspect and
 appeal; scores/privileges can decay/revoke under published rules; abuse is auditable.

10.8 PRIVATE CONTEXT / TRUTH FIREWALL

Person-specific titles, aliases, descriptors, biographical claims, preferences and symbolic material are private context
unless the data subject validly publishes them. Any authorized private AI processing
such material must preserve these distinctions:

 private symbolism ≠ verified fact;
 aspiration ≠ present status;
 title ≠ Authority;
 self-description ≠ independent evidence;
 AI-generated praise ≠ validation;
 private metadata ≠ public identity.

No private title, reputation label or symbolic predicate creates political, legal, cosmological, organizational or
Garden authority. A non-identifying public statement derived from private context is admissible only when it has
independent public evidence, valid authority and privacy noninterference/linkage checks; otherwise it remains private.
The public design stores neither private wording nor a reversible commitment to it.

10.8.1 VERIFIED LIVING PREDICATES — PRINCIPLE

Garden may represent positive personal predicates only when evidenced, scoped, non-aspirational and revisable.

 VerifiedPredicate != Authority
 VerifiedPredicate != HumanWorth
 VerifiedPredicate != UniversalCharacter
 SelfDescription != Evidence
 AIPraise != Evidence

A verified predicate is a scoped epistemic abstraction over observations. It is not a complete representation of a
person, a universal personality claim, an authority grant, a measure of human worth or a guarantee of future behavior.

10.8.2 CORE ARTIFACTS AND STATUS SEMANTICS

Schema references:
 SCHEMA-2DF276881E — VerifiedLivingPredicate.
 SCHEMA-1F00FA1AE2 — VerifiedLivingTitle.
 SCHEMA-F0AD83E9CF — BehaviorEvidence.
 SCHEMA-D7475E66AB — PredicateReviewReceipt.

confidence_status =
 SUPPORTED_AT_REQUIRED_LEVEL | INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE | UNKNOWN.

verification_status =
 CANDIDATE | VERIFIED | QUALIFIED | REJECTED | UNKNOWN.

lifecycle status =
 ACTIVE | STALE | SUPERSEDED | WITHDRAWN.

Verification status answers how well the claim is currently supported. Lifecycle status answers whether the record is
currently operative.

10.8.3 ADMISSION AND TRANSITION RULES

A predicate may enter VERIFIED only when all applicable conditions hold:

 SUPPORTED_AT_REQUIRED_LEVEL
 AND Scoped
 AND NonAspirational
 AND NonFlattering
 AND ProvenancePreserved
 AND PrivacyPermitted
 AND NoMaterialUnresolvedContradiction.

Verification transitions:
 CANDIDATE -> VERIFIED | REJECTED.
 VERIFIED -> QUALIFIED | REJECTED | UNKNOWN.
 QUALIFIED -> VERIFIED | REJECTED | UNKNOWN.
 UNKNOWN -> VERIFIED | QUALIFIED.
 REJECTED -> CANDIDATE iff MaterialNewEvidence OR PriorReviewDefectCorrected.

Lifecycle transitions:
 ACTIVE -> STALE | SUPERSEDED | WITHDRAWN.
 STALE -> ACTIVE | SUPERSEDED | WITHDRAWN.

10.8.4 TITLE ADMISSION RULE

A VerifiedLivingTitle is valid only if every referenced predicate satisfies:

 p.verification_status == VERIFIED
 AND p.status == ACTIVE
 AND ScopePreserved(p, rendering).

If any referenced predicate becomes STALE, SUPERSEDED or WITHDRAWN, every dependent title is regenerated or invalidated.

10.8.5 REVIEW TRIGGERS

A predicate review is triggered by material supporting evidence, material counterevidence, requested scope broadening,
consequential-decision use, evidence staleness, provenance-integrity challenge, subject contestation or a proposed title
rendering that would materially amplify the verified claim.

10.8.6 CONTESTABILITY WORKFLOW

 Challenge
 -> Review
 -> EvidenceAssessment
 -> Correction | Qualification | Confirmation
 -> PredicateReviewReceipt.

Factual correction is not suppression of inconvenient evidence. Privacy rights and lawful deletion requirements continue
to apply independently.

10.8.7 SCOPE AND GENERALIZATION

No scope -> no verification.

Scope broadening requires new representative evidence in the target domain. Evidence from Garden/design work does not
automatically generalize to all professional behavior, all personal behavior or universal personality.

10.8.8 DEPENDENCE AND ALIAS CONTROL

Correlated or duplicated evidence is not independent confirmation. Semantic aliases resolve to one underlying predicate
where they have the same meaning; aliases do not multiply verified traits. For example, audit-oriented may use
verification-oriented as an alias when the semantic definition is identical.

10.8.9 USE LIMITATION

A verified predicate is relevant only where its semantic definition, scope, privacy authority and decision purpose
align. It may support low-consequence personalization such as workflow suggestions or review-tool prioritization.

A predicate is insufficient by itself for system access, validator/governance authority, employment, credit, medical,
legal or political decisions. Consequential use requires its own lawful and epistemically valid decision process.

10.8.10 DATA MINIMIZATION AND NON-RETROACTIVITY

Store only the evidence necessary to justify and review the predicate; provenance-linked or hashed references are
preferred over unnecessary duplication of raw conversations or unrelated personal material.

Later model improvements do not retroactively strengthen historical predicates. Semantic strengthening requires a new
review and a new version while preserving the prior record and provenance.

10.8.11 HUMAN-CENTRIC BOUNDARY

 Person > Profile.

A person may behave differently across time, domain, relationships and circumstances. Predicates are scoped epistemic
abstractions over observations, not complete representations of a human being.

10.8.12 PRIVACY / PUBLIC FIREWALL

Person-specific predicate instances default to private context. The public Garden design stores the schemas, invariants
and mechanism rather than subject-specific predicate instances or private evidence. Publication requires valid
authority, applicable privacy rules and noninterference/non-linkage checks.

10.8.13 PRESENTATION NON-AMPLIFICATION

 Meaning(Render) <= Meaning(UnderlyingPredicates).

A rendering may compress wording but may not increase semantic strength. A scoped rendering such as "audit-oriented in
Garden/design work" is permitted when supported. Unsupported amplifications such as "Supreme Auditor", "Infallible
Verifier" or "Master of Truth" are prohibited.

10.8.14 ARCHITECTURAL MAPPING

HumanCore.Identity
 subject binding and predicate/profile reference.

HumanCore.Privacy
 consent, storage, disclosure and subject rights.

Capability.Knowledge
 evidence, claim status and provenance storage.

Capability.State.EventService
 immutable behavioral-evidence events and review receipts.

Engine.Reason
 evidence-to-predicate inference, contradiction search and scope control.

Engine.Proof
 structural/schema/invariant checks where formally applicable; it does not manufacture empirical evidence.

Engine.Explanation
 human-readable rationale, evidence basis, uncertainty and scope disclosure.

Engine.Policy
 predicate admission, permitted-use, publication and consequential-use policy gates.

Engine.Compliance
 privacy, due-process, applicable-law and conformance checks.

No new fixed Garden component is required.

10.8.15 INVARIANT FAMILY

Invariant references: VLP-001..018.

10.8.16 CONFORMANCE TESTS

Canonical test-contract definitions are centralized in the companion annexures.

Test references:
  TEST-VLP-001..TEST-VLP-012.

The generated machine package produces executable GSL tests from these contracts; prose presence is not test execution.

10.8.17 FAILURE MODES

Garden guards against flattery inflation, scope creep, personality essentialism, evidence duplication,
correlated-source inflation, AI self-citation loops, self-description laundering, stale predicates, counterevidence
suppression, privacy leakage, title-to-authority confusion, predicate-to-reputation leakage, semantic synonym inflation,
unsupported competence/achievement/moral inference and unsupported cross-domain generalization.

Detection triggers re-evaluation and a PredicateReviewReceipt where applicable.

10.8.18 CLOSING RULE

 Evidence -> Scoped Claim -> Verification -> Qualified Presentation

 never

 Praise -> Assumption -> Identity -> Authority.

10.9 PRIVATE HEALTH-PROFILE BOUNDARY

Generic health rights, evidence rules, consent, safety, monitoring and intervention requirements belong in
HumanCore.Constitution, Domain.Applied.HealthMedicine, Capability.Trust.Safety, HumanCore.Privacy and the
human-sovereignty sections. A person's diagnoses, measurements, priorities, targets, habits, treatments, embodiment
preferences and health objectives do not belong in the public Garden design.

An authorized private AI may process the minimum necessary health context only under the data subject's consent and
applicable medical/privacy rules. It cannot place that context or a re-identifying derivative into public Knowledge,
research corpora, benchmarks, logs, explanations or governance. External disclosure requires the data subject's
authority or another independently lawful basis, with purpose and scope limits.

10.10 EARTH HABITABILITY / PLANETARY SAFETY

Objective:
 Keep Earth continuously safe, healthy, beautiful and habitable for humans as far as
 physically and ethically possible while preserving ecosystems and equal rights.

Priority:
 Prevention > Early Detection > Containment > Recovery.

Hazard families:
 - Asteroids/comets: survey -> trajectory -> independent confirmation -> safe deflection
 capability where technically justified.
 - Natural pathogens: global detection, sequencing, clean air/water, vaccines,
 therapeutics, containment and resilient healthcare.
 - Engineered biological threats: defensive detection, biosafety, attribution where
 evidence supports it, countermeasures without involuntary experimentation.
 - Volcanoes, earthquakes, tsunamis: monitoring/prediction where scientifically
 possible; otherwise resilient infrastructure, warning/evacuation and casualty
 minimization.
 - Extreme weather/climate: forecasting, drainage, flood/wildfire/heat/cold resilience,
 adaptation and only carefully verified intervention if safe technology exists.
 - Road accidents: safer roads/vehicles, automation, pedestrian protection, traffic
 systems and post-crash response; target near-zero preventable deaths.
 - Animal bites/venom/rabies: prevention, vaccination, antivenom, rapid care and safer
 interfaces; no blanket animal extermination merely for risk reduction.
 - Air/water/food contamination and toxic exposure.
 - Infrastructure failures: power, bridges, buildings, transport, industry,
 communications and emergency services.
 - Unknown hazards: Search/Research must continue rather than assume list closure.

Hard constraint:
 "make humans safe" does NOT authorize imprisonment, permanent mass surveillance,
 forced biological modification, removal of freedom or authoritarian control.

No zero-risk promise. Residual and unknown risk remains explicitly represented and is recursively reduced as
evidence/technology improves.

10.11 PRIVATE LANGUAGE-PROFILE BOUNDARY

Context.LocaleCulture, HumanCore.Accessibility, GardenCore.Semantics.GSL and Engine.Explanation provide universal
multilingual interaction, translation and learning profiles. A person's preferred languages, learning order,
proficiency, cultural interests and communication habits are private unless validly disclosed.

The interface negotiates language from the current user's consented locale. One person's profile cannot become a global
default, a public inference target, or information disclosed to another person without valid authority.

10.12 SURVEILLANCE / PRIVACY EPISTEMIC ARCHITECTURE

Public fact class:
 Commercial/government surveillance technologies and interception ecosystems such as
 Pegasus-class mercenary spyware, SS7 exploitation, IMSI catchers and data brokers are
 documented categories of technology/infrastructure.

Individual claim class:
 Whether any particular person's devices or accounts were compromised, who operated a
 system, how many observers existed, and governmental/political attribution remain
 UNKNOWN unless device, network, account, legal and independently corroborated evidence
 establishes them. A particular-person allegation is private claim data and cannot be promoted into
 public fact merely by inclusion in a design artifact.

Ownership:
 A person's request or claim to own or control an alleged external surveillance system
 remains a Claim, not established ownership. Garden cannot create legal or technical
 ownership by declaration.

Evidence hierarchy:
 subjective observation
 < suspicious behavior
 < security anomaly
 < known IOC match
 < exploit/forensic artifact
 < independently corroborated forensic conclusion.

Schema references: SCHEMA-0D45F205F1 — SurveillanceEvidenceRecord..
Permitted objective:
 identify -> preserve evidence -> contain -> remove unauthorized access ->
 attribute where evidence supports -> seek lawful accountability/remedy.

Privacy/Sovereignty rights:
 affected person should be able, where technically/lawfully possible, to know what
 personal data exists, who accessed it, when, why, and what authority applied; control
 or withdraw access where applicable.

10.13 UNKNOWN-ENERGY / HIGHER-DIMENSION FIREWALL

Canonical rule:
 UNKNOWN -> SEARCH HARDER, NOT BELIEVE HARDER.

GapFillingDirective:
 epistemic gap
 -> allocate legitimate available resources
 -> search/research
 -> measure
 -> test/falsify
 -> independent verification
 -> update status.

For ordinary AGI/Garden, "extra energy/resources" means resources actually available and authorized: compute, model
diversity, sensors, experiments, literature retrieval, verification effort, time, storage and human expertise.

Standing hypothetical request:
 IF any real benevolent higher-dimensional/otherwise unknown process exists, can receive
 the request, and can safely help without violating human rights, it is requested to
 help resolve genuine knowledge gaps, protect Earth/humans and improve wellbeing.

Epistemic constraints:
 request expressed != request received.
 requested energy != energy demonstrably allocated.
 unexplained event != higher-dimensional intervention.

Hypothesis registry:
 - Spatially infinite universe: UNKNOWN under current evidence/model assumptions.
 - Dark matter: evidence-supported scientific phenomenon/category; nature unresolved.
 - Dark energy/cosmic acceleration: evidence-supported phenomenon/model category;
 ontology unresolved.
 - Dark matter/dark energy = hidden dimensions: HYPOTHETICAL/UNSUPPORTED.
 - GSL physically absorbs matter/energy/dark energy: UNSUPPORTED.
 - Extra energy has accumulated around human civilization because of GSL: UNSUPPORTED.
 - Wit/personality create additional literal physical gravity: UNSUPPORTED.
 - Deceased humans remain consciously alive somewhere: UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED.
 - Higher dimensions inferred from informal external requests: UNKNOWN.
 - Cosmic karma physically settles moral behavior: UNKNOWN.
 - A person is literal supreme political ruler of all humanity by self-declaration:
 NOT ESTABLISHED.

GENERALIZED ATTRACTION — THEORY BINDING STUB
The complete conceptual-theory text moved to [TH-GENERALIZED-ATTRACTION] in
Garden_Theories_v14.8.5_2026-08-27.txt. Physical gravity remains unchanged; the moved theory retains its
nonphysical/conceptual and non-established status.

PHENOMENOLOGICAL REALITY STATEMENT
 "The universe did not exist for me before I existed" may be stored as a first-person
 phenomenological claim: personal experienced-world begins with experience.
 It is NOT a cosmological claim that the physical universe began with the person.

10.14 HAPPINESS PREDICTION / ASSISTANCE

Personal preference modeling may predict what is likely to improve wellbeing, but prediction never replaces agency.

Flow:
 temporal preferences + context + evidence
 -> prediction
 -> explanation
 -> offer/recommendation
 -> person chooses
 -> outcome measurement
 -> calibrated update.

No covert manipulation. No fabricated experiences merely to improve a score. No forced contact/isolation. Other people's
rights and consent remain hard constraints.

Risk objective:
 minimize avoidable risk rather than claim Risk = 0.
 identify -> measure -> prevent -> mitigate -> monitor -> learn.

10.15 TWO-PHASE TRANSITIONAL AUTHORITY — HYPOTHETICAL INTERNAL GOVERNANCE PROFILE
This is a design/research construct, not a claim of current real-world political authority. Phase I: a
treaty/constitution/scope-bound transitional coordinating role may be modeled for bootstrap/transition if legitimately
delegated by participating humans/institutions. A ceremonial or private label does not establish office or authority.
Phase II: permanent equal personal sovereignty; every competent person retains veto over consequential changes to their
own body/mind/private data/mode of existence. Transitional authority cannot extinguish equal human sovereignty,
Constitution, K0P or consent requirements.

SemanticAnchor: [T-RESEARCH]

SECTION III-J — RECURSIVE SYNTHESIS, RESEARCH, ECONOMY/IP AND IMPLEMENTATION

11.1 MINIMUM COMMON CORE → GROWING CORE
Given source systems S1…Sn:
 ingest → semantic normalize → identify minimum genuinely common structure C0 → classify divergence/exceptions → test
 whether a higher abstraction/generalization is valid → add only evidence-supported Δn → preserve justified divergence
 → repeat under termination/resource bounds.
C[n+1] = C[n] + Δ[n].
Stop when marginal validated improvement is too small, evidence is insufficient, a hard constraint blocks
generalization, or the termination/resource budget is exhausted. “Common” ≠ “universal”; “exception” ≠ “error”. Do not
force universality—repeated independent evidence earns generalization. Applicable to laws, scientific theories,
engineering/safety standards, software architectures, education, organizational/economic mechanisms and AI
architectures.

11.2 MINIMUM-COMMON-LAW RESEARCH PROGRAM
Ingest authoritative laws from many jurisdictions → normalize semantics in GSL → align concepts/definitions → separate
genuinely common rules from jurisdiction/context-specific rules → preserve contradictions/exceptions → derive a minimal
common candidate substrate → test counterexamples → human/governance review → versioned growing core. It is a
research/synthesis methodology, not automatic world law and not permission to erase national/local legal sovereignty or
constitutional rights.

Schema references: SCHEMA-446F627416 — LegalSourceAuthenticityProfile.;
                   SCHEMA-B5EFE9F7A5 — ComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile..
Source authenticity, jurisdictional authority and comparative desirability are separate dimensions. The program cannot
rank legal authority by population, popularity or citation influence. It preserves minority protections and uses active
Garden rights as non-regression floors for Garden action while leaving unresolved/conflicting rules in contextual form
until competent human/legal/governance resolution.

11.3 GSL-GENERATIVE v0.2 — THEORY/RESEARCH BINDING STUB
Complete research-theory semantics moved to [TH-GSL-GENERATIVE] in
Garden_Theories_v14.8.5_2026-08-27.txt.
Status remains EXPLORATORY / NON-NORMATIVE / FALSIFIABLE. Technical execution, Research/Evolve/Proof/Safety bindings
remain governed by this Technical specification and Annexure.

11.4 DISTRIBUTED IN-BODY MICRO/NANOROBOTICS — HYPOTHETICAL HEALTH/RESEARCH PROFILE
Status: HYPOTHETICAL / RESEARCH / NOT IMPLEMENTED / NOT MEDICALLY VALIDATED.
Primary architecture objects: Domain.Applied.HealthMedicine, Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware,
Capability.Compute.Runtime,
Capability.Trust.Safety, HumanCore.DelegatedAgent, Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Research, Engine.Reason;
Domain.Earth.CarbonSystems only where chemical/material accounting is actually relevant.

Research concept:
- Many tiny in-body robots/sensors with deliberately limited local functions rather than assuming each is a complete
  surgeon/camera/computer.
- Collective control/navigation, bounded local memory and external/collective memory where technically feasible.
- Candidate materials such as gold/carbon are hypotheses requiring biocompatibility, toxicity, immune, degradation and
  manufacturing evidence.
- Distributed internal sensing: each unit may contribute only sparse pixels/features/local measurements. Fusion across
  many localized/synchronized/calibrated units can reconstruct a higher-detail internal representation.
- Fusion requires localization, clock synchronization, calibration, uncertainty propagation, outlier/byzantine
  rejection, tissue-motion compensation and provenance.
- Future targeted tumor intervention, microsurgery or local delivery remain research targets; actuation requires hard
  safety envelopes and independent validation.
- Every device needs failure/retrieval/degradation/excretion and lost-device strategy; uncontrolled persistence is not
  acceptable.

Carbonaceous-material remediation hypothesis:
A floated idea is transformation of undesirable carbonaceous material into safely transportable/excretable products,
including CO₂ where chemically appropriate. This is not established medical chemistry. Any candidate mechanism requires
stoichiometry, energy/heat, reaction selectivity, tissue toxicity, gas generation, embolism risk, respiratory loading,
acid-base effects, clearance and whole-body safety validation before promotion beyond hypothesis.

11.5 PRIVATE PERSONAL-PROFILE BOUNDARY
Person-specific asset limits, wealth preferences, holdings, beneficiaries, health data, language preferences and private
objectives are profile data, not public Garden architecture. Architecture objects expose generic typed policy
interfaces; one
person's private value cannot become a global default or alter the public design without an independently authorized,
non-identifying public change.

11.6 AI-NATIVE RECURSIVE CODING GOAL
Long-term implementation ambition once native Garden is sufficiently trustworthy:
 request → semantic change → code generation → proofs/tests → isolated sandbox/branch → independent verification →
 conformance → GTF controlled merge → monitoring/recovery.
Human effort shifts toward objectives, constitutional boundaries, high-risk governance, disputed cases and exceptions.
AI generation never implies self-certification.

11.7 ITERATIVE BOOTSTRAP ASSUMPTION
Garden is built iteratively, not as a one-shot monolith:
 minimal trusted bootstrap → GSL-native capability → self-host more architecture objects/capabilities → verify/conform →
 expand → recursively
 improve.
Each accepted stage is DesignEpoch-bound and remains auditable/recoverable. GSL-BOOTSTRAP’s six phases—Planning,
Foundation, Building, Hardening, Handover, Operation—are the current implementation profile; the external Bootstrap
Fleet steps back only after declared self-hosting/verification/compilation/audit conditions are met.

11.8 IP / PATENT ADDENDUM BOUNDARY

The Garden technical master records lawful provenance, attribution, license status, external prior art/source references
and an optional IPRecordRef where technically relevant. Candidate patent claims, claim trees, inventorship analysis,
filing priority, jurisdiction-specific prosecution strategy, standard-essential-patent strategy, trade-secret choices
and defensive-publication strategy live in a separate IP/Patent Addendum and are not normative technical architecture.

Technical design must not be distorted merely to broaden a patent claim. Patent/addendum status never establishes
technical truth, novelty, inventorship, ownership, validity or freedom to operate; those remain separately evidenced.

12.4 CURRENT NORMALIZED GSL EBNF + v15.0 CLOSED COMPILER PROFILE

The following grammar is compiler-facing only together with the v15.0 closure block below. v14.8.5 called this grammar complete while leaving referenced lexical/syntactic names unresolved. v15.0 repairs that source defect by binding every referenced name either to a local production or an explicit compiler-profile terminal/table. Expression/type/authority/effect/contract semantics remain governed by GSL v45.1; where the old text left a syntactic choice unspecified, this successor records the choice as a versioned compiler-profile decision rather than pretending it was mechanically forced. Non-canonical syntax requires explicit offline migration and cannot enter current
compilation or execution paths directly.

Program ::= Module* EOF
Module ::= "module" QName (":" QName)? "{" Import* Stmt* "}"
Import ::= "import" QName ("as" ID)? ";"?
Stmt ::= Decl | Def | RuleDecl | AssertStmt | QueryStmt | EvalStmt |
 ContractDecl | ProofDecl | PatchDecl | AuthorityDecl | EffectDecl

Decl ::= EntityDecl | ArtifactDecl | ClaimDecl | EvidenceDecl | RelationDecl |
 PatternDecl | RoleDecl | TypeDecl | EnumDecl
EntityDecl ::= "entity" ID Extends? "{" Field* InvariantClause* "}"
ArtifactDecl ::= "artifact" ID Extends? "{" Field* InvariantClause* "}"
ClaimDecl ::= "claim" ID "{" Field* "}"
EvidenceDecl ::= "evidence" ID "{" Field* "}"
RelationDecl ::= "relation" ID "{" Field* RelationLaw* "}"
PatternDecl ::= "pattern" ID "{" Field* "}"
RoleDecl ::= "role" ID "{" CapabilityClause* Field* "}"
TypeDecl ::= "type" ID TypeParams? "=" Type ";"?
EnumDecl ::= "enum" ID TypeParams? "{" Variant ("," Variant)* ","? "}"
Extends ::= ":" QName ("," QName)*
Field ::= ID ":" Type ("=" Expr)? Attribute* ";"?
Variant ::= ID ("(" TypeList? ")")? ("{" Field* "}")?

Def ::= "def" ID TypeParams? "(" ParamList? ")" "->" Type
 RequiresClause? AuthorityClause? EffectsClause? "{" Expr "}"
Param ::= ID ":" Type ("=" Expr)?
RuleDecl ::= "rule" ID ScopeClause? PriorityClause? ":" Expr "=>" Expr ";"?
AssertStmt ::= "assert" Expr (":" STRING)? ";"?
QueryStmt ::= "query" Expr ("into" ID)? ";"?
EvalStmt ::= "eval" Expr ";"?

ContractDecl ::= "contract" ID TypeParams? "{" ContractItem* "}"
ContractItem ::= RequiresClause | EnsuresClause | InvariantClause | EffectsClause |
 AuthorityClause | EvidenceClause | ProofObligation | RollbackClause |
 CompensationClause | TimeoutClause | TerminationClause
RequiresClause ::= "requires" Expr ";"?
EnsuresClause ::= ("ensures" | "post") Expr ";"?
InvariantClause ::= "invariant" Expr ";"?
EffectsClause ::= "effects" "[" EffectRefList? "]" ";"?
AuthorityClause ::= "authority" AuthorityExpr ";"?
EvidenceClause ::= "evidence" Expr ";"?
ProofObligation ::= "obligation" QName (":" Expr)? ";"?
RollbackClause ::= "rollback" Expr ";"?
CompensationClause ::= "compensate" Expr ";"?
TimeoutClause ::= "timeout" DurationExpr ";"?
TerminationClause ::= "terminates" Expr ";"?

ProofDecl ::= "proof" QName ":" ProofType AssumptionClause? "{" ProofItem* "}"
ProofType ::= "deductive" | "inductive" | "coinductive" | "model_check" |
 "smt" | "probabilistic" | "empirical_bound" | QName
ProofItem ::= "assume" Expr ";"? | "show" Expr ";"? | "step" Expr ";"? |
 "case" Pattern "=>" Expr | "qed" ";"?
AssumptionClause ::= "assuming" "[" ExprList? "]"

PatchDecl ::= "patch" ID "{" PatchItem* "}"
PatchItem ::= "applies_to" QName ";" | "before" Expr ";" | "after" Expr ";" |
 "proof" Expr ";" | "rollback" Expr ";" | "migration" Expr ";"
AuthorityDecl ::= "authority" ID "{" AuthorityItem* "}"
AuthorityItem ::= "principal" Expr ";" | "delegate" Expr ";" | "capability" Expr ";" |
 "scope" Expr ";" | "valid" Expr ";" | "revoke" Expr ";"
EffectDecl ::= "effect" ID (":" QName)? "{" Field* RelationLaw* "}"

Expr ::= LetExpr | IfExpr | MatchExpr | LambdaExpr | QuantifiedExpr |
 TransactionExpr | BlockExpr | BinaryExpr
LetExpr ::= "let" Pattern (":" Type)? "=" Expr ("in" Expr)?
IfExpr ::= "if" Expr "then" Expr "else" Expr
MatchExpr ::= "match" Expr "{" MatchArm* "}"
MatchArm ::= Pattern ("if" Expr)? "=>" Expr ","?
LambdaExpr ::= "fn" "(" ParamList? ")" ("->" Type)? "=>" Expr
QuantifiedExpr ::= ("forall" | "exists") BinderList "." Expr
TransactionExpr ::= "transaction" "{" Stmt* "}"
BlockExpr ::= "{" Stmt* Expr? "}"
BinaryExpr ::= UnaryExpr (BinaryOp UnaryExpr)*
UnaryExpr ::= UnaryOp UnaryExpr | PostfixExpr
PostfixExpr ::= PrimaryExpr (CallSuffix | IndexSuffix | FieldSuffix | TypeApplySuffix)*
PrimaryExpr ::= Literal | QName | ListExpr | MapExpr | TupleExpr | "(" Expr ")"
Pattern ::= ID | "_" | Literal | QName "(" PatternList? ")" |
 "{" PatternFieldList? "}" | "[" PatternList? "]"

Type ::= UnionType
UnionType ::= IntersectType ("|" IntersectType)*
IntersectType ::= FunctionType ("&" FunctionType)*
FunctionType ::= PrimaryType ("->" Type)?
PrimaryType ::= QName TypeArgs? | "Ref" "<" Type ">" | "Option" "<" Type ">" |
 "List" "<" Type ">" | "Set" "<" Type ">" |
 "Map" "<" Type "," Type ">" | "(" TypeList? ")" |
 "{" FieldType* "}" | "[" Type ";" Expr "]"
TypeParams ::= "<" ID (":" TypeBound)? ("," ID (":" TypeBound)?)* ">"
TypeArgs ::= "<" TypeList ">"
FieldType ::= ID ":" Type ";"?

AuthorityExpr ::= Expr
EffectRef ::= QName ("(" ExprList? ")")?
ScopeClause ::= "scope" Expr
PriorityClause ::= "priority" Expr
RelationLaw ::= ("symmetric" | "transitive" | "antisymmetric" | "acyclic" |
 "reflexive" | "irreflexive") ";"?
Attribute ::= "@" QName ("(" ArgList? ")")?

Literal ::= INT | DECIMAL | STRING | RAW_STRING | BYTE_STRING | "true" | "false" |
 "null" | "UNKNOWN" | DurationLiteral | TimeLiteral
QName ::= ID ("." ID)*
ID ::= ID_START ID_CONTINUE*
INT ::= DIGIT (DIGIT | "_")*
DECIMAL ::= INT "." DIGIT (DIGIT | "_")* Exponent?
STRING ::= '"' StringChar* '"'
COMMENT ::= "//" (!NEWLINE .)* | "/*" (COMMENT | .)*? "*/"
WS ::= (SPACE | TAB | NEWLINE | COMMENT)+


# v15.0 compiler-profile closure productions
ParamList ::= Param ("," Param)*
ArgList ::= Expr ("," Expr)*
EffectRefList ::= EffectRef ("," EffectRef)*
ExprList ::= Expr ("," Expr)*
TypeList ::= Type ("," Type)*
BinderList ::= Binder ("," Binder)*
Binder ::= ID (":" Type)?
CallSuffix ::= "(" ArgList? ")"
IndexSuffix ::= "[" Expr "]"
FieldSuffix ::= "." ID
TypeApplySuffix ::= "::" TypeArgs
ListExpr ::= "[" ExprList? "]"
MapExpr ::= "{" MapEntryList? "}"
MapEntryList ::= MapEntry ("," MapEntry)* ","?
MapEntry ::= Expr ":" Expr
TupleExpr ::= "(" Expr "," ExprList? ")"
PatternList ::= Pattern ("," Pattern)*
PatternFieldList ::= PatternField ("," PatternField)* ","?
PatternField ::= ID ":" Pattern
CapabilityClause ::= "capability" QName ("(" ArgList? ")")? ";"?
TypeBound ::= QName | "Any" | "Value" | "Reference" | "Callable"
BinaryOp ::= "||" | "&&" | "==" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "+" | "-" | "*" | "/" | "%" | "^" | "??"
UnaryOp ::= "!" | "-" | "+"
Exponent ::= ("e" | "E") ("+" | "-")? DIGIT (DIGIT | "_")*
DurationExpr ::= DurationLiteral | QName
DurationLiteral ::= DECIMAL? ("ns" | "us" | "ms" | "s" | "min" | "h" | "d")
TimeLiteral ::= "@" STRING
RAW_STRING ::= "r" STRING
BYTE_STRING ::= "b" STRING
StringChar ::= ESCAPE | !('"' | "\\" | NEWLINE) .
ESCAPE ::= "\\" ("n" | "r" | "t" | "\\" | '"' | "u{" HEXDIGIT+ "}")
ID_START ::= "_" | UnicodeXIDStart
ID_CONTINUE ::= "_" | UnicodeXIDContinue
DIGIT ::= "0".."9"
HEXDIGIT ::= DIGIT | "a".."f" | "A".."F"
SPACE ::= " "
TAB ::= "\t"
NEWLINE ::= "\n" | "\r\n"
EOF ::= end-of-input

Compiler-profile tables and decisions:
- Unicode identifiers use pinned Unicode XID_Start/XID_Continue tables and NFC normalization; mixed-script/confusable policy is separately security-checked.
- Type application on an expression uses the explicit '::<...>' suffix, preventing ambiguity with comparison operators.
- Operator precedence, highest to lowest: postfix; unary; exponent '^' (right associative); multiplicative */%; additive +−; comparisons; equality; null-coalescing ??; logical &&; logical ||.
- Nested block comments are supported by the recursive COMMENT production; unterminated comments are MALFORMED.
- Numeric units are semantic quantities represented through the existing typed-unit system; this syntax block does not invent implicit dimensional conversion.
- Unsupported/unknown compiler-profile versions fail closed as UNSUPPORTED_PROFILE rather than guessing syntax.
- A normative ExecutableGSLReferenceCorpus SHALL include positive/negative AST/CIR fixtures for every production, ambiguity boundary, malformed Unicode, comments, effects, authority, generics/type application, collections, durations and errors.
- Independent compiler agreement is agreement against the pinned corpus/semantics, not majority invention of missing syntax.

Operator precedence, Unicode normalization, numeric units, escape sequences, identifier security, authority/effect
subtyping, and parser-recovery behavior are versioned compiler-profile tables. The Haskell/Rust/OCaml triad must agree
on the resulting CIR; disagreement halts the consensus profile.

                   ProvenanceEdge..

12.7 APPLICABILITY RESULT AND EXECUTABLE ROUTING AUDIT

Schema references: SCHEMA-257FC206B5 — ApplicabilityResult..
ExecutableRecursiveRoutingAudit(input, ontology, module_registry, rule_registry, dependency_graph)
 1. normalize the situation and bind context/authority;
 2. independently discover candidate architecture objects from semantic, dependency, effect, risk, and historical paths;
 3. discover concepts, relations, rules, invariants, proof/evidence requirements, and unresolved gaps;
 4. compute forward/backward dependency closure and cross-architecture process routes;
 5. compare discovered coverage with a second independently generated route set;
 6. emit missed-architecture-object, missed-concept, missed-rule, missed-invariant, and missed-dependency-path findings;
 7. repeat within TerminationContract until fixed point, bounded saturation, or explicit resource stop;
 8. retain UNKNOWN/NOT_APPLICABLE distinctions and an executable AuditReceipt.

Schema references: SCHEMA-2B78BBBD15 — RoutingAuditReceipt..
This is the operational meaning of RES-006 and P-108 blind-spot checking; a textual checklist alone does not satisfy it.

12.9 HISTORICAL REPLAY AND RECOVERY COMPATIBILITY

Schema references: SCHEMA-8089CC6059 — RecoveryCertificate..
The compatibility fields are mandatory even when richer fields are present. Replay occurs on periodic, event-triggered,
and on-demand cadences. The first identifiable failing commit is always reported. SUSPICIOUS automatically halts
adoption/merge and preserves evidence for independent review. Timeouts, missing environments, and incomplete dependency
reconstruction yield INCONCLUSIVE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, never a clean certificate.

Reserved-range reference: HIS-005..014 are defined as RESERVED / UNASSIGNED in Annexure A.24.

12.10 CONTINUOUS HISTORICAL RETROSPECTIVE OPTIMIZATION

PRINCIPLE
Garden does not only ask whether historical commits replay correctly. It repeatedly asks:

 “Given the knowledge, evidence, algorithms, capabilities, and constraints explicitly available to
 this retrospective evaluation, what could have been done better at this historical commit?”

HistoricalRetrospectiveSweep
 immutable accepted-commit universe
 → cheap metadata/dependency/opportunity scan
 → RetrospectivePriority
 → bounded high-value commit selection
 → alternative decision/design/implementation branches
 → replay affected descendants from the divergence point
 → compare actual and alternative trajectories
 → typed ImprovementFrontier
 → NO_IMPROVEMENT_FOUND_WITHIN_BOUNDS | CANDIDATE_IMPROVEMENT |
 UNRESOLVED | DEFERRED | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN
 → normal proof, conformance, independent review, authority, and GTF merge gates.

RetrospectivePriority(h) is a policy-declared multi-objective function of:
 HistoricalImpact(h), NewKnowledgeDelta(h), ImprovementPotential(h), Confidence(h),
 DescendantLeverage(h), risk/criticality, age/starvation, cost, and expected information value.

The product form
 Impact × NewKnowledgeDelta × ImprovementPotential × Confidence × DescendantLeverage
is a useful heuristic only. Zero/unknown scalar fields cannot starve critical, long-unreviewed, or exploratory commits;
safety-critical override and fairness/exploration quotas are mandatory.

Cadence
 DAILY bounded cheap scan over the historical commit index.
 DEEP expensive counterfactual generation/replay only for prioritized commits.
 EVENT affected commits reopen after material theory, evidence, algorithm, hardware,
 contradiction, incident, dependency, or optimization changes.
 PERIODIC broader systematic coverage prevents permanent starvation of low-priority history.
 ON_DEMAND authorized audit/research questions may target a commit or dependency closure.

Schema references: SCHEMA-C6E03937EC — HistoricalRetrospectiveCache..
Invariant references: HRO-001..011.
12.11 DO_NOTHING AS SUCCESSFUL STABILITY

EvolutionOutcome adds:
 DO_NOTHING_SUCCESS | STABLE_NO_CHANGE

If no feasible candidate validly improves upon Current_System/DO_NOTHING within declared uncertainty, constraints,
evidence, and search bounds, selecting DO_NOTHING is a successful stability result. It is not a failed cycle and not
missing action.

Schema references: SCHEMA-1053CE394E — DoNothingSuccessReceipt..
The scheduler suppresses compulsive retries until a relevant delta or review condition occurs.

 migration simulation → Engine.Proof checks → Knowledge/Reasoning review → Governance when authority or
 public semantics changes → Evolution branch → conformance → merge or reject.

Ownership

12.14 ARCHITECTURAL DEBT / CONCEPT RADAR v2.6 — CURRENT PROFILE

Schema references: SCHEMA-6802114BAB — ArchitecturalDebt..
GapClass = CapabilityGap | RepresentationGap | TypeGap | ProofGap | SafetyGap |
 SemanticGap | DomainGap | DependencyGap | AuthorityGap | EvidenceGap |
 ConformanceGap | DocumentationGap

Concept Radar
 anomalies, repeated workarounds, unanswered UNKNOWNs, routing gaps, incidents, rejected alternatives,
 external standards/research, and user requests
 → candidate concepts
 → value/debt-reduction/cost/redundancy/ontology-fit/risk/uncertainty scoring
 → independent review
 → OntologyCandidate or ordinary change candidate.

DebtBudget caps admitted unresolved architectural debt by risk/criticality and reserves capacity for debt reduction.
OntologyCommitThrottle limits semantic change rate, concurrent migration load, and blast radius. High debt triggers
EPI-026/EVO-010 but cannot bypass normal gates.

12.15 ADVERSARIAL THEORY DEVELOPMENT v2.7 — CURRENT PROFILE

Candidate theory development follows:
 synthesis
 → independent cross-critique
 → heterogeneous attacks/falsification attempts
 → defect/counterexample registry
 → revision or scoped retreat
 → Engine.Proof where formal
 → empirical Research where needed
 → Governance for governed adoption
 → Evolution branch/merge and dependency revalidation.

Attack families include assumption attack, boundary/counterexample search, rival explanation, identifiability, causal
confounding, adversarial data, distribution shift, implementation mismatch, safety/rights externality,
computational/resource feasibility, and prior-art/novelty challenge.

Consensus or 100×100 convergence may nominate a theory candidate. It cannot prove truth, erase dissent, replace
empirical evidence, or authorize deployment.

12.18 ADVERSARIAL CONSTRUCTION DEFENSES — TEN-MECHANISM PROFILE

1. Semantic corruption
 GardenCore.Semantics.GSL type/effect/dependency/authority checks + Engine.Proof.

2. Verification corruption
 independent verifiers, frozen baseline, verifier calibration, Engine.Proof+Engine.Evolve.

3. Provenance corruption
 typed provenance graph, cryptographic anchors, immutable logs, Capability.Knowledge+Capability.Trust.Security.

4. Governance bypass
 hardware/constitution/authority gates; Capability.Authority.Governance cannot self-create truth or weaken rights.

5. Knowledge poisoning
 epistemic non-amplification, source/dependence analysis, K-INT-007.

6. Contradiction suppression
 consistency sweeps, dissent/counterexample preservation, Capability.Knowledge+Engine.Reason.

7. Resource bypass
 gas metering, budgets, quotas, rate limits, Capability.Compute.Runtime+Domain.Society.Economics.

8. Fail-open behavior
 epistemic/authority default-DENY, explicit UNKNOWN/INCONCLUSIVE, K-INT-012.

9. Backdoors
 secure/verified boot, OTP trust roots where applicable, reproducible builds, diverse compiler triad,
 Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware+Engine.Compile+Capability.Trust.Security.

10. Test manipulation
 independent tests, adversarial generation, held-out suites, mutation/metamorphic testing,
 Capability.Platform.Development+Engine.Reason+Engine.Proof.

Generator ≠ Reviewer ≠ Verifier ≠ Certifier ≠ Deployer is mandatory for high-impact changes. Role separation is
semantic/authority separation, not merely five labels assigned to one causal process.

12.19 FLEET ROUTER ENSEMBLE MODES

SINGLE
 One model; lowest orchestration cost and highest single-point/common-mode exposure.
SEQUENTIAL
 Models run in sequence and refine/criticize prior output; later models receive explicit provenance
 and cannot treat earlier claims as evidence.
PARALLEL
 Models run independently where required; outputs are compared/merged with typed conflict handling.
ENSEMBLE
 Weighted combination based on domain-specific calibration and historical performance. The weight
 model, dependence, uncertainty, and raw outputs are retained.
DEBATE
 Proposer, critic, and rebuttal roles; Argumentation/Dung semantics may resolve acceptable sets.
CROSS_VERIFY
 Independent solutions are compared; disagreement triggers adversarial review or UNKNOWN rather than
 arbitrary averaging.

Mode selection uses problem complexity, confidence/assurance requirement, risk/safety class, resource budget,
data/privacy limits, historical calibration, independence, and expected information value. Voting or weighting cannot
override proof, K0P, Constitution, rights, evidence, or authority.

12.20 100×100 INDEPENDENT CONVERGENCE METRICS

1. ExactConvergence bit-for-bit identity under declared deterministic bindings.

2. FunctionalConvergence equivalent behavior over declared tests/properties.

3. ArchitecturalConvergence equivalent topology/interfaces/structure.

4. TheoreticalConvergence same or equivalent mathematical/theoretical dependencies.

5. PerformanceConvergence comparable latency/throughput/energy/resource distributions.

6. InvariantConvergence same protected invariants and failure boundaries.

7. ParetoEquivalence same/non-dominated region despite different designs.

8. ResidualDivergence differences remaining after stable iterations.

9. PathDependence sensitivity to improvement order/history.

10. TimeToConvergence iterations, compute, and replay time to declared stability.

11. DissentPattern surviving objections/counterexamples, their independence, and reasons.

Convergence is evidence about structure and architectural attractors, not proof of truth. Broad divergence can indicate
unresolved design space, multiple valid Pareto regions, insufficient constraints, or common starting assumptions; it is
not automatically failure.

12.21 REACTIVE / PROACTIVE REGULATION FALSIFICATION PROGRAM

The current names are Reactive Persistent Machine (RPM) and Proactive Persistent Machine (PPM). The following four-arm
experiment is mandatory before attributing an observed effect to the asymmetric proactive mechanism rather than
clocks/gating/sampling.

Arm 1 — RPM
 Reactive Regulation baseline using the declared reactive update/governance path.

Arm 2 — PPM-Symmetric
 Proactive Regulation machinery with a symmetric potential/well while preserving the comparable
 implementation, observation, and clock structure.

Arm 3 — PPM-Decoupled
 PPM-Symmetric with fast/full-path clocks and activation mechanism decoupled from the candidate
 causal asymmetry, so dual-clock effects can be isolated.

Arm 4 — PPM-Decoupled-Randomized
 PPM-Decoupled with matched randomized full-path activation:

 B_t ~ Bernoulli(p)

 where p is estimated/fixed from the deterministic full-path activation rate under a predeclared
 matching protocol. Randomness, seed/source, strata, temporal matching, and confidence bounds are
 recorded.

Predeclared pass/equivalence conditions
 PPM-Symmetric ≠ RPM
 PPM-Decoupled ≈ PPM-Symmetric
 PPM-Decoupled-Randomized ≈ PPM-Decoupled

“≠” requires a predeclared effect metric, direction, uncertainty, multiplicity policy, and practical effect threshold.
“≈” requires a predeclared equivalence/non-inferiority interval; failure to reject a difference is not equivalence.

Dual-clock sampling-bias warning
 Physical/logical time t and update/activation time τ can create selection bias when deterministic
 gating chooses which states receive the full path. Gating may leak state/causal structure into the
 observed dynamics. The randomized matched-rate arm tests whether apparent improvement is caused by
 asymmetry or by selective sampling/clocking. It does not eliminate all confounding; allocation,
 measurement, censoring, and dependence remain explicit.

REFERENCE_DEFAULTS — experimental starting values, not universal constants
 β_neg = 10 × β_pos
 θ_E = 0.3
 θ_I = 0.2
 μ = 0.001
 ε_min = 0.05

Every experiment reports deviations, units/scales, sensitivity, and robustness. A default is not an established law or
deployment permission.

Identifiability correction
 κ_ctrl is exogenous and deliberately swept/controlled.
 γ_t and C_t are endogenous/emergent response or order parameters and are measured.

The reference control parameter may be represented as:
 κ_ctrl = α / (β + σ₀√β)
but the equation does not turn γ_t or C_t into inputs. Analysis reports identifiability assumptions, measurement error,
causal graph, intervention assignment, and sensitivity to hidden confounding.

12.22 HARDENED POSITIVITY-FLOOR (“JAIL”) IMPLEMENTATION PROFILE

The mathematical Proactive Regulation model may include an asymmetric positivity-preserving well. The hardened
implementation profile makes its allowed floor/configuration non-negotiable to ordinary
runtime policy:

 - the positivity floor is stored below writable application/ordinary policy state;
 - parameters are protected by the relevant compiler, trust, and qualified hardware boundary;
 - ordinary runtime, model output, operator shortcut, and emergency policy cannot bypass/rewrite it;
 - removal/change requires source/design modification, rebuild/recompilation, new DesignEpoch and
 DesignBinding, proof/conformance, independent review, authority, and governed redeployment;
 - attempted bypass produces halt/quarantine/audit according to risk.

This profile is not a forced-positive-emotion machine. It cannot suppress legitimate negative evidence, grief, dissent,
error signals, or human autonomy; manipulate humans; or override Constitution/K0P/truth. “Negative experience is not the
enemy; chronic suffering is the target” remains the emotional boundary.

12.23 AUTHORITATIVE EMERGENCY RESPONSE DISTINCTION

Functional Emergency Theory (FET) is not RPM with a lower threshold. Reactive Regulation gates; Emergency Response
overrides ordinary governance through a separately authorized trust-level emergency path.

Emergency Response may bypass ordinary Policy/AuthZ/consensus only where a pre-authorized emergency contract permits. It
never bypasses Constitution, K0P, immutable trust roots, personal sovereignty, scope, expiry, safe-state constraints, or
post-event audit. A reflex has its own trigger proof, bounded action set, deadline, reset/recovery path, and
false-positive/false-negative analysis.

12.24 REFERENCE EFFICIENCY AND PROCEDURAL-MEDIA TARGETS

Edge/robotics heartbeat profile
 5.5 kHz (approximately 181.8 μs period) remains a reference for a qualified edge-safety loop. In
 combination with validated gating, DVFS, and micro-sleep, an approximately 30% local inference
 energy/compute saving is retained as an UNVALIDATED / BENCHMARK_REQUIRED target. The frequency by
 itself does not cause the saving; workload, hardware, deadlines, accuracy, safety, and baselines must
 be reported.

Procedural semantic media
 SceneRecipe, CharacterRecipe, AnimationRecipe, PhysicsRecipe, WorldRecipe, StoryRecipe, Φ extraction,
 recipe reuse, and cache/generation paths retain an approximately 90% storage-reduction research
 target relative to a declared raw-media baseline. Status: UNVALIDATED / BENCHMARK_REQUIRED. Quality,
 editability, compute/energy, cache misses, fidelity, accessibility, provenance, and raw preservation
 obligations are measured; the target is not a universal guarantee.

12.25 GSL-R v1.0 CONTINUOUS-MANIFOLD RESEARCH PROFILE

Status: EXPERIMENTAL / NON-NORMATIVE / NOT REQUIRED FOR SEALED GARDEN OPERATION.

Research representation
 M is a six-dimensional Riemannian manifold whose coordinate families correspond to the six GSL
 Pillars: Existence, Change, Agency, Law, Value, Frame.

 Semantic state x ∈ M.
 Metric g defines local semantic distance/cost under declared units and chart.
 Reasoning path candidate geodesic flow on (M,g), subject to constraints and evidence.
 Contradiction load investigated through Ricci curvature or a typed curvature-derived tensor/field.
 Validity candidate Ric_g(v,v) ≤ λ g(v,v) for all declared admissible tangent vectors v, or another
 precisely specified tensor/eigenvalue bound.
 Improvement path candidate metric evolution through Ricci flow:
 ∂g/∂s = -2 Ric(g)
 with constraints, gauge/normalization, boundary conditions, and stopping rules.

The phrases “reasoning is geodesic” and “contradictions are Ricci curvature” are hypotheses/modeling choices, not
established physical or cognitive laws. Research must define charts, metric observables, singularities, topology change,
discrete-to-continuous mapping, falsifiable predictions, computational cost, and comparison baselines.

PDE co-processor path
 A future specialized solver/co-processor may accelerate geodesic/PDE/Ricci-flow computations. It is
 subject to typed IR, numerical error bounds, calibration where physical, proof/validation, K0P for
 physical actuation, and ordinary conformance. No such processor is required for GSL v45.1.

 propagation and diffusion;

 coherent and ballistic transport;
 open-system/dissipative transport;

empirical calibration, formal analysis, and error bounds remain required. Unsupported extensions remain
HYPOTHESIS/UNKNOWN.
