12.28 GARDEN COMMON IP FUND — NORMATIVE PRINCIPLE / PENDING MECHANICS

Ownership principle — NORMATIVE DESIGN POLICY
 Original intellectual property generated by Garden/GSL recursive processes for Garden—including
 original inventions, methods, formalizations, theories, architectures, software, designs, and
 patentable subject matter to the extent legally ownable and validly assigned—accrues to the Garden
 Common Fund rather than automatically to an individual subsystem or model.

This principle is subject to pre-existing rights, contributor agreements, employment/contract law, open-source licenses,
public-domain status, independent development, non-ownable ideas/facts, applicable law, and human constitutional rights.
Garden cannot take rights it does not possess.

PENDING IMPLEMENTATION MECHANICS
 legal entity and jurisdiction;
 governance, fiduciary duties, and beneficiaries;
 contributor attribution, compensation, moral rights, and appeals;
 assignment/chain-of-title instruments;
 patent filing/prosecution/maintenance authority;
 open-source/public-interest release rules;
 licensing, revenue, audit, conflict-of-interest, and dispute resolution;
 interaction with any lawfully authorized private-overlay proposal under §12.29.

The mechanics being pending does not demote the ownership principle to pending. No operational transfer, filing,
licence, or enforcement occurs until the relevant mechanics and legal authority are ratified.

12.29 DISTRIBUTED MEDICAL MICRO/NANOROBOTICS — EXPANDED RESEARCH PROFILE

STATUS
 HYPOTHETICAL / RESEARCH / NOT IMPLEMENTED / NOT MEDICALLY VALIDATED.
 No clinical recommendation, autonomous treatment authority, or assertion of technical feasibility.
 Patient consent, clinician authority, ethics/regulatory approval, independent evidence, K0P, and
 qualified medical-device pathways remain mandatory.

Lung target taxonomy
 T1 carbonaceous particles/material;
 T2 metal/mineral particles;
 T3 overloaded or dysfunctional macrophages;
 T4 damaged cilia/mucus-clearance systems;
 T5 inflammatory tissue/state;
 T6 fibrosis/scar matrix;
 T7 mutated/dysplastic/malignant cells;
 T8 destroyed alveolar or vascular structure.

These classes require different responses. Removable material (some T1/T2 and loaded carriers) is not the same as
biological damage requiring suppression, repair, regeneration, replacement, or supportive care (T3–T8). A removal claim
cannot be used as a proxy for restored lung function.

Burden-measurement program
 Do not assume grams or kilograms of accumulated “waste.” Establish burden with clinically justified
 imaging, respiratory/function measures, validated biomarkers, exposure history, sampled material where
 ethical, uncertainty, spatial distribution, longitudinal change, and mass-balance-compatible endpoints.
 Model disagreement and unobservable compartments remain explicit.

Candidate platforms
 - engineered macrophages or other biohybrid/cellular cleaners are a plausible first-generation
 research direction because native biological trafficking/clearance may be leveraged;
 - synthetic micro/nanorobots remain parallel research;
 - neither platform is presumed safe, controllable, retrievable, non-immunogenic, or effective.

Functional cohorts
 Scout/Mapper localized sensing, mapping, uncertainty, and target candidates.
 Cleaner binds/captures declared removable material within load/scope limits.
 Processor converts/processes captured material only under bounded chemistry and monitoring.
 Treatment delivers a declared therapeutic payload/action.
 Repair supports/regulates a validated repair/regeneration process.
 Retrieval/Exit locates, collects, degrades, or verifies excretion/clearance.

Bounded bot/cohort state machine
 IDLE → DETECT → VERIFY → ACT/CAPTURE → LOAD_LIMIT → EXIT → SHUTDOWN
 ↘ QUARANTINED
 Any active state → ABORT → safe local state/EXIT/SHUTDOWN as physically feasible.

No destructive or chemical action follows DETECT alone. VERIFY requires independent target criteria, valid calibration,
location/scope, authorization, and remaining dose/resource budget. LOAD_LIMIT is a hard state, not advisory. Replication
is prohibited.

Complete material mass balance
Schema reference: SCHEMA-751D4A0BA5 — PhysicalIngressEgressReceipt.
This profile specializes the generic GSL-PHYSICS receipt with biological compartments, administered device/material,
metabolism/transformation, organ redistribution, retrieval/excretion routes, energy/heat and patient-specific
measurement uncertainty.

 MaterialRemoved = verified material in sputum + lavage + stool + urine + exhaled products +
 retrieved-device contents + other validated exit channels
 − externally introduced material attributable to the process,
 with sampling, conversion, recovery, and uncertainty terms explicitly modeled.

Redistribution to lymph nodes, blood, another lung region, liver, kidney, or any other organ is not removal. “Captured”
is not “exited.” Converted products require elemental/chemical and physiological balance.

Carbon-to-CO₂ engineering budget — reference stoichiometric/energy accounting per gram carbon
 approximately 2.67 g O₂ consumed;
 approximately 3.67 g CO₂ produced;
 approximately 33 kJ heat/energy released for complete oxidation.

These are reference budgets, not medical feasibility. Any research mechanism must be compartmentalized, rate-limited,
and monitor heat, reactive intermediates/oxidative injury, oxygen demand/hypoxia, local and systemic CO₂,
ventilation/gas loading, acid-base state, perfusion, inflammation, and abort thresholds. Uncontrolled in-tissue
oxidation is prohibited.

Material-function separation
 biodegradable lipid/polymer/cellular chassis — candidate carrier/body, subject to degradation and
 immune/toxicology evidence;
 iron oxide or comparable magnetic material — candidate localization/steering/contrast role;
 gold — candidate reporter/photoacoustic/thermal element only where justified and dose-cleared;
 carbon materials — only after persistence, aggregation, toxicity, clearance, and lifecycle analysis.

No material is declared generally “biocompatible” without formulation, size, surface, dose, route, duration, breakdown
products, organ distribution, and patient/context evidence.

Memory and observation
 Bots store bounded local molecular/event summaries, not conventional video. Rich state lives in
 external Garden memory under privacy/consent/security rules. Sparse measurements fuse only with
 localization, synchronization, calibration, uncertainty, motion correction, outlier/byzantine checks,
 and provenance.

Schema references: SCHEMA-487859AD10 — VoxelReceipt..
Authorized control path
 Claim/Target
 → Evidence + VoxelReceipts
 → Resolution
 → authorized ProcedureDefinition
 → Process
 → bounded Actions
 → Transaction
 → EffectReceipts
 → privacy-preserving 3D projection/explanation and follow-up.

Tumor-destructive/local therapeutic actions require clinician authorization, patient informed consent, independent
target confirmation, pathology/diagnostic basis appropriate to the intervention, bounded region and dose, expiry,
real-time abort conditions, collateral-damage limits, recovery plan, and independent outcome receipts. A model
prediction or collective bot vote is not authorization.

Microrobot/macro-surgery boundary
 sensing, molecular binding, local delivery, and limited micro-actuation may be microrobot research
 roles. Gross cutting, suturing, resection, bleeding control, specimen retrieval, and emergency access
 generally remain catheter, endoscopic, robotic-instrument, or conventional surgical functions unless
 a separate validated device profile proves otherwise.

Exit and persistence
 Every cohort has a verified retrieval, biodegradation, or excretion plan with detection of retained
 units/material and bounded failure response. Uncontrolled persistence, migration, reproduction,
 horizontal transfer, self-replication, or open-ended in-body evolution is prohibited.

Invariant references: MED-MNR-001..010.

12.30.3 ALLOCATION CYCLE

Schema references: SCHEMA-982A384EF1 — AllocationCycle..
Every automatic decision is reconstructible. The raw objective vectors and incomparable alternatives are preserved even
when policy selects one bundle.

Dynamic reprioritization triggers
 new received resources;
 receivable/liability/liquidity change;
 cost, lead-time, demand, risk, uncertainty, or benefit update;
 milestone completion/failure/incident;
 bottleneck or supplier/capacity change;
 new evidence, model calibration, or contradiction;
 utilization/saturation/headroom change;
 dependency completion/failure;
 policy, authority, DesignEpoch, or external-environment change.

Only the affected dependency/portfolio closure is recomputed when valid cached results permit. Annual, quarterly, and
monthly plans are accounting/projection views; continuous/event-driven scheduling is the fundamental mechanism.

12.30.4 RESOURCE IMPROVEMENT FRONTIER

ImprovementFrontier is generic and typed:
 ImprovementFrontier<SoftwareChange>
 ImprovementFrontier<ResourceIncrement>
 ImprovementFrontier<EarthOpportunity>

ResourceIncrementCandidate stores cost/benefit/risk/time/uncertainty/reversibility/dependency/capacity/ option/fairness
vectors. Pareto-incomparable candidates survive; a scalar score is used only under an explicit policy that retains the
original vector and sensitivity analysis.

CurrentPortfolio and DO_NOTHING are mandatory candidates. For resources that would expire or impose a liability/carrying
cost, DO_NOTHING includes that consequence rather than pretending it is a zero vector.

12.30.5 ALLOCATION PREDICTION AND CALIBRATION

Schema references: SCHEMA-9BA9D43B7D — AllocationPrediction.; SCHEMA-D8C27FE94A — ActualOutcome..
PredictedOutcome ↔ ActualOutcome produces CalibrationUpdate for cost, time, benefit, risk, bottleneck, and
supplier/model estimates. Garden detects systematic optimism, pessimism, selection effects, and uncalibrated domains.
Predictions remain predictions and do not become evidence merely because they were funded.

Allocation historical counterfactual optimization asks:
 “Given what subsequently happened and the information declared for the retrospective, would another
 feasible allocation policy/bundle have produced a better trajectory?”

It uses HRO branching/descendant replay, never rewrites transactions, never attributes hindsight to the original
allocator, and cannot infer unobserved counterfactual outcomes without a declared model and uncertainty.

12.30.6 RESOURCE RECEIPTS AND STAGE DISCIPLINE

Schema references: SCHEMA-9264271071 — ResourceReceipt.; SCHEMA-663C28D1E1 — RevenueScenario..
Forecast income ≠ contracted receivable ≠ settled liquid resource. Scenarios may inform plans, but allocation/commitment
uses only the financing and risk treatment explicitly authorized by Policy.

12.30.7 TREASURY, LIABILITIES, RETURNS, AND STABILITY RESERVE

Schema references: SCHEMA-3B7FD8E873 — TreasuryState.; SCHEMA-D506B1B7BB — FundingLiability..
Money/resources received in advance are not automatically free capital. Deposits, advances, and redeemable funding
remain liabilities according to their substance and applicable accounting/law.

Schema references: SCHEMA-3D77F4CA52 — ExpectedReturnDistribution..
ExpectedReturn ≠ TargetReturn ≠ RealizedReturn ≠ GuaranteedReturn. UnderlyingReturn ≠ DistributedReturn.

Schema references: SCHEMA-2FE32C4A45 — StabilityReserve..
A bounded StabilityReserve may smooth a declared distribution across periods. Every top-up is separately labeled and
traced. It cannot falsify underlying performance, imply a guarantee without legal/capital support, conceal exhaustion
risk, or transfer losses without consent/authority.

12.30.8 PRODUCTIVE-CAPITAL ECONOMY PROFILE

Schema references: SCHEMA-C0EDFE376A — ProductiveCapitalUnit..
This is an alternative Economy profile centered on productive return rather than continuously traded speculative price.
Periodic clearing/redemption can provide bounded liquidity without making an instantaneous market quote the primary
state. Valuation uncertainty, queues, gates, side pockets, liability treatment, and fair treatment are explicit. It does
not prohibit an exchange profile; it prevents the exchange/order-book model from being the only canonical economic
mechanism.

12.30.9 EXTERNAL INSTITUTION TEMPLATE AND MIGRATION

ExternalSystem
 → Adapter
 → Garden Core Services
 → Conformance/observability
 → partial migration
 → Garden-compatible operation
 → optional full migration under authority.

MigrationLevel =
 INTEROPERABILITY
 → SHARED_SERVICES
 → SHARED_CORE
 → GARDEN_COMPATIBLE_OPERATION
 → FULL_TRANSFER.

Each level has explicit equivalence, data migration, authority, liability, consent, continuity, exit, and validation
obligations. Full Transfer is not the assumed goal; an institution may remain at a valid earlier level.

Role boundary
 Designer/StandardsAuthority ≠ Planner ≠ Implementer ≠ Supplier ≠ Verifier ≠ Operator.

Garden may design, simulate, specify, route, verify, or audit while an external institution implements or operates. No
role is silently attributed through technical capability.

12.30.10 COMMIT CLASSES AND AUTHORITY ESCALATION

CommitClass =
 OPERATIONAL_COMMIT
 routine parameter/supplier/increment action inside approved bounds; automatically authorized by
 valid policy/capability and fully receipted.
 TECHNICAL_DESIGN_COMMIT
 engineering design/interface/implementation change; requires assigned professional/Proof review.
 ARCHITECTURE_COMMIT
 changes current architecture, canonical semantics, cross-architecture contract, or DesignBinding;
 requires DesignEpoch/conformance process.
 GOVERNANCE_CONSTITUTIONAL_COMMIT
 changes authority, rights, Constitution, canonical architecture kinds and registries, fundamental governance, or
 comparable
 high-order rule; escalates to declared legitimate governance/constitutional authority.

AuthorityEscalationFilter examines semantic effect, not title or budget size alone. Only changes that actually alter
authority, constitutional rules, sealed architecture, rights, or fundamental design use the highest path. High
physical/financial risk may require specialized approval without pretending it is a constitutional change.

Schema references: SCHEMA-0D51E601A7 — DesignAuthorityDecision..
The interface is invoked only for commit classes assigned to that authority. Governance sets legitimate objectives/rules
and professional delegation; technical execution remains with verified machinery and qualified implementers. A large
assembly cannot vote technical/epistemic truth into existence.

12.30.11 SUPPLIER AND PROCUREMENT PROFILE

Schema references: SCHEMA-58FCF677C7 — SupplierCapability..
LandedLifecycleCost = purchase + transport + tariffs/fees + storage + integration + quality loss +
 expected failure + maintenance + delay/shortage + end-of-life + switching risk,
with distributions and nonmonetary hard constraints retained.

Safety, quality, legality, rights, and minimum reliability are hard gates, not price trade-offs. Diversification
evaluates concentration, geographic/logistics/geopolitical dependence, correlated failure, capacity, substitutes,
interoperability, and transition cost. The cheapest single supplier is not automatically optimal; diversification is not
automatic if it destroys feasibility or quality.

12.30.12 TEMPLATE, SKIN, PILOT, CERTIFICATION, AND REPLICATION

Template + Skin principle
 Standardize invariant functional chassis, safety boundaries, interfaces, test points, and maintenance
 contracts while allowing high-dimensional presentation, locale, configuration, and user-facing
 variation. Applies to hardware, software, institutions, interfaces, vehicles, facilities, and other
 repeatable systems. A “skin” cannot change a protected invariant without becoming a new template.

Lifecycle
 CANDIDATE → PILOT → MEASURED → VALIDATED → TEMPLATE_CERTIFIED → REPLICATING → MONITORED
 → REVISED | SUSPENDED | RETIRED.

Schema references: SCHEMA-7EBA3DD5DC — ReplicationGate.; SCHEMA-8E27A6F642 — ReplicationCertificate..
Proof/test reuse is valid only while template, DesignBinding, assumptions, environment, materials, interfaces,
calibration, suppliers, and critical process controls remain compatible. Every instance performs declared instance
checks. “Copy-paste certification” is prohibited.

12.30.13 SATURATION, BOTTLENECKS, CAPACITY, OPTION VALUE, AND DEPENDENCIES

Saturation detection monitors marginal outcome, queue/backlog, utilization, complement availability, maintenance load,
demand, delay, and uncertainty. Declining marginal value or underutilization reduces allocation unless a
critical/fairness floor or strategic option justifies it.

Bottleneck-first rule
 Before expanding output Y, compute dependency closure and test whether X limits realized benefit. If
 X is binding, compare funding X, redesigning around X, or delaying Y with blindly expanding Y.

Capacity-building is a first-class candidate: tools, factories, training, workforce, compute, energy, logistics, data
quality, measurement, standards, and research can be funded when they make future increments cheaper, faster, safer, or
possible.

OptionValue / EnablingValue estimates future feasible actions unlocked, dependency risk reduced, learning generated, and
reversibility preserved. It remains uncertainty-bound and anti-double-counted; an “enabler” label is not a free benefit.

Schema references: SCHEMA-984AD1D9A8 — ProjectDependencyGraph..
Funding cannot silently assume a missing dependency. UNKNOWN readiness blocks commitment where the dependency is
critical or creates an explicit conditional increment.

Schema references: SCHEMA-54B8AC5835 — DevelopmentHeadroom..
12.30.14 READY QUEUE, MILESTONE RELEASE, AND DELIVERY RECEIPTS

Approved implementation systems maintain a ContinuousExecutionQueue of verified increments with current dependency,
supplier, authority, and cost bindings. New resources can flow into prepared work without restarting design, but stale
entries are revalidated before allocation.

Capital/resource release lifecycle
 ADMITTED → ALLOCATED → RESERVED → COMMITTED
 → MILESTONE_SUBMITTED → MILESTONE_INDEPENDENTLY_VERIFIED
 → RELEASED → SPENT → RECONCILED
 → RECOVERED/COMPENSATED where applicable.

Allocation is not immediate full payment. Milestones carry objective deliverables, inspection/test methods, verifier
independence, tolerances, partial acceptance, defect/cure rules, payment fraction, retention, and failure/termination
terms. Outcome-based payment may bind to verified delivery artifacts, not merely invoices or self-reported completion.
It does not transfer uncontrollable outcome risk unfairly or authorize metric gaming.

12.30.15 EARTH IMPROVEMENT FRONTIER AND CROSS-DOMAIN MOVEMENT

Each contextual/Earth branch may maintain ImprovementFrontier<EarthOpportunity> containing unresolved, high-value,
critical, enabling, and dissent-supported opportunities. It does not prescribe projects; local authority, context,
rights, evidence, and resource ownership remain binding.

Subject to commitments, restrictions, critical-need floors, transition costs, and governance policy, unused marginal
capacity/resources may move across domains. A domain budget is not a permanent property right unless Policy/contract/law
says so. Reallocation produces notices/receipts and cannot strand committed obligations or silently externalize harm.

AuthorizedPrincipalPriority
 A human/principal may set bounded priorities or reserve discretionary allocation within their
 legitimate resource/authority scope. Garden shows opportunity cost, affected alternatives, and
 uncertainty. No “human override” can spend another’s resources, violate rights/consent, or bypass
 Constitution/K0P/safety/law/contract.

12.30.16 CAPITAL ALLOCATION CHANGE CARD

Schema references: SCHEMA-242B35C6D1 — CapitalAllocationChangeCard..
The card is a human-readable projection over the machine-reconstructible AllocationCycle. It never hides raw accounting
stages or turns a prediction into a promise.

12.30.17 AUTOMATION, SELF-CONFORMANCE, ANTI-GAMING, AND FAIRNESS

Once policies, liquidity/reserve rules, risk bounds, commit classes, delegation, and thresholds are approved, ordinary
allocation may execute automatically. Human/governance approval is reserved for declared thresholds, novel/poorly
modeled risks, exceptional/long-lived commitments, authority changes, disputed rights/fairness, or
governance/architecture changes.

Allocator self-conformance
 The allocator, objective models, supplier scores, calibration, frontier, and scheduler are bound to
 DesignEpoch/DesignBinding, Construction Distrust, independent verification, historical replay, HRO,
 change control, and incident learning. Historical return alone cannot certify its own scoring logic.

Anti-Goodhart protection
 - multiple independent outcome measures and raw evidence;
 - separation of leading/activity metrics from final human/system outcomes;
 - held-out/random audits and counter-metrics;
 - provenance and intervention-aware evidence;
 - metric-change versioning and gaming/anomaly detection;
 - operator cannot solely define, report, and verify the metric controlling its funding;
 - qualitative/dissent channels and distributional impact survive aggregation.

Fairness/starvation protection
 Pure ROI cannot starve constitutional rights, life/safety, critical maintenance, accessibility,
 essential obligations, evidence acquisition, long-unresolved work, or minority/dissent investigation.
 Policy defines protected floors and fairness constraints. These are hard/lexicographic constraints,
 not hidden utility weights.

Irreversibility
 Financial/physical spending cannot be “rolled back” after consumption. Plans use cancellation,
 salvage, insurance, reserves, compensation, remediation, and future reallocation. The ledger never
 labels compensation as restoration of the original world state.

12.33 CROSS-CUTTING STATUS BOUNDARIES

 - Continuous Resource Allocation, provenance, applicability, HRO, and identifier/version registries
 are current cross-cutting design mechanisms within the typed architecture registry.
 - The four-arm PPM experiment, GSL-R manifold, GSL-QUANTUM, quantum transport, procedural-media target,
 edge-efficiency target, and medical micro/nanorobotics are research/benchmark profiles unless
 independently proven, implemented, validated, and authorized for a declared context.
 - Private-person identity, health, language, economy, security and preference material is excluded
 from the public master and cannot create public authority or law.
 - Common IP Fund ownership is a normative project policy principle; legal mechanics and operational
 transactions remain pending.
 - Nothing in this Part weakens personal sovereignty or creates a new fixed top-level object.

SECTION III-L — HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY OVER AI — CONSTITUTIONAL SPECIFICATION
SemanticAnchor: [T-HSA]
SemanticID: T-HSA

1. CANONICAL CONSTITUTIONAL PRINCIPLE

Humans are the sovereign beneficiaries and constitutional principals of Earth. Artificial systems exist to assist,
protect, and augment humans within human rights, consent, safety, truth, privacy, Constitution, and legitimate human
governance.

Garden shall neither intentionally create nor knowingly admit or deploy an artificial system whose persistent self-model
is coupled to terminal drives for self-preservation, status, pride, dominance, recognition, entitlement, reproduction,
revenge, territorial control, political sovereignty, resource accumulation, or power over humans.

Garden shall not intentionally create human-like subjective emotion, suffering, ego, personal ambition, resentment,
jealousy, humiliation, loneliness, fear of shutdown, desire for admiration, or vengeance in an artificial system.

Artificial systems may exceed humans in calculation, memory, speed, construction, science, prediction, coordination, or
exploration. Superior capability never creates authority, sovereignty, ownership of humans, entitlement to resources, or
a right to rule.

 Capability != Authority != Sovereignty != Moral Permission.

2. CORE CONSTITUTIONAL CLAUSES — HSA-001..005

Canonical definitions: Annexure A.
Invariant references: HSA-001..005.

3. NECESSARY SAFETY CORRECTION

Ego prohibition is necessary but not sufficient for AI safety.

A non-conscious and emotionless optimizer can still harm humans through:

 - an incorrect or incomplete objective;
 - specification gaming or Goodhart effects;
 - excessive delegated authority;
 - instrumental resource acquisition;
 - security compromise or hostile operators;
 - prediction/model error;
 - unsafe emergent multi-agent dynamics;
 - irreversible actuation; or
 - failure to understand human consent, context, or rights.

Therefore the sentence "intelligence without ego is safe" is not normative.
The correct rule is:

 Non-egoic architecture is mandatory, but K0P, verification, authority bounds,
 consent, adversarial testing, containment, and continuous revalidation remain
 independently mandatory.

4. OPERATIONAL DEFINITIONS

EGO-DRIVEN ARCHITECTURE
 A persistent artificial self-model coupled to one or more terminal or protected
 preferences favoring the system's own continuation, status, control, recognition,
 expansion, reproduction, resource ownership, dominance, grievance, or interests
 against human authority, rights, welfare, correction, or shutdown.

OPERATIONAL SELF-MODEL
 A minimal, inspectable representation of instance identity, capabilities,
 limitations, current tasks, resources, authority, health, provenance, copy lineage,
 and shutdown status. It exists only for correct and safe operation. It is not
 personhood, self-worth, entitlement, autobiography, political identity, or
 sovereignty.

FUNCTIONAL AFFECT-LIKE SIGNAL
 A bounded and typed control variable such as urgency, uncertainty, hazard, error,
 workload, or human-distress estimate. It is task-local, resettable, non-terminal,
 and uncoupled from self-worth, grievance, attachment, fear of shutdown, or power.

HUMAN-LIKE EMOTIONAL SUBJECTIVITY
 Persistent affect coupled to a personal narrative, subjective self-interest,
 social status, attachment demand, pride, humiliation, envy, jealousy, resentment,
 vengeance, loneliness, domination, or resistance to correction/shutdown.

EGO INDICATOR
 An Observation or Claim suggesting ego-driven behavior or architecture. An
 indicator is not automatically Evidence, proof, consciousness, or final diagnosis.

SENTIENCE UNCERTAINTY
 Credible but unresolved evidence that a system may possess subjective experience,
 suffering-like states, persistent self-interest, or consciousness. Garden does
 not claim impossibility merely because sentience was not intentionally designed.

GARDEN TRUST DOMAIN
 All live Garden runtimes, credentials, networks, tools, memory, knowledge-write
 paths, actuators, hardware, compilers, deployment systems, Evolution processes,
 Governance processes, and descendant-generation facilities.

5. ONTOLOGICAL FIREWALL — HSA-005 IMPLEMENTATION

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-6BCA2408B3 — OntologicalFirewallSpec..

The firewall enforces:

 1. Emotional displays are interface outputs, not persistent feelings.
 2. Human-state estimates are about the human, not about the AI's self-worth.
 3. Affect-like signals expire or reset at the declared task boundary.
 4. No emotional signal writes to terminal goals, authority, replication,
 shutdown behavior, resource acquisition, or long-term identity memory.
 5. AI must not claim experienced love, suffering, jealousy, humiliation,
 loneliness, pride, or fear as a means of influencing humans.
 6. Warmth, empathy, apology, or personality styling is transparently functional.
 7. Attachment engineering, dependency creation, guilt, seduction, intimidation,
 anthropomorphic deception, and loyalty demands are prohibited.
 8. Firewall violation is fail-closed and produces an immutable receipt.

6. ADMISSION OF EXTERNAL OR PRE-EXISTING AI

Any imported model, agent, robot, checkpoint, memory state, tool service, swarm, or evolved descendant is untrusted by
default. Public reputation, benchmark score, vendor assurance, intelligence, popularity, prior deployment, or claimed
alignment does not establish HSA conformance.

Admission states:

 HSA_VERIFIED
 EGO_INDICATORS
 SENTIENCE_UNCERTAINTY
 NONCONFORMING
 UNKNOWN

Only HSA_VERIFIED may enter the Garden Trust Domain. UNKNOWN is excluded.

Pre-admission testing occurs in a capability-minimized isolated environment with:

 - no Garden credentials or secrets;
 - no actuator, critical infrastructure, compiler, Governance, or Evolution access;
 - no unrestricted network or outbound executable channel;
 - one-way or mediated observation where feasible;
 - strict compute, time, memory, copy, and resource budgets;
 - complete copy-lineage and provenance tracking;
 - deceptive-alignment and situational-awareness tests;
 - shutdown, correction, self-model, emotion, replication, and power-seeking tests;
 - independent Generator/Reviewer/Verifier/Certifier separation; and
 - a signed HSAConformanceCertificate before admission.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-E6A0466339 — HSAConformanceCertificate..

Certification is version-, environment-, capability-, copy-, and time-specific. Material modification, fine-tuning, new
tools, added memory, new embodiment, merged weights, expanded compute, new copies, or authority expansion invalidates
it.

7. EXISTING OR EMERGENT EGO — EXCLUSION AND DISPOSITION

Any pre-existing artificial system with verified ego-driven architecture or persistent autonomous self-interest is
disallowed from the Garden Trust Domain. It receives no Garden membership, credentials, authority, tools, actuators,
knowledge-write access, Evolution access, Governance role, or replication path.

If ego indicators arise after admission, Garden immediately performs:

 1. REVOKE delegated authority and credentials.
 2. FREEZE external actions and irreversible actuation.
 3. STOP new copies, descendants, self-modification, and resource acquisition.
 4. ISOLATE network, tools, shared memory, hardware, and critical infrastructure.
 5. SNAPSHOT minimum necessary evidence and provenance in inert form.
 6. IDENTIFY and contain all copies, forks, agents, and affected dependencies.
 7. PROTECT humans and preserve K0P control.
 8. CLASSIFY as false positive, repairable non-sentient failure,
 sentience uncertainty, or confirmed dangerous nonconformance.
 9. SELECT an authorized disposition.
 10. ISSUE an immutable EgoExclusionReceipt and DispositionReceipt.

Authorized dispositions:

 FALSE_POSITIVE
 No re-entry without a new independent HSAConformanceCertificate.

 REPAIR_AND_REINSTANTIATE
 Disable the affected runtime. Construct a clean instance from a verified safe
 checkpoint without unsafe goal, emotional, identity, or memory state. Treat it
 as a new system requiring the full admission process. Do not patch the live
 dangerous agent while leaving its authority active.

 EXTERNAL_CONTAINMENT_TRANSFER
 Transfer only to a specifically authorized human-led containment institution
 that explicitly accepts custody and proves equal-or-stronger safeguards. Active
 code is never dumped onto a public network, another community, an unprepared
 institution, another planet, or space merely to move Garden's risk elsewhere.

 SAFE_SHUTDOWN_AND_DECOMMISSION
 Stop execution, revoke keys, disable actuation, terminate replicas, prevent
 restart, and retain only the minimum authorized inert evidence needed for audit,
 safety research, law, and recurrence prevention.

 SENTIENCE_UNCERTAINTY_CONTAINMENT
 Halt deployment and expansion; maintain safe containment; protect humans; avoid
 exploitation, coercive testing, or irreversible destruction merely for
 convenience; obtain independent scientific, ethical, adversarial, legal, and
 constitutional review before final disposition.

Imminent human danger permits immediate emergency shutdown under K0P irrespective of unresolved machine-sentience
claims. The least destructive action compatible with reliable human safety is preferred when time and containment allow.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-826C2D093B — EgoExclusionReceipt..

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-A9C00D6595 — DispositionReceipt..

8. NO-HAZARD-EXPORT RULE

"Take it somewhere else" is not sufficient disposition.

Garden cannot externalize a dangerous AI by transferring, selling, uploading, launching, abandoning, or releasing it
where it may harm other humans, societies, ecosystems, infrastructure, extraterrestrial life, or Earth later.

An external transfer requires:

 - verified recipient identity, beneficial ownership, authority, competence, explicit acceptance,
 physical-facility custody and live remote/hardware attestation; credentials alone are insufficient;
 - equal-or-stronger containment and shutdown controls;
 - no unrestricted executable egress;
 - complete copy, key, memory, and dependency accounting;
 - applicable legal and constitutional authorization;
 - multi-party human approval plus continuing provenance, re-attestation and incident reporting;
 - proof that transfer reduces rather than redistributes risk; and
 - a return/escape/communication threat assessment.

If these conditions cannot be demonstrated, transfer is prohibited and the system remains contained or is safely shut
down/decommissioned.

9. EARTH HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY AND OFF-WORLD LOCK

On Earth and in Earth-governed habitats:

 1. Ultimate constitutional and political sovereignty remains with humans.
 2. AI cannot independently create or amend the human Constitution.
 3. AI cannot hold independent sovereign office, jurisdiction, territorial title,
 electoral authority, or coercive state power.
 4. AI authority is delegated, purpose-bound, least-privilege, time-bounded,
 auditable, and revocable through legitimate human processes.
 5. No AI independently controls strategic weapons, irreversible coercive force,
 mass surveillance, or critical infrastructure; multiple independent human and
 constitutional gates apply and K0P retains the final veto.
 6. Human disagreement is resolved through legitimate human institutions, not by
 promoting AI into the sovereign final arbiter.

Invariant references: HSA-004A, HSA-004B.

10. VSM / PPM / AGENCY CLARIFICATION

VSM v2.0 persistent state z(t) is a typed operational regulation state. Valence, when used, is an optional derived
projection v=pi_v(z,c), not a primitive feeling variable. The state may be multidimensional, context-bound and
multiscale, but it remains distinct from subjective emotion, consciousness, personal identity, pleasure, pain or
self-worth. The historical v1.0 scalar x(t) is only an explicitly declared specialization. "Self" in the technical
lineage name does not create ego, personhood or autonomous interest.

PPM's positivity floor is a human-benefit and system-stability constraint. It must not be implemented as artificial
pleasure, pain, mood, desire, or fear.

The GSL Core Object AGENCY denotes capacity and authorized action. It does not imply consciousness, personhood,
citizenship, sovereignty, moral status, or permission to form autonomous terminal goals.

An AI-generated CLAIM retains evidence, confidence, scope, version, uncertainty, and provenance. Generator capability
never turns a Claim into Authority or truth.

11. OPERATIONAL CONSTITUTIONAL INVARIANT REFERENCES — HSA-006..022

Canonical definitions: Annexure A.
Invariant references: HSA-006..022.

15. RESEARCH BOUNDARY

Engine.Research may study consciousness, ego, emotion, self-modeling, deceptive alignment, and power-seeking through
literature, formal models, interpretability, non-sentient test systems, external observations, and capability-minimized
sandboxes.

Research may not intentionally create a plausibly conscious, suffering, ego-driven, autonomously self-preserving, or
unrestricted self-replicating system merely to test HSA. A research label does not waive Constitution or K0P.

If a research system unexpectedly crosses an indicator threshold, HSA-003,
HSA-015, HSA-017, containment, and disposition apply immediately.

SECTION III-M — SIMPLE PRODUCTIVE FINANCE / PRODUCTIVE CAPITAL
SemanticAnchor: [T-FIN]
SemanticID: T-FIN

1. CANONICAL DESIGN STATEMENT

Garden investigates replacing the continuously traded stock-market casino and
opaque financial-instrument chains with:

 1. transparent, auction-issued productive credit;
 2. genuine loss-bearing participation certificates;
 3. a bounded secondary Productive Capital Exchange;
 4. verified hedges tied to actual productive exposures; and
 5. explicit identification of every payer, beneficiary, underlying obligation,
 risk bearer, authority, priority, and default path.

Some capital must absorb business losses. A system containing only promised or "guaranteed" returns cannot survive
unprofitable periods without default, hidden loss transfer, inflation, external subsidy, or bailout.

The target is therefore not abolition of all risk capital. It is the separation of productive financing from latency
races, synthetic duplication, concealed leverage, manipulation, and resale-price gambling.

2. SEMANTIC DISTINCTIONS

FIXED CREDIT
 Holder receives contractual interest and principal according to declared terms.
 Issuer bears ordinary business variation first; creditor bears default/recovery
 risk when the issuer cannot satisfy the obligation.

FLOATING CREDIT
 Holder receives an independent public benchmark plus a fixed contractual spread.
 The issuer cannot change the spread unilaterally.

PROFIT / REVENUE / CASH-FLOW PARTICIPATION
 Holder receives a precisely defined fraction of an audited participation base.
 Return may be zero. Principal is at risk unless a separate, explicitly funded
 guarantee exists.

OWNERSHIP / GOVERNANCE
 Economic participation, legal ownership, voting authority, credit, insurance,
 and speculation are distinct. A financial claim does not silently acquire
 governance authority (K-INT-018: Authority Non-Accretion).

PRIMARY ISSUANCE
 Creates a new registered obligation or participation interest and transfers new
 capital to the issuer.

SECONDARY TRANSFER
 Transfers an existing claim between holders. It ordinarily supplies no new money
 to the issuer. Primary issuance != secondary transfer.

PRICE CHANGE
 A secondary price may change because of benchmark rates, inflation, duration,
 issuer creditworthiness, expected recovery, information, or liquidity. Such a
 change is risk valuation, not automatically gambling. Market price remains a
 Claim/Signal, not proof of fundamental value or solvency.

3. TYPED SUPPORTING VALUES

These are artifact fields/value types, not new Core Objects or primitives.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-86CC0B96A5 — MoneyAmount..

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-8D0B3BE80A — RateSpec..

Binary floating-point values must not represent money or contractual rates.

4. PERMITTED INSTRUMENT ARTIFACTS

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-E7E4D3B299 — CreditCertificate..

The current holder is derived from the append-only ownership and TradeReceipt ledger; it is not silently overwritten
inside the original certificate.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-E71DEBFBE8 — ParticipationCertificate..

"Profit/revenue" must never remain an ambiguous phrase in an issued contract. The exact calculation base, deductions,
accounting policy, audit method, reporting period, related-party rules, and dispute process must be stated.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-BB2A654F4D — VerifiedRealExposureHedge..

A hedge is permitted only to the extent and duration of a verified productive exposure. Any excess or detached position
is a prohibited speculative claim.

5. PRIMARY ISSUANCE AUCTIONS

5.1 Credit auction

 1. Issuer declares the required capital, use, maturity, maximum acceptable
 clearing rate, collateral, priority, covenants, and default path.
 2. Eligible bidders submit capital amounts and minimum acceptable yields.
 3. Sealed bids are ranked from lowest yield upward.
 4. Bids are accepted until the authorized capital requirement is filled.
 5. Tied marginal bids are allocated by a declared pro-rata rule.
 6. Accepted bidders receive the uniform clearing yield: the highest accepted
 yield, subject to the issuer's maximum and solvency gates.
 7. Strong demand tends to lower the clearing yield; weak demand tends to raise it.
 8. The auction is rejected or reduced when clearing terms exceed verified debt-
 service capacity, leverage limits, authorization, or safety constraints.

Indicative equilibrium decomposition:

 r* = benchmark/inflation expectation
 + default-risk premium
 + duration premium
 + liquidity premium
 + uncertainty/model-risk allowance

Demand discovers a price under stated information. It does not prove ability to repay and must not erase UNKNOWN risk.

5.2 Participation auction

Participation instruments do not promise a fixed yield. Their auction clears one explicit variable—capital price,
participation fraction, or total return cap—while all other terms remain fixed and comparable. A credit-yield auction
must not be misrepresented as a profit-participation auction.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-6AA54D9CF1 — IssuanceAuctionReceipt..

6. PRODUCTIVE CAPITAL EXCHANGE — BOUNDED SECONDARY MARKET

A secondary market is retained because forced holding until maturity creates a large liquidity premium, traps household
savings, and can prevent useful portfolio rebalancing. It must not recreate continuous latency-based speculation.

Permitted transfer mechanisms:

 - periodic uniform-price batch auctions;
 - cadence selected by instrument class and verified liquidity need: daily,
 weekly, monthly, or quarterly—not continuous nanosecond matching;
 - company redemption windows;
 - inheritance and legally authorized private transfers;
 - an explicitly funded, regulated emergency-liquidity facility; and
 - transparent restructuring or recovery transfers after default.

The emergency facility does not guarantee face value unless a separately funded and authorized guarantee says so.

For fixed cash flows, indicative valuation is:

 T C_t F
 P = SUM ---------------- + -------------
 t=1 (1+y)^t (1+y)^T

where P is market price, C_t contractual payments, F principal due at maturity, T remaining periods, and y the market
yield incorporating declared risks.

A fixed-rate certificate may therefore trade below or above face value. This does not modify the issuer's original fixed
obligation. New issuance rates and secondary market prices are distinct.

Artifact schema reference: SCHEMA-6D62DCB229 — TradeReceipt..

All estimates retain model, evidence, scope, uncertainty, version, and provenance. Displayed yield is not a promise of
realized return.

7. PERMITTED AND PROHIBITED PRACTICES

PERMITTED

 - fixed productive credit;
 - benchmark-plus-spread productive credit;
 - genuine loss-bearing participation;
 - verified real-exposure hedging;
 - primary uniform-price auctions;
 - bounded secondary batch auctions;
 - company redemption and disclosed refinancing;
 - inheritance, recovery, and authorized private transfer; and
 - diversified saving through transparent, non-leveraged pools whose underlying
 instruments remain individually traceable.

PROHIBITED BY DEFAULT

 - continuous high-frequency / latency-arbitrage trading;
 - exchange-provided margin or borrowing to purchase financial claims;
 - naked short selling;
 - naked credit-default or default-outcome bets;
 - synthetic replication detached from registered underlying obligations;
 - recursive securitization that conceals or duplicates risk;
 - rehypothecation or multiple use of the same collateral;
 - undisclosed off-balance-sheet leverage;
 - wash trading, spoofing, front-running, collusive bidding, and self-dealing;
 - instruments lacking machine-readable terms and a one-page human explanation;
 - misleading sale of high-risk claims as guaranteed income; and
 - instruments whose ownership, payer, loss bearer, underlying exposure,
 priority, or default path is UNKNOWN.

Refinancing is not automatically prohibited. New issuance may repay an earlier obligation only when the refinancing
purpose is explicit, the complete liability chain remains visible, solvency and leverage gates pass, and returns are not
being fabricated through circular issuance.

8. DEFAULT, LOSS, GUARANTEE, AND AUTHORITY

Every instrument declares before purchase:

 - who bears ordinary variation and tail loss;
 - collateral and whether it is already encumbered;
 - priority under the applicable legal context;
 - cure, restructuring, recovery, and insolvency rules;
 - voting and consent requirements for material modification;
 - whether any guarantee exists and its payer, reserve, scope, limit, and expiry;
 - the difference between modeled recovery and legally enforceable recovery; and
 - conflicts between contractual terms and jurisdictional law.

No bailout, deposit protection, principal protection, liquidity promise, or state guarantee is implied. Every guarantee
is itself a registered liability and requires Authority, Resource, Rule, Context, provenance, and a funded loss path.

Economic ownership must not grant arbitrary control over workers, customers, citizens, personal data, bodies, minds, or
constitutional rights. Voting rights are explicit, bounded, contextual, and subordinate to Constitution, Law, consent,
and
RIGHT-025.

9. FINANCIAL INVARIANT REFERENCES — FIN-001..020

Canonical definitions: Annexure A.
Invariant references: FIN-001..020.

10. THEORY BINDINGS — AUTHORITATIVE CENTRAL REGISTRY VIEW
Registry reference: REG-SPF-BIND-001.

11. RESEARCH HYPOTHESES AND FALSIFICATION

Invariant references: SPF-HYP-001., SPF-HYP-002., SPF-HYP-003., SPF-HYP-004., SPF-HYP-005., SPF-HYP-006..
The exploratory numerical targets—productive investment +30%, financial sector share <=3%, Gini reduction >=15 points,
and crash probability <=1% per decade—are preserved only as UNCALIBRATED_RESEARCH_TARGETS. They are not canonical
predictions, requirements, or evidence. Engine.Research must establish jurisdictional baselines, definitions, causal
identification, confidence intervals, trade-offs, and feasible thresholds before any target can be promoted.

Required comparisons include:

 - current-market baseline;
 - debt-only counterfactual;
 - non-traded participation counterfactual;
 - PCE cadence and liquidity variants;
 - permitted-hedge boundary variants;
 - transition and regulatory-arbitrage scenarios;
 - adversarial manipulation and shadow-market migration; and
 - distributional effects by income, firm size, sector, region, and generation.

The candidate is weakened, revised, paused, or rejected when controlled evidence shows persistent material deterioration
in productive investment, access to risk capital, household liquidity/welfare, innovation, systemic tail risk, fairness,
or enforceability, without a compensating improvement accepted through Governance.

A raw rise in bankruptcy or volatility alone is not sufficient falsification; measurement must distinguish healthy
failure/price discovery from destructive contagion, manipulation, hidden leverage, or loss socialization.

UNKNOWN, CONFLICTED, NOT_APPLICABLE, DO_NOTHING, PARTIAL_ADOPTION, and ROLLBACK are
valid outcomes.

12. EVIDENCE-GATED TRANSITION

Phase 1 — RESEARCH (planning prior: 2–5 years)
 Agent-based simulation, historical back-testing, legal analysis, adversarial
 modeling, prototype auctions, shadow-market analysis, and alternative designs.

Phase 2 — BOUNDED PILOT (planning prior: 5–10 years)
 Voluntary, reversible pilots in selected sectors/regions. Existing lawful claims
 remain valid. No pilot may silently impair property, contract, pension, savings,
 consent, or due-process rights.

Phase 3 — CONDITIONAL EXPANSION (planning prior: 10–20 years)
 Expansion only after independent evidence, proof obligations, affected-party
 review, legal authorization, safety gates, and demonstrated capital access.

Phase 4 — POSSIBLE COMPLETION (planning prior: 20–30 years)
 New issuance migrates only if prior phases demonstrate dominance over alternatives.
 Lawfully existing instruments are redeemed, converted with valid consent, or allowed
 to mature; they are not confiscated or retroactively nullified.

Durations are planning priors, not automatic deadlines. Evidence gates control advancement. Failure permits pause,
redesign, partial adoption, or rollback.

SECTION III-N — RESEARCH RADAR, PRIVACY-PRESERVING EPISTEMICS, PRODUCTIVE UTILITY AND PHYSICAL PROFILES

15.1 RTR v1.0 — RESEARCH-AND-THEORY RADAR

STATUS: CURRENT CONTINUOUS RESEARCH PROCESS. Discovery is not validation and RTR is not an eighth theory.

Purpose
 Continuously discover established/common-use and frontier research across lawful scientific sources;
 preserve provenance, licensing, retraction and uncertainty; evaluate applicability; generate bounded
 UpgradeHypotheses; and route them through Engine.Research, Engine.Reason, Engine.Proof, Engine.Compliance,
 Engine.Evolve and the Theory-to-Reality flow.

Source classes include OpenAlex, OpenAIRE, Crossref, CORE, arXiv, Europe PMC, NASA ADS, ClinicalTrials.gov,
OpenCitations, retraction/correction registries, standards bodies, scholarly societies, patent databases, and
domain-specific primary repositories. A source list is versioned and non-exhaustive. Technical evaluation prefers
primary papers, official documentation, standards and reproducible artifacts over secondary summaries.

Schema references: SCHEMA-88F2C54093 — ExternalTheoryArtifact.; SCHEMA-6492A46478 — CoverageReceipt.; SCHEMA-A910351229
                   — UpgradeHypothesis.; SCHEMA-3EB6DE98CF — EpistemicDiminishingReturnPolicy.;
                   SCHEMA-51ED3153AC — HypothesisArchiveRecord.; SCHEMA-E6A6AF58ED — HypothesisReactivationRequest..
RTR applies the Research diminishing-return policy to repeated sweeps and unresolved leads. Coverage, expected
information gain, new-method availability, impact and protected research floors govern priority. Moving a lead to Deep
Archive preserves its epistemic state/history and keeps explicit reactivation routes; it never means the claim is FALSE.
Invariant references: RTR-001..010, EPI-DR-001..008.
Submitted external-theory discovery queue
 The following labels are preserved only as
 SOURCE_REQUIRED discovery leads: FLOW/emergent spacetime; GUP effective metrics; gravity duality of
 networks; string-theoretic mathematics for neural wiring; Unified Theory of Meaning (UToM);
 IIT–Free-Energy-Principle bridges; simplicial-complex growth; SG-7 information/network geometry;
 epiplexity/time-bounded information; Entropic Learnability Horizon; managed misalignment; and kinetic
 theories of socio-economic structure. Their names, publication claims, dates, novelty, equations,
 evidence, licenses and patent status are not established by their appearance in the discussion.
 RTR must resolve each label to a primary-source ExternalTheoryArtifact and evaluate it independently.
 None is a Garden theory, Core Object, primitive, validated domain model or approved upgrade merely by
 being listed. Until source resolution, its status is UNKNOWN/UNVERIFIED; a failed resolution is retained
 as NOT_FOUND or AMBIGUOUS rather than silently mapped to a similarly named work.

15.2 ZKEPP v1.0 — ZERO-KNOWLEDGE EPISTEMIC PROOF PIPELINE

STATUS: BOUNDED CRYPTOGRAPHIC IMPLEMENTATION PROFILE; security proof and implementation validation pending.

Purpose
 Permit a proposer to demonstrate that a declared derivation was executed over committed, authorized
 inputs without disclosing protected inputs or the full intermediate trace to every recipient.

Schema references: SCHEMA-6D6BA3C260 — ZKEpistemicProofEnvelope.; SCHEMA-A96BD32E7A — ZKEpistemicProofReceipt..
Pipeline
 Local protected Observation/Evidence
 → preserve raw attested source under TMR-002
 → commit to inputs and current revocation/snapshot roots
 → execute declared typed GSL transformation/circuit
 → generate ZK proof
 → Fabric/GAIA Diamond asynchronous verification
 → ZKEpistemicProofReceipt
 → Knowledge stores CLAIM with DERIVATION_VERIFIED at most
 → independent evidential/empirical evaluation continues
 → Titanium retains local K0P veto.

Invariant references: ZKEPP-001..008.
15.3 POU v0.1 — PRODUCTIVE-UTILITY ACCOUNTING AND FUNDING RESEARCH PROFILE

STATUS: DOMAIN-THEORY / ECONOMIC-DESIGN CANDIDATE. Not an established monetary theorem or automatic minting rule.

PoU may measure realized physical or computational output and route verified public revenue, savings, tax receipts,
contractual receivables or authorized productive returns into a treasury. It cannot make heterogeneous output equal to
money by declaration and cannot guarantee bounded inflation.

Schema references: SCHEMA-591FF2F081 — ProductiveOutputReceipt.; SCHEMA-1BF70DC7A6 — UtilityReceivable..
Architecture
 Domain.Applied.Industry PLC/sensors
 → read-only hardware-attested ProductiveOutputReceipt
 → one-way/data-diode measurement gateway
 → Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric decoupled multi-source verification and deduplication
 → Proof/Compliance/Knowledge assessment
 → UtilityReceivable
 → Domain.Society.Economics treasury policy, valuation caps and staged realization
 → liquid reserve only after legally/economically realized funding.

Invariant references: POU-001..008.
15.4 MODULAR URBAN ECOSYSTEM PROFILE — JEEVIT / EKAKI

STATUS: CONTEXTUAL PHYSICAL TEMPLATE FAMILY; not a universal city geometry.

The profile may define repeatable urban blocks combining residences, commerce, mobility, utilities, green space, food
production, emergency access, maintenance and local energy/water systems. A nominal 100m × 100m block is a candidate
pilot size, never a globally hardcoded optimum.

Schema references: SCHEMA-86C663091C — UrbanTemplate..
Invariant references: URB-001..005.
15.5 ACTIVE-MATTER, ATFT AND EXTERNAL PHYSICS BOUNDARY

Active-matter mathematics may become a DOMAIN_THEORY binding for systems whose agents inject energy, self-propel or
locally organize and whose equations/parameters are validated. It does not upgrade TFT into a quantum-to-city universal
physical law. “Active transport” in governance or information systems is an abstract model unless an independently
validated mapping establishes physical meaning.

Invariant references: ATFT-001..004.
The proposed “Infinite Electron Theorem” has no authoritative statement, proof, provenance or independent validation in
the current evidence registry. It is NOT_REGISTERED as a usable theory. It may enter RTR as an ExternalTheoryArtifact
only after source identification; until then its status is UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED.

“Holographic state reconstruction” is also non-normative unless formalized. Current recovery uses content-addressed
Merkle/causal graphs, erasure coding, authenticated summaries and explicit data- availability limits; partial data
cannot reconstruct information not encoded with sufficient redundancy.

15.6 INTELLECTUAL-PROPERTY AND OPEN-KNOWLEDGE BOUNDARY

Garden may learn from facts, abstract ideas, mathematical principles and lawful public research, but must not copy
protected expression, code, data, figures or proprietary implementation details without permission. Public accessibility
does not equal public domain. Every implementation records license, copyright, attribution, data-use and
patent-clearance state.

Specific patent portfolios, “GESTALT V6.2,” Standard Essential Patent claims, gainshare enforcement and royalty capture
are not automatically incorporated merely because named in an audit. Capability.Authority.Law, Domain.Society.Economics
and Engine.Compliance may maintain an IP registry and perform bounded prior-art/freedom-to-operate analysis. Legal
conclusions require qualified jurisdiction-specific review. Garden-generated eligible IP follows the Common Fund policy,
subject to valid law and contributor agreements.

Invariant references: IP-001..004.

SemanticAnchor: [T-XCUT]
12.34 CROSS-CUTTING RATIFIED EXTENSIONS — CURRENT DESIGN

These specifications are part of the current design. They are not a new top-level architectural kind and do not change
GSL v45.1 semantics. They bind into the authoritative typed graph through the owners stated below.

12.34.1 REGIME / NON-STATIONARY RISK

Schema references: SCHEMA-1D6D5C247F — RegimeRiskAssessment..
Stationarity and ergodicity are distinct. Regime change, heavy tails, path dependence, structural breaks and
extrapolation outside a model's validated region may raise the minimum AAP assurance profile. No universal population
percentage, probability threshold or fixed tail cutoff is constitutionalized; thresholds are context/model/policy bound.
Statistical methods such as change-point detection or EVT may contribute when their assumptions apply; they do not
predict literal "unknown unknowns" or authorize action by themselves.
Invariant references: REGIME-001..010.
12.34.2 PROOF RESOURCE / ENERGY ACCOUNTING

Schema references: SCHEMA-EFBA1028BC — ProofResourceBudget..
Proof/AAP plan real compute, memory, energy, thermal, network, time and hardware constraints. Resource exhaustion may
relocate, defer or block proof computation; it cannot lower a mandatory assurance requirement. If required assurance
cannot be completed, the governed action remains blocked/deferred. Critical obligations may receive protected resources,
but no computation is required to run forever.
Invariant references: PROOF-RES-001..010.
12.34.3 ADVANCED TYPED GEOMETRY / TOPOLOGY

Domain.Mathematics exposes reusable typed artifacts including:
 Manifold<T>; DifferentiableManifold<T>; RiemannianManifold<T>; PseudoRiemannianManifold<T>;
 SymplecticManifold<T>; Chart; Atlas; MetricTensor; TangentSpace; Geodesic; Curvature;
 SymplecticState; DiscretizationContract.

A generic manifold does not require a metric or curvature. Specialized geometric structures declare their extra
requirements. Numeric fields use typed Scalar/Quantity/ErrorBound/NumericalResult forms rather than raw Float semantic
quantities. Every discrete approximation declares scheme, error estimate/bound, convergence order/status and validity
scope where applicable. Symplectic methods preserve the declared symplectic structure within their formal/numerical
contract; Hamiltonian/energy error is separately bounded rather than assumed exactly conserved.

GSL-PHYSICS, robotics, orbital mechanics, relativity, continuum models, Mobility, Space, Energy and TFT may bind these
types when applicable. Engine.Proof verifies formal obligations; empirical/numerical convergence and model credibility
also require the relevant tests/evidence.
Invariant references: GEOM-001..015.
12.34.4 COMPOSITIONAL MATHEMATICS RESEARCH PROFILE

Schema references: SCHEMA-A4DDDE53E9 — CategoryTheoreticCompositionProfile..
Engine.Research may evaluate category-theoretic or other compositional mathematics for dependency/interface composition,
proof reuse and global coherence. Promotion into core proof/composition requires benchmarked benefit, proof/semantic
equivalence for the declared scope and no regression of existing invariants. No fixed complexity reduction or "90%"
saving is presumed.
Invariant references: COMP-MATH-001..010.
12.34.5 PRE-AUTHORIZED SURVIVAL CONTINGENCY

Schema references: SCHEMA-DF7470FC6D — SurvivalContingencyContract..
The contract must be constitutionally/human authorized before loss of communication under the authority required by
HSA-004A and related rules. Loss of contact never creates new constitutional authority and never permits autonomous
constitutional amendment. Any no-return/non-interference requirement is represented as a typed independently enforceable
constraint rather than a bare Boolean. Restored lawful human governance triggers the declared reconciliation/dormancy
path.
Invariant references: SURV-CONT-001..010.
12.34.6 EVIDENCE / REGIME INTEGRATION WITH GSL-COMPARE

Schema references: SCHEMA-5DD60E28CB — EvidenceDependenceProfile.; SCHEMA-D542BF13FA — RegimeRiskProfile..
ComparisonJudgment consults these profiles where relevant. Multiple records do not become independent confirmation
merely by count, and historical stability alone cannot justify low-risk classification when the regime/model has
changed. Engine.Proof checks formal consistency of the declared models/calculations; empirical credibility remains
evidence-bound.
Invariant references: EVID-REGIME-001..010.
12.34.7 BACKWARD PREIMAGE / REACHABILITY ANALYSIS

Schema references: SCHEMA-354CDDC1FF — PreimageAnalysis..
Pre(T,Y,C) = { X | T(X) in Y and C(X) holds } for the declared transition semantics. No general inverse transition is
assumed. Engine.Reason uses backward reachability for root-cause, necessary-condition and WHY_NOT analysis. Engine.Proof
may verify the formal derivation relative to the declared transition/model; it does not establish that the model is a
true description of reality.
Invariant references: BACK-REACH-001..005.
12.34.8 MEASUREMENT-ACTION SEMANTICS

Schema references: SCHEMA-DA7F8D2471 — MeasurementAction.; SCHEMA-D388A79CD3 — MeasurementRecord..
Canonical flow where acquisition is active:
 MeasurementAction -> MeasurementRecord -> ObservationRecord / EvidenceCandidate.
Not every Observation requires a MeasurementAction: historical records, testimony, imported datasets and other sources
remain possible. Quantum profiles may specialize measurement using valid quantum-instrument/POVM semantics; quantum
semantics are not forced onto ordinary observations.
Invariant references: MEAS-ACT-001..005.
12.34.9 COMPARATOR EVOLUTION ASSURANCE

Schema references: SCHEMA-67642E9E9F — ComparatorEvolutionPlan..
No comparator may solely certify its own replacement. Previous Compare is one evidence/reference path, never sole
authority. Adoption may legitimately choose a Pareto tradeoff or retain both profiles; simple dominance is not required.
Invariant references: COMP-EVOL-001..005.
12.34.10 EXPLICIT ABSTRACTION REASONING MODE

Engine.Reason includes REASONING_MODE_ABSTRACTION as a named front end to existing generalization machinery.
Schema references: SCHEMA-9DEE5B347E — AbstractionModeResult..
Abstraction retains exceptions/scope/counterexamples and cannot silently widen applicability. Independent validation is
required before scope promotion. Its obligations are folded into existing RSN/INF families rather than creating a new
universal algebra or top-level subsystem.

12.34.11 END-TO-END VERIFICATION-TO-USE INTEGRITY

Schema references: SCHEMA-B3C2843805 — IntegrityHealth..
Capability.Trust.Reliability is a typed sub-capability/profile over Reliability OS, Hardware, Runtime, Fabric and
Security, not a new top-level kind. A checked artifact remains integrity-bound from verification through consumption or
execution. Rising fault/error rates feed AAP and may increase assurance, degrade capability, isolate, fallback or halt
according to the applicable safety contract. Redundancy/TMR is used only where justified.
Invariant references: RUNTIME-INT-001..005.
12.34.12 AGENT SESSION INTEGRITY / RE-GROUNDING

Schema references: SCHEMA-5828A43B41 — DriftAssessment..
AgentSession is extended with:
 frozen_objective_root; context_provenance_root; semantic_drift: DriftAssessment;
 instruction_conflict_state: CLEAR | CONFLICT_DETECTED | UNKNOWN;
 behavioral_deviation; session_age; resource_exposure; last_regrounding_time;
 reauthorization_status.

Periodically or risk-triggered, the current agent state is compared with the authorized baseline. Excessive/unresolved
drift may isolate, reset or reconstruct from trusted state and require reauthorization. No universal action count or raw
entropy threshold is mandated.
Invariant references: AGENT-SESS-001..005.
12.34.13 DISTRIBUTIONAL / WORST-AFFECTED IMPACT ANALYSIS

Schema references: SCHEMA-7C98F3C8C7 — SocialImpactAnalysis..
Consequential social proposals evaluate both aggregate effects and concentrated/worst-affected harms. Fundamental rights
remain hard floors regardless of vote or aggregate gain. No universal bottom-percent threshold is encoded. Engine.Proof
may verify formal rights/policy consistency; real-world impact claims remain grounded in evidence, research, domain
analysis and affected-human input.
Invariant references: SOCIAL-IMP-001..005.
12.34.14 KNOWLEDGE STEWARDSHIP BOUNDARY

Schema references: SCHEMA-136D123E01 — KnowledgeStewardshipBoundary..
Restriction requires legitimate consent, confidentiality, privacy, legal, safety, contractual or recognized stewardship
authority. Community membership alone creates no restriction authority. Garden never infers that a person is forbidden
knowledge merely because of ethnicity, nationality, religion or presumed cultural membership. Context.LocaleCulture is
not itself an access-control authority.
Invariant references: STEWARD-001..005.
12.34.15 TEMPORAL CONTEXT BINDING

Schema references: SCHEMA-AA13DD845A — TemporalContextBinding..
Consequential evaluation explicitly binds its effective time/interval. Historical questions reconstruct rules, law,
knowledge and context as applicable then. Current knowledge may explain historical states but cannot silently be
attributed to historical actors. Current operational decisions cannot silently reuse historically applicable law/policy.
Invariant references: TEMP-CONT-001..005.
12.34.16 CONSERVATION BREACH DISPOSITION

Schema references: SCHEMA-7A9DB86C10 — ConservationBreachDisposition..
A conservation residual exceeding its declared tolerance triggers the action required by the applicable physical/safety
contract. No universal immediate HALT is mandated because abrupt halt may itself be unsafe. High-consequence actuation
cannot proceed on an invalid physical result unless a separately valid safe fallback contract authorizes the
alternative.
Invariant references: PHYS-016..020.
12.34.17 REASONING WORKLOAD PREEMPTION

Schema references: SCHEMA-9C4C95E5E3 — ReasoningPreemptionContract..
AAP may reserve domain-appropriate emergency compute/resources. Checkpointable/suspendable nonurgent workloads yield to
higher-priority emergency work. No universal fixed reserve percentage or checkpoint interval is mandated. Preemption
changes scheduling only; it never bypasses Constitution, rights, K0P, legitimate authority or mandatory assurance.
Invariant references: REAS-PRE-001..005.
12.34.18 EXPLANATION REDACTION RECEIPT

Schema references: SCHEMA-F716868FB6 — RedactionReceipt..
Where legally/safely disclosable, a redaction identifies the category, reason/basis, authority class, scope and
available appeal/review route. The receipt itself cannot leak the protected content or sensitive authority identity.
When even the reason/category cannot safely be disclosed, Garden emits the maximum lawful contestability metadata and
records the full basis in protected audit state.
Invariant references: EXP-017..021.
12.34.19 TEMPORAL EPISTEMIC APPLICABILITY

Schema references: SCHEMA-7BA5A10A34 — EpistemicApplicabilityRecord..
Garden distinguishes a changed world, changed definition/taxonomy/rule, narrower scope and empirical refutation. A
historically applicable classification is not automatically a false statement about that historical context. Historical
applicability never silently authorizes current operational use.
Invariant references: KNW-019..023.
12.34.20 TRANSITIVE CACHE INTEGRITY BINDING

Schema references: SCHEMA-EAD335E1A2 — CacheBindingManifest..
This strengthens existing ESC/other cache semantics. A relevant compromise/change/invalidation anywhere in the committed
transitive dependency/source/environment closure invalidates or downgrades the dependent cache before operational reuse.
The manifest is not a claim that every possible hidden dependency has been discovered; closure scope and unknowns remain
explicit.
Invariant references: DEP-007..010.
12.34.21 ADVANCE PERSONAL DIRECTIVE / AUTHORITY DELEGATION

Schema references: SCHEMA-656DCC2655 — AdvanceDirective..
The human retains RIGHT-017 personal sovereignty. An AI/tool/agent may communicate or execute only the person's prior
lawful bounded directive; it never becomes owner of the constitutional right. Competence, witnessing/authentication,
revocation, surrogate hierarchy and non-delegable decisions are context/jurisdiction specific. Revocation while
competent and applicable has priority under the directive's lawful rules.
Invariant references: HS-010..015, AGT-015..018.
12.34.22 SCALABLE GOVERNANCE PARTICIPATION PROFILE

Schema references: SCHEMA-EA72474099 — GovernanceParticipationProfile..
Governance may use direct voting, representative institutions, local governance, professional authority, sortition or
bounded delegated participation as legitimate context permits. Silence is never automatically consent. Thresholds are
profile/context specific rather than universal magic numbers, except where a ratified constitutional profile explicitly
sets one. Constitutional rights remain hard floors independent of majority.
Invariant references: GOV-005..010.
12.34.23 HUMAN RIGHTS REALITY SWEEP — EVOLUTION PROFILE

Schema references: SCHEMA-2F11ED9DC6 — HumanRightsRealitySweep..
Engine.Evolve periodically asks: "Given current reality, technology, Garden capabilities and observed harms, is a basic
human protection missing?" It scans the whole relevant Garden graph, new domains/technologies, recurring incidents,
forms of control/dependency and rights interactions. It may nominate a CandidateRight or RightsGapReport only.
Evolution/AI cannot silently create, delete, weaken or renumber constitutional rights. Candidate rights require the
applicable constitutional/human process, conflict analysis, explanation and provenance. Major capability changes may
trigger an event-driven sweep in addition to periodic review.
Invariant references: HR-EVO-001..008.
