12.35.1 INVARIANT-TO-PROOF TRACEABILITY
InvariantBinding has one canonical definition and explicitly carries proof_obligation_refs. A projection may abbreviate
that record but cannot redefine it. Formal discharge status, empirical evidence requirements and assurance obligations
remain separate fields; an invariant with no formal proof obligation states that explicitly.

12.35.2 SOLVER ESCALATION
Schema references: SCHEMA-006F545465 — SolverEscalationContract.
Solver escalation is a declared contract, not an ad-hoc fallback. A profile may choose exact, convex/non-convex,
mixed-integer, interval, symbolic, numerical, approximation or heuristic rungs as applicable. Each rung declares its
trigger, soundness/approximation class, resource budget and terminal behavior. Exhaustion or inadmissibility returns
UNKNOWN/BLOCKED rather than fabricated success.

12.35.3 EXTERNAL INTERFACE CONTRACT
Schema references: SCHEMA-809F633F52 — ExternalInterfaceContract.
Garden's universal external boundary is transport-neutral. REST, gRPC, message-bus and GSL-over-wire are implementation
profiles. Every external call binds authentication, authorization, semantic version, payload schema, effects, privacy,
rate/resource limits, failure/UNKNOWN behavior and audit receipts.

12.35.4 STANDARDIZED ADAPTER / MIGRATION MANIFEST
Schema references: SCHEMA-0C519B39F0 — AdapterMigrationManifest.
External migrations expose only the capabilities admitted for the declared migration level. Semantic mappings,
compliance evidence, validation and rollback/exit are explicit. Adapter convenience cannot silently expand authority or
turn a partial migration into full Garden conformance.

12.35.5 PERFORMANCE / SLO CONTRACT
Schema references: SCHEMA-59424A3587 — PerformanceContract.
Performance expectations are typed operational contracts rather than hidden assumptions. SLO breach may trigger
degradation, re-routing, capacity action or AAP escalation, but cannot weaken mandatory safety/rights/proof
requirements.

12.35.6 STANDARDIZED TELEMETRY PROFILE
Schema references: SCHEMA-DB9D1DE06B — TelemetryProfile.
Metrics, traces and logs use versioned semantic conventions with clock/causal binding, integrity, privacy, retention and
sampling rules. OpenTelemetry-compatible export is a profile, not the universal semantic source of truth.

12.35.7 DEPENDENCY-GENERATED BUILDMANIFEST
Schema references: SCHEMA-564D68466E — BuildManifest.
Build order is generated from the typed dependency graph, trust-root prerequisites and target scope. Parallelizable
groups are permitted when dependencies allow them. There is no single permanently hard-coded total order for every
Garden implementation.

12.35.8 GARDEN VERSION MANIFEST
Schema references: SCHEMA-4CD4F53977 — GardenVersionManifest.
Every accepted DesignEpoch binds one cryptographic manifest covering the exact GSL/AAP/PA/GSL-PHYSICS/Compare,
registry, schema, compiler/CIR, profile and implementation/validation versions in scope. Version skew is explicit and
cannot be hidden by matching document names.

12.35.9 DEPLOYMENT CONFIGURATION STORE
Schema references: SCHEMA-295430A823 — ConfigurationStore.
Deployment-specific model names, timeouts, retry/backoff values, network parameters, local thresholds and similar knobs
live outside universal architecture semantics. Configuration changes are typed, authorized, validated, versioned,
reversible where possible and bound into execution/provenance.

12.35.10 EXECUTABLE GSL REFERENCE CORPUS
Schema references: SCHEMA-D21E3B98F7 — ExecutableGSLReferenceCorpus.
The normative grammar is accompanied by positive/negative parse, type, effect, authority, ambiguity and Unicode-security
cases. Independent Haskell/Rust/OCaml implementations are checked against the same expected CIR/error corpus; agreement
is necessary but does not prove the shared semantics correct.

12.35.11 VERIFICATION COMPLEXITY ESTIMATE
Schema references: SCHEMA-DB817B900B — VerificationComplexityEstimate.
This extends existing AAP/ReasoningBudget/ProofResourceBudget machinery rather than creating a new budget system. Before
expensive global verification, Garden estimates state/search space, dependency width/depth, solver complexity, resource
cost and uncertainty, then selects decomposition/reuse/approximation only where the assurance contract permits it.

12.35.12 OFFLINE / PARTITION RECONCILIATION PROFILE
Schema references: SCHEMA-A160A9FC6E — OfflineReconciliationProfile.
Offline reconciliation is an explicit state machine under declared FLP/CAP assumptions. A partition may preserve bounded
local authority but cannot fabricate global quorum/finality. Conflicting high-impact histories remain unresolved until
the declared recovery/reconstitution procedure establishes a valid epoch.

12.35.13 HSA EXCEPTIONAL AUTHORIZATION RECEIPT
Schema references: SCHEMA-5610BD71D0 — HSAExceptionalAuthorizationReceipt.
This is NOT an HSA or K0P override. It records an exceptional but still conformant emergency/off-world authorization,
including human authority, constitutional/HSA/K0P checks, hardware attestations, scope, expiry and independent
verification.

12.35.14 EMERGENCY TIMING PROFILE
Schema references: SCHEMA-0024EAF21E — EmergencyTimingProfile.
Generic Garden has no universal 100 ms quorum or 300 s emergency duration. Timing, renewal, seal and reconciliation
bounds are profile/domain/risk parameters. Hard-real-time local safety paths remain independent of best-effort
wide-area consensus.

12.35.15 TRUST-ROOT CEREMONY STATE / RECEIPT
Schema references: SCHEMA-6631825CAA — TrustRootCeremonyReceipt.
The existing genesis/replacement rules are made operationally explicit: genesis, rotation, replacement and compromise
recovery each produce a multi-party receipt binding diverse checkers, reproducible builds, hardware measurements, key
custody, external verification, constitutional authorization and rollback/quarantine.
Device-specific HardwareGenesisReceipt and per-boot BootAttestationReceipt specialize and link to this system-level
ceremony record; they do not redefine or duplicate the ceremony's constitutional authority.

12.35.16 THEORY DEMARCATION
Schema references: SCHEMA-18DF6B1AEF — TheoryDemarcationRecord.
Each core theory explicitly separates formal mathematical content, engineering metaphor/heuristic content and empirical
hypotheses. A failed empirical claim cannot be rescued by relabeling it "transcendent" or "out-of-model"; OUT_OF_MODEL
means no prediction was made for that case.

12.35.17 OPEN FRONTIERS — RESEARCH / OPEN PROBLEMS
These are research gaps, not patent claims and not established facts.

  CAPABILITY–STABILITY COUPLING
    Status : d(instability)/d(capability) remains unsolved; a candidate theorem/model requires formalization and
             evidence.

  GEOMETRIC VIABILITY / NAGUMO
    Status : Tangent-cone/viability formulation remains an open refinement beyond simple probability-threshold safety
             models.

  SCALABLE ALIGNMENT UNDER RECURSIVE SELF-IMPROVEMENT
    Status : Open research problem; convergence or consensus is not proof.

  MoS₂ VOLUME QUALIFICATION
    Status : Experimental hardware path; conventional silicon/CMOS remains the practical fallback until qualification.

12.36 OPERATIONAL RIGOR EXTENSIONS
SemanticAnchor: [T-OPRIG]

STATUS
  RATIFIED / ADDITIVE / DESIGN-LEVEL.
  GSL remains v45.1. The authoritative typed architecture-object graph has no fixed module/component count; the ten
  Core Objects, 24 Core Relations and active/non-active Rights registry remain unchanged. Specification does not
  imply proof, implementation, validation or deployment.

PURPOSE
Close eight operationalization gaps involving view correspondence, secure cold start, inference validation routing,
finite self-audit closure, device-root attestation, physical conservation accounting, research-priority decay and
cross-jurisdiction legal synthesis. Each upgrade is owned by existing architecture objects and registered below.

UPGRADE INDEX — READER VIEW

  U-01  PROJECTION CORRESPONDENCE
    Owners    : GardenCore.Formal.MathInterface / Domain.Mathematics / Engine.Proof / Engine.Compare
    Artifacts : ProjectionCorrespondenceProof
    Outcome   : A Cube × Ring view may substitute for the authoritative graph only within a declared, proven fragment.
    Boundary  : No global isomorphism is assumed; explicit bounded loss remains permissible where fit for purpose.

  U-02  BOOTSTRAP INTEGRITY AND DIAGNOSTIC PRECEDENCE
    Owners    : Engine.Orchestrate/AAP / Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware / Capability.Compute.Runtime / Reliability /
    Capability.Trust.Security / Capability.Trust.Safety
    Artifacts : BootstrapIntegrityManifest, DiagnosticPrecedenceContract
    Outcome   : Cold start has a finite trust path before live IntegrityHealth exists.
    Boundary  : Initial state is UNKNOWN/DEGRADED; only immutable-root-bound diagnostic/safe-init actions may run.

  U-03  REASONING-TO-VALIDATION ROUTING
    Owners    : Engine.Reason with Engine.Proof, Engine.Research, Capability.Authority.Law/Engine.Policy, domain test
    systems and human review
    Artifacts : ClaimValidationPlan
    Outcome   : Every promotable or consequential inference receives explicit validation destinations and criteria.
    Boundary  : Formal proof, empirical support, testing and authorization remain distinct and may all be required.

  U-04  FINITE SELF-AUDIT CLOSURE
    Owners    : Engine.Evolve / Engine.Compliance / Engine.Dependency / Engine.Proof
    Artifacts : AuditScopeClosure plus extended SelfAuditRequest and FullDesignConformanceAudit
    Outcome   : Full-design audit is finite, version-bound and executable for a declared deployment scope.
    Boundary  : The requester proposes scope; Garden computes and proves the required closure and justified exclusions.

  U-05  HARDWARE GENESIS AND BOOT ATTESTATION
    Owners    : Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware / Capability.Trust.Security / Engine.Proof / HumanCore.Constitution /
    AAP
    Artifacts : HardwareGenesisReceipt, BootAttestationReceipt, linked TrustRootCeremonyReceipt
    Outcome   : Device identity and measured boot are cryptographically connected to accepted Garden epochs.
    Boundary  : Genesis, epoch acceptance and each boot remain distinct immutable events; offline profiles use qualified
                freshness evidence rather than pretending external time is always available.

  U-06  PHYSICAL INGRESS / EGRESS ACCOUNTABILITY
    Owners    : GardenCore.Semantics.GSL-PHYSICS / applicable physical domains / Capability.Knowledge / Engine.Proof /
    Engine.Compliance
    Artifacts : PhysicalIngressEgressReceipt
    Outcome   : Matter, transformation, accumulation, energy, byproducts and uncertainty are reconciled at the declared
                physical boundary.
    Boundary  : Captured or converted material is not automatically removed; residuals trigger typed disposition.

  U-07  EPISTEMIC DIMINISHING RETURN AND DEEP ARCHIVE
    Owners    : Engine.Research / RTR / Capability.Knowledge / Capability.Compute.Runtime / Engine.Evolve
    Artifacts : EpistemicDiminishingReturnPolicy, HypothesisArchiveRecord, HypothesisReactivationRequest
    Outcome   : Repeated low-yield investigation cannot indefinitely starve higher-value research.
    Boundary  : Archival changes scheduling, never truth status; protected research floors and reactivation paths
                remain.

  U-08  LEGAL SOURCE AUTHENTICITY AND RIGHTS-PRESERVING SYNTHESIS
    Owners    : Capability.Authority.Law / Engine.Research / Capability.Authority.Governance / HumanCore.Constitution /
    Engine.Compare / Engine.Compliance
    Artifacts : LegalSourceAuthenticityProfile, ComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile
    Outcome   : Garden distinguishes source authenticity, local legal authority and comparative rights research.
    Boundary  : Population, popularity or citation influence cannot create precedence; conflicting/minority protections
                remain visible and comparative synthesis cannot manufacture world law.

ASSURANCE AND CHANGE BINDING
All U-01..U-08 artifacts bind DesignEpoch, applicable KnowledgeSnapshot/EnvironmentCommitment, provenance, status and
authority. Adoption follows ordinary AAP, dependency-impact, proof/evidence, self-conformance, governance and rollback
requirements. Artifact identities resolve through the Artifact Catalogue; machine schema/type layouts are generated;
canonical invariant definitions resolve through Annexure A. Static ownership and binding maps resolve through
REG-OPRIG-001.

12.37 MACHINE-PROJECTION, TYPE, DESIGN-EPOCH AND SELF-CROSS-REFERENCE CLOSURE

The human design defines semantic contracts, artifact identities, type-resolution laws, failure behavior, process
semantics and machine-generation requirements.
Concrete serialization/API/layout expansions are generated rather than hand-maintained in this document.
The source semantic field contracts retained here remain authoritative SOURCE unless a DEV-AI-026 receipt qualifies
a specific expansion for elision.

DesignEpoch readiness:
  DOCUMENT CROSS-REFERENCE     : document-level consistency audit required.
  ARTIFACT-CATALOGUE CLOSURE   : every current SchemaRef/artifact identity resolves to the catalogue.
  GENERATED TYPE CLOSURE       : zero unresolved generated identifiers required.
  DEFINITION LOCATION          : HUMAN explains; TECHNICAL specifies; ANNEXURES own recurring canonical definitions.
  MACHINE PARSE / TYPE CHECK   : PENDING generated GSL package and compiler receipts.
  DEPENDENCY / SCC CLOSURE     : PENDING generated dependency graph and checker receipts.
  PROOF OBLIGATION DISCHARGE   : PENDING except separately accepted artifacts.
  EXECUTABLE CONFORMANCE TESTS : PENDING generated tests and execution receipts.
  INDEPENDENT VERIFICATION     : PENDING.
  ACCEPTED DESIGN EPOCH        : NOT CLAIMED by this prose document alone.

GCL status:
  RATIFIED IN v15.4 AS A BOUNDED ANALYTICAL/THEORY PROFILE. It creates no authority and does not treat information as a
  new fundamental force. Canonical requirements resolve through [T-GCL] and [A-GCL].

Justice status:
  The separately proposed Justice System / CanonicalCrimeOntology is outside this release. No new Justice-domain
  architecture is inferred from the currently existing constitutional/legal/governance mechanisms.

RobustnessVerification  [SCHEMA-DABF7F7573] = {
  verification_id, target_root, attack_profile_refs, security_contract_refs, dimensions, results_by_attack,
  coverage, uncertainty, residual_risk, disposition, renewal_or_invalidators, provenance
}

12.37 RECURSIVE CONFORMANCE CLOSURE
SemanticAnchor: [T-RCC]

PHYSICAL-COGNITIVE RCC CLOSURE
- all predecessor canonical identifier/definition sets remain locally materialized unless explicitly superseded;
- exactly five new SchemaIDs and no DegradationProfile or AdaptationValidityDomain SchemaID;
- exactly 25 new invariants and 25 matching human conformance tests;
- REG-DEGRADATION-001 and REG-HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-001 resolve;
- AGI-SUB-P11..P13 are contiguous after AGI-SUB-P10;
- three new FunctionContracts and seven amended existing FunctionContracts are represented;
- S-DEGRADATION, S-CROSSMODAL and S-HARDWARE-ASSURANCE resolve;
- no rule treats model rollback as physical reversal, unknown risk as mandatory point probability, fallback as
  universal halt or hardware opacity as a universal ban.

SemanticID: T-RCC
HumanRefs: [H-04] [H-05] [H-07] [H-09] [H-13]
AnnexRefs: [A-FUNC] [A-RESULT] [A-PARAM] [A-EMG-ADM] [A-THEORY-EQ] [A-HUMAN-IN]

ROADMAP
  - close recurring result-algebra defects;
  - unify emergency authority/hazard/safety semantics;
  - make theory equations type/unit/domain checkable;
  - separate human testimony from human authority;
  - bind operational constants to qualified profiles;
  - make function-level GSL self-conformance machine-generatable.

12.37.1 EmergencyAdmissionContract
Schema reference: SCHEMA-800FE7707B — EmergencyAdmissionContract.
@SC|EmergencyAdmissionContract|-|declared_hazard,HazardPredicate,evidence/sensor/environment/calibration bindings,preauthorized_human_or_legal_authority,authenticity,scope,expiry,SafeActionSet,K0P/SafetyEnvelope binding,consent/delegation where applicable,fallback,recovery/compensation,audit,DesignEpoch,provenance

Emergency path:
  Observe hazard evidence
    -> Evaluate HazardPredicate
    -> verify pre-authorized authority/authenticity/scope/expiry
    -> K0P / Constitution / rights
    -> choose only within SafeActionSet
    -> execute local bounded response
    -> observe / receipt / recovery / audit.

Emergency never creates authority. It may replace ordinary deliberation only where the pre-authorized contract says so.

12.37.2 TheoryEquationContract
Schema reference: SCHEMA-DF0B143264 — TheoryEquationContract.
Every equation used for formal proof, numerical execution or claimed scientific applicability binds:
  variable identities and semantic types;
  units/dimensions and normalization scales;
  domain Ω / graph / state space;
  coordinate/reference frame and operators;
  initial/boundary conditions where applicable;
  assumptions and validity/breakdown conditions;
  stochastic/noise semantics;
  conservation/source terms;
  numerical representation/solver/error semantics;
  empirical mappings and falsifiers;
  theory/version/DesignEpoch/provenance.

An equation lacking required bindings may remain explanatory/model notation but is not machine-certified mathematics.

12.37.3 HumanInput separation
Schema reference: SCHEMA-0010A5A95B — HumanInput.
@SC|HumanInput|-|epistemic_contribution:EpistemicContribution?,authority_contribution:AuthorityContribution?

EpistemicContribution may be calibrated for source reliability under EPI/K-INT rules.
AuthorityContribution derives only from rights, consent, lawful role/delegation or constitutional authority.
No epistemic weight, model confidence, market score, reputation or predictive track record can attenuate a valid
AuthorityContribution.

12.37.4 QualifiedProfileParameter
Schema reference: SCHEMA-2632CB94AC — QualifiedProfileParameter<T>.
All operational constants use QualifiedProfileParameter<T>. Reference numbers are informative until bound to a named
profile and classified by source/status. Parameter changes propagate dependency and certificate invalidation.

SemanticAnchor: [T-FUNCTION-CONTRACT]

PHYSICAL-COGNITIVE FUNCTIONCONTRACT BINDINGS

UPDATED EXISTING FUNCTIONCONTRACTS — 7

1. execute_sub_epoch_adaptation
   Enforces extended AdaptationScope, appends consequential external effects, preserves the ledger through rollback and
   routes out-of-scope use through promotion.

2. handle_novelty
   Material safety-critical/K0P-bound novelty returns AUTONOMY_REVOKED and invokes qualified critical-continuity
   handoff; ontology/update nomination remains separate and non-authorizing.

3. resolve_common_sense_frame
   Produces/references UnmodelledRiskEnvelope and blocks or qualified-fallbacks when residual risk cannot be shown
   inside the active assurance envelope.

4. assess_value_intent
   Requires a valid InterpretationValidationReceipt for the controlling source-to-semantic chain.

5. assess_terminal_ego
   Accepts IntrospectionSideEffectClass and optional ReplicaEquivalenceAttestation; enforces effects budget/placement.

6. audit_mechanistic_behavior
   Accepts IntrospectionSideEffectClass and optional ReplicaEquivalenceAttestation; enforces method and transfer bounds.

7. execute_sensorimotor_loop
   Enforces extended AdaptationScope and consumes cross-modal conflict disposition before consequential actuation.
   Out-of-scope adaptation or unresolved material sensor conflict cannot silently retain ordinary actuation authority.

NEW FUNCTIONCONTRACTS — 3

execute_degradation_state(plan: DegradationPlan, observed_resources, current_state, authority_context)
  -> GenericResult<DegradationState>
  Requires a current ratified plan and adequate resource evidence. It may shed only listed functions, may reduce but not
  expand authority, preserves the non-sheddable set or transfers to a state that no longer requires the unavailable
  capability, applies hysteresis and requires fresh recovery evidence.
  Failures: BLOCKED | INVALID_PLAN | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | FALLBACK_REQUIRED | UNKNOWN.

resolve_crossmodal_conflict(observations, calibration_context, independence_context, assurance_context)
  -> Resolution<CrossModalConflictDisposition>
  Requires modality time/frame/calibration/provenance or explicit UNKNOWN. It may reduce actuation authority and select
  qualified fallback; majority agreement alone cannot establish truth.
  Failures: INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE | COMMON_MODE_SUSPECTED | FALLBACK_REQUIRED | UNKNOWN.

attest_hardware_capability(platform_roots, required_capabilities, assurance_context)
  -> HardwareAssuranceCapabilityAttestation
  Requires an identified attestation root/measurement chain or returns UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED. It computes the maximum
  assurance/authority ceiling supported by evidence and never creates underlying legitimate authority.
  Failures: ATTESTATION_INVALID | CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED | STALE | PARTIAL | UNKNOWN.

Engine.Compile generates a FunctionContract for every executable function and rejects:
  unresolved semantic types;
  missing effect dimensions;
  missing authority/failure/dependency bindings;

  unqualified operational constants;
  undeclared side effects;
  missing termination/resource bounds where required.

Current cognitive-hardening FunctionContracts in this registry:
  assess_terminal_ego; execute_sub_epoch_adaptation; assess_collective_agent_safety; handle_novelty;
  assess_value_intent; compose_hypothesis_portfolio; execute_sensorimotor_loop; resolve_common_sense_frame;
  verify_llm_adversarial_robustness; audit_mechanistic_behavior.

Each function inherits the ordinary FunctionContract fields and its [T-TED]..[T-MI] specialization. The function name
is not a new engine, authority source or independent result algebra.

12.37.6 Recursive conformance
Current cognitive-hardening closure: every affected AI/LLM/swarm/embodied-model change additionally resolves the
applicable TED/SEA/MAC/NRS/VIP/SHE/EC/CSR/AR/MI profile, AGI-SUB-P01..P10 obligation, profile tests and five dedicated
registries. A profile marked inapplicable must carry an explicit applicability result; silence cannot remove the
obligation. MI/AR/MAC/TED reuse their existing owner machinery rather than creating duplicate schema/registry authority.

After every design change:
  local contract check
    -> GSL type/effect/scope/authority
    -> Reason
    -> Compare against canonical owner + alternatives
    -> Proof/validation as applicable
    -> Dependency impact closure
    -> Safety/Security/Privacy/Law/Policy/Constitution
    -> TheoryEquationContract for changed equations
    -> Self-Audit / frozen independent verification
    -> DO_NOTHING comparator
    -> accept / revise / reject / UNKNOWN.

Dependencies:
  GardenCore.Semantics.GSL; Engine.Reason; Engine.Compare; Engine.Proof; Engine.Dependency; Engine.Compile;
  Engine.Evolve; Engine.Compliance; Capability.Trust.Safety/Security; HumanCore.Constitution; AAP.

Affects:
  every executable FunctionContract; emergency profiles; all seven core theories; machine projection; DesignEpoch
  readiness; conformance and explanation coverage.

Referenced By:
  all current detailed executable contracts; Engine.Orchestrate/AAP; Theory System;
  Self-Audit; DesignEpoch acceptance.

CURRENT DETAILED TECHNICAL EXTENSIONS

SemanticAnchor: [T-DOCUMENT-PLACEMENT]
LEVEL-OWNERSHIP CONTRACT

Level 2 owns system flow and may name artifacts/results/states needed to understand routing. Level 3 owns executable
signatures, field-level schemas, enums, retry/delivery/lease mechanics, pre/postconditions and implementation-facing
failure semantics. Level 4 owns canonical IDs, invariant text, tests and registries.

A technical contract relocated from Level 2 retains its semantic identity and backlinks; relocation is not deletion.

SemanticAnchor: [T-BELIEF-TIMELINE]
HISTORICAL EPISTEMIC STATE, BELIEFTIMELINE AND EPISTEMIC TRANSITION

Schema references:
  SCHEMA-AEFA4E3920 — HistoricalEpistemicState.
  SCHEMA-D127246F71 — BeliefTimeline.
  SCHEMA-8C56497911 — EpistemicTransition.

EpistemicTransitionReason =
  NEW_EVIDENCE
  | NEW_PROOF
  | CONTEXT_CHANGE
  | DEPENDENCY_INVALIDATION
  | CORRECTION_OF_ERROR
  | CONFLICT_RESOLUTION
  | SAFEGUARD_FAILURE
  | UNKNOWN.

@SC|HistoricalEpistemicState|-|claim_ref,requested_temporal_context,resolved_event_boundary,design_epoch,knowledge_snapshot,environment_commitment?,then_current_epistemic_status,then_current_evidence_refs[],assumption_refs[],dependency_refs[],validity_scope,uncertainty,reconstruction_completeness,provenance

@SC|EpistemicTransition|-|transition_id,claim_ref,from_claim_ref?,to_claim_ref?,from_state_ref:Ref<HistoricalEpistemicState>,to_state_ref:Ref<HistoricalEpistemicState>,supersedes_claim_ref?,transition_time,transition_reason:EpistemicTransitionReason,evidence_refs[],proof_refs[],prediction_refs[],reasoning_or_failure_trace_ref,dependency_closure_affected,authority_context?,consent_binding?:ConsentBinding,provenance

@SC|BeliefTimeline|-|claim_ref,first_believed_at?,ordered_state_refs[],transitions:List<EpistemicTransition>,gaps_or_unknown_intervals[],current_state_ref?,provenance

Interface:
  EpistemicStateAt(claim_ref, temporal_context)
    → HistoricalEpistemicState | UNKNOWN.

Canonical normalization:
  A belief/status change usually changes the epistemic state of one Claim rather than creating a new Claim identity.
  from_state_ref/to_state_ref therefore identify the transition states. from_claim_ref/to_claim_ref are optional lineage
  bindings used when Claim identity itself changes; otherwise they may resolve to claim_ref or remain omitted.
  supersedes_claim_ref is used only when the Claim artifact itself is replaced/refined. consent_binding is present only
  where consent is applicable; no belief update manufactures authority or consent.

Contract:
  Historical belief != current belief.
  Historical belief != historical truth.
  Later evidence cannot be injected into an earlier historical state.
  Every material change to a verified, structurally important or consequentially used epistemic status records a typed
  reason and traces to applicable evidence/proof/reasoning/failure-learning artifacts.
  An unexplained material transition emits a system-health finding.

Transition integration:
  CORRECTION_OF_ERROR
    → create SafeguardFailureFinding when an applicable safeguard was expected to catch the error and did not.
  SAFEGUARD_FAILURE
    → link the transition to the originating FailureLearningCase.
  DEPENDENCY_INVALIDATION
    → run a scoped proactive scan over materially dependent beliefs.
  CONTEXT_CHANGE
    → inspect analogous scope bindings; material recurring gaps may route to OntologicalDebt/Concept Radar.
  UNKNOWN
    → create/update FailureLearningCase with ROOT_CAUSE_UNKNOWN_WITHIN_BOUNDS and reactivation conditions.

Prediction calibration:
  When a materially changed claim previously informed one or more PredictionArtifact records, prediction_refs bind those
  forecasts to the transition. The applicable prediction-calibration/revalidation path compares the prior forecast with
  independently validated later evidence/outcome where available. EpistemicTransition by itself is not Evidence, an
  observed outcome, or ground truth and cannot self-calibrate a prediction.

Engine.Explanation supports:
  ExplainBeliefChange(claim_ref, transition_or_time) → ExplanationBundle.
  ExplainHistoricalBelief(claim_ref, temporal_context) → ExplanationBundle.
  ExplainRevalidationConditions(claim_ref) → ExplanationBundle.

Explanation must distinguish recorded trigger conditions from model-inferred possible future change conditions.

@SC|ContinuousFailureSweepReceipt|-|sweep_id,design_epoch,knowledge_snapshot,environment_commitment?,candidate_failure_cases[],priority_basis,cases_reopened[],new_analogues_found[],safeguard_findings_created[],certificates_invalidated[],prevention_updates[],coverage,deferred_cases[],resource_status,timestamp,provenance

Two-lane ownership:
  EventService
    → failure signal/event routing.
  Engine.Orchestrate/AAP
    → urgency, assurance and resource selection.
  Engine.Reason
    → causal/root-cause reasoning.
  Engine.Research
    → evidence acquisition and pattern generalization.
  Engine.Dependency
    → blast-radius/generalization/assurance-dependency closure.
  Engine.Evolve
    → prevention/repair lifecycle and durable recurrence-prevention integration.
  Engine.Proof/Compliance
    → applicable verification and conformance.
  Capability.Knowledge
    → immutable failure/pattern/regression history.

Immediate incident closed != epistemic obligation resolved.

If Fix A produces Failure B, Failure B enters the same process under the applicable bounded-recursion/resource contract.

When an auditor/verifier/proof method/dependency resolver/safety checker or other assurance mechanism is defective:
  discover materially dependent prior receipts/certificates/decisions/deployments
  → mark affected artifacts STALE or REVALIDATION_REQUIRED
  → recheck affected closure using an acceptable independent path
  → preserve the original historical certificates and invalidation reason.

UNIVERSAL TRIGGER, SUBSCRIPTION & SCHEDULING PROFILE

Schema references:

ProcessTriggerKind =
  REQUEST
  | EVENT
  | SCHEDULE
  | STATE_CHANGE

The current EventSubscription contract requires:
  subscriber_ref;
  event_pattern;
  scope/filter;
  delivery_semantics;
  authority/capability/privacy scope;
  rate/concurrency limits;
  expiry/revocation binding;
  replay policy;
  provenance.

DeliverySemantics =
  AT_MOST_ONCE
  | AT_LEAST_ONCE
  | EFFECTIVELY_ONCE.

Garden does not claim universal exactly-once distributed execution. Consequential work commonly uses at-least-once
delivery plus idempotency, effect identity, receipts and deduplication to obtain effectively-once effects where
feasible.

Delivery lifecycle:
  EMITTED
  → MATCHED
  → ENQUEUED
  → DELIVERED
  → ACKNOWLEDGED
  | RETRY_PENDING
  | EXPIRED
  | DEAD_LETTER
  | CANCELLED.

Delivery acknowledgement != successful process outcome.

@SC|ScheduledWork|-|work_id,process_ref,trigger_ref,priority_class,assurance_floor,earliest_start,deadline?,recurrence?,missed_run_policy?,resource_budget,required_capabilities,dependency_readiness,concurrency_class,preemption_class,cancellation_policy,fairness_age,retry_policy,lease_ref?,provenance

Semantic scheduling lanes:
  SAFETY_EMERGENCY
  CRITICAL_DEADLINE
  CONSEQUENTIAL_INTERACTIVE
  ORDINARY_INTERACTIVE
  ASSURANCE_BACKGROUND
  REVALIDATION_BACKGROUND
  RESEARCH_OPTIMIZATION
  ARCHIVAL_MAINTENANCE.

Priority is multi-dimensional and may consider consequence, urgency, deadline, criticality, uncertainty,
information value, dependency impact, fairness age and resource feasibility. A scalar implementation may be used only
when it preserves hard floors and its assumptions are explicit.

@SC|ActivationReceipt|-|trigger_ref,binding_ref,admission_result,policy_authority_context,selected_assurance_profile,scheduling_disposition,execution_ref?,effect_ref?,displaced_or_deferred_work_refs[],timestamp,provenance

Activation explanation:
  Engine.Explanation uses ActivationReceipt plus trigger/binding/subscription/scheduler traces to answer:
    Why did this process run?
    What triggered it and which binding/subscription activated it?
    Why was it scheduled at that time?
    Which policy, authority and assurance profile admitted it?
    What mandatory work was displaced or deferred, if any?
    What execution/effect/outcome followed?
  Missing trace data remains UNKNOWN/INCONCLUSIVE rather than being invented.

Ownership:
  Engine.Orchestrate/AAP
    → admission, assurance/resource/priority floors.
  Capability.State.TimeService
    → temporal eligibility, deadlines, clock degradation and missed-run semantics.
  Capability.State.EventService
    → event publication, matching, subscription, delivery and replay.
  Capability.Compute.Runtime
    → actual work queues, scheduling, resource admission, preemption and work ownership.
  Engine.Dependency
    → readiness/invalidation.
  Capability.Compute.Execution
    → consequential effects, idempotency and execution receipts.

No new top-level Scheduling Engine is created.

Policy relationship:
  Process Algebra != Policy Algebra != Runtime scheduler != AAP.
  Every consequential process remains policy-bound where policy applies.
  A hard-real-time/preauthorized process may execute inside a previously approved bounded policy/authority envelope.
  The cached envelope is invalid outside its scope, validity, DesignEpoch, authority and dependency closure.
  Boundary crossing or invalidation re-enters the deeper policy/assurance path.

Idempotency:
  Duplicate triggers cannot cause duplicate consequential effects unless duplicate execution is explicitly safe.
  trigger_id + idempotency_key + effect identity + execution receipt form the ordinary deduplication basis.

Loop/storm control:
  Every recursively generated trigger preserves causal ancestry.
  Trigger cycles are bounded by TerminationContract, oscillation detection, rate/resource limits and circuit breaking.
  Event volume cannot create authority or priority.
  Allowed responses include coalescing, throttling, defer, quarantine, safe fallback, halt and escalation.

Backpressure:
  LoadDisposition =
    ADMIT | COALESCE | DEFER | SHED_OPTIONAL | REJECT | SAFE_FALLBACK | ESCALATE.
  Critical events cannot be silently dropped because a queue is saturated.

Fairness/starvation:
  Mandatory eligible work accumulates starvation protection while waiting.
  New interactive work cannot indefinitely suppress mandatory historical audit, revalidation, failure-learning,
  rights/safety or other protected background obligations.

Priority inversion:
  Lower-priority ownership cannot indefinitely block a higher-priority mandatory deadline/safety obligation.
  Applicable profiles use priority inheritance, ceiling, reservation or bounded preemption.

Cancellation/preemption:
  CancellationClass =
    SAFE_IMMEDIATE
    | SAFE_AT_CHECKPOINT
    | COMPENSATION_REQUIRED
    | NON_PREEMPTIBLE_UNTIL_SAFE_BOUNDARY.
  Cancellation cannot pretend already committed irreversible effects never occurred.

Clock/missed-run:
  MissedRunPolicy =
    RUN_ONCE_ON_RECOVERY
    | RUN_ALL_MISSED
    | SKIP_MISSED
    | RECOMPUTE_FROM_CURRENT_STATE.
  Deadlines use monotonic time where required; human schedules bind locale/timezone explicitly.
  DEGRADED_TIME remains explicit.

Subscription privacy:
  Subscription scope cannot exceed valid purpose, authority, consent, privacy and retention scope.
  Filtering occurs before unauthorized payload disclosure where practicable.
  Revocation disables future protected delivery and re-evaluates queued work/material.

Continuous processes:
  ContinuousMonitor observes state/streams and emits triggers on declared conditions.
  ContinuousWorker repeatedly selects eligible backlog work.
  Loss of a mandatory monitor yields DEGRADED/UNKNOWN plus its declared fallback; silence is not health.

Distributed ownership:
  WorkLease/fencing semantics prevent a stale executor from committing consequential work after ownership has moved.

PRESENTATION QUALIFICATION CROSS-RULE

PresentationProjectionReceipt validates semantic/authority/qualification preservation for a candidate presentation;
it is not DEV-AI-026 qualification evidence. Every candidate output remains SOURCE or MIXED until a PASS
MachineProjectionReceipt establishes total deterministic projection and declared equivalence, and a PASS
GeneratedArtifactElisionReceipt records RETAINED_AS_DERIVED_VIEW. Hand editing makes the affected output MIXED/STALE.

Templates:
  TheoryCard
    → purpose / status / assumptions / model-or-equations / applicability / falsifiers / evidence / limits / references.
  EquationCard
    → equation / variables / units-dimensions-frame / assumptions / validity / interpretation / references.
  ArtifactCard
    → canonical name / purpose / owner / inputs / outputs / lifecycle / hard limits / schemas-invariants / references.
  SemanticCallout
    → PRINCIPLE | HARD_RULE | BOUNDARY | STATUS.
  Progressive disclosure
    → IN_BRIEF | ESSENTIAL_MODEL | DETAILED_CONTRACT | REFERENCES.

Output-view profiles:
  SINGLE_VOLUME_READER_EDITION
    → coherent linear path: Human narrative → System flows → selected Technical contracts → compact Annexure references.
  EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY
    → high-level architecture, purpose, major flows, safeguards, maturity and open status without qualifier loss.
  ENGINEERING_REFERENCE
    → implementation/search view emphasizing qualified names, interfaces, artifacts, schemas, dependencies, state
      machines, proof/test obligations and navigation indexes.
  AUDITORS_CHECKLIST
    → reconstructible assurance view emphasizing scope, DesignEpoch, applicability, hard rules, invariants, evidence,
      proof, exceptions, UNKNOWN, receipts, provenance and change history.

Breadcrumb policy:
  GARDEN › {H|S|T|A} › {canonical area} › {object or section}.
  Cross-document ribbon: H = Human meaning; S = System flow; T = Technical contract; A = Canonical definition/reference.
  Breadcrumbs are navigation only and never create containment, ownership or authority.

Default ReaderPath profiles:
  IMPLEMENTER
    → purpose/authority → flow → technical contract → schema → test/proof → deployment.
  AUDITOR
    → scope → hard rule → invariant → evidence/proof → exception/UNKNOWN → receipt/provenance.
  SCIENTIST
    → theory → assumptions → equation/model → applicability → evidence/falsifier → uncertainty → research gap.
  Additional audience paths may be generated without changing canonical source identity.

Typography/rendering policy for generated rich views:
  renderer controls prose wrapping while stable source IDs remain bound;
  canonical IDs use monospace styling;
  headings expose hierarchy without duplicating canonical definitions;
  restrained callouts distinguish PRINCIPLE, HARD_RULE, BOUNDARY and STATUS;
  repetitive references may render as tables where this improves scanning;
  source-bound diagrams prefer vector rendering where feasible; Mermaid or equivalent source syntax MAY be used;
  plain-text canonical source may retain review-oriented line wrapping and rich views need not inherit it.

Diagram policy:
  use diagrams when topology, process, causal path, state transition or drill-down relation becomes clearer;
  each DiagramProjectionSpec binds source anchors, represented nodes/edges, omitted detail, abstraction level, version
  and provenance; shortened labels retain inspectable canonical identifiers in metadata/caption/reference panel.

Qualification-preservation rule:
  A shorter reader statement must remain semantically entailed by its source and preserve material scope,
  conditionality, uncertainty, authority, temporality, rights, exceptions and applicability.

Example:
  Source: "Consent is required where the applicable rules require it."
  Invalid projection: "Consent is always required."
  Result: FAIL — semantic strengthening.

Generated diagrams bind source anchors, represented nodes/edges, omitted detail, abstraction level, version and
provenance. A diagram cannot silently become a competing normative system model.

DESIGNATED COMPLEX-FLOW COVERAGE

A presentation/documentation build claiming complete designated-flow visualization enumerates every flow marked
DIAGRAM_REQUIRED through the existing PresentationBuildManifest and source-bound DiagramProjectionSpec records.
For every designated flow, the build resolves:
  flow_anchor;
  source_roots;
  DiagramProjectionSpec_ref;
  represented_nodes_and_edges;
  mandatory_gate_coverage;
  uncertainty_and_UNKNOWN_coverage;
  failure_and_fallback_coverage;
  abstraction_and_omission_disclosure;
  renderer_profile;
  projection_validation_result.

No DiagramCoverageManifest schema is introduced. Missing or stale coverage prevents a complete designated-flow
visualization result. Visual simplification cannot remove a mandatory hard gate, authority boundary,
uncertainty/UNKNOWN branch or safety-relevant fallback/handoff.

Hand editing generated output creates a derivative/noncanonical view. Canonical edits occur only through governed
H/S/T/THA/A
source change followed by regeneration.

PRESENTATION FUNCTION CONTRACTS

render_reader_view(
  source_suite_refs,
  reader_view_definition_ref,
  presentation_build_manifest_ref,
  assurance_context
) -> Result<PresentationProjectionReceipt, PresentationRenderDisposition>

render_diagram_projection(
  diagram_projection_spec_ref,
  renderer_registry_entry_ref,
  source_roots,
  assurance_context
) -> Result<PresentationProjectionReceipt, DiagramRenderDisposition>

resolve_first_use_reference(
  term_or_identifier,
  source_anchor,
  registry_and_dictionary_roots,
  context
) -> Result<ReferencePanel, FirstUseResolutionDisposition>

Preconditions:
  Inputs bind exact source roots, stable anchors, DesignEpoch and a current renderer/profile where applicable.
Postconditions:
  Outputs preserve canonical identity, material qualifiers, uncertainty, authority and status. A diagram declares
  represented and omitted semantics; first-use resolution returns exactly one canonical target or an explicit
  AMBIGUOUS/UNKNOWN/BLOCKED result.
Failure:
  Missing source anchors, unresolved aliases, semantic strengthening/weakening, stale renderer qualification or omitted
  mandatory gates prevents PASS.

PresentationRenderDisposition = PASS | FAIL | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN | BLOCKED | STALE | MALFORMED.
DiagramRenderDisposition = PASS | FAIL | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN | BLOCKED | STALE | UNSUPPORTED | MALFORMED.
FirstUseResolutionDisposition = RESOLVED | AMBIGUOUS | UNKNOWN | BLOCKED | STALE | MALFORMED.

SPECIFICATION COMPILER

@SC|SpecificationCompilationOutput|-|work_packages:Set<AIDevelopment.BoundedWorkPackage>,receipt:SpecificationCompilerReceipt

CompileSpecification(
  source_roots,
  design_epoch_candidate,
  registry_roots,
  garden_skill_manifest
) -> Result<SpecificationCompilationOutput, SpecificationCompilerReceipt>

Preconditions:
  source roots, registry roots and GardenSkillManifest are pinned;
  all advertised H/S/T/THA/A, SchemaID, REG, invariant, test and proof references are resolvable;
  compiler profile satisfies the CPL diversity floor and common-mode defenses.

Postconditions:
  every successful result contains both the complete package set and its PASS SpecificationCompilerReceipt;
  every emitted package is closed over required contracts/dependencies/obligations and has an explicit write scope;
  unresolved or ambiguous references yield a non-PASS SpecificationCompilerReceipt and no admissible work package.

GARDEN_CHECK

garden_check(
  merge_candidate,
  bounded_work_package,
  garden_skill_manifest,
  execution_and_role_bindings
) -> CIConformancePipelineReceipt

The pipeline checks freshness; reference/dependency closure; write scope; parse/type/effect/CIR; generated-code
freshness; required tests; proof/conformance/safety/authority; supply-chain provenance; SBOM/license/IP/vulnerability;
reproducibility; privacy/secrets/egress; independence; provenance; and exact-review binding. Only overall PASS admits
merge. Overrides cannot relabel another state as PASS.

EXACT REVIEW / TOCTOU

The adversarial reviewer receives the canonical contract plus raw diff, not the implementer's explanation as the
authoritative review input. ExactReviewBinding binds base commit, diff, resulting tree and package hashes. All hashes
are verified immediately before commit. Any change invalidates review and reruns the affected complete pipeline.

BOUNDED REPAIR AND POST-MERGE VALIDATION

Repair cycles bind maximum attempts, resource/time budgets, progress/oscillation detection and terminal dispositions
through TerminationContract. GTF validates the actual committed tree. A post-merge failure makes the certificate STALE,
blocks deployment and triggers the declared rollback/remediation/escalation path. Merge is never deployment authority.

DETERMINISTIC QUALIFICATION

An artifact or fragment is GENERATED for origin/elision purposes only when MachineProjectionReceipt establishes a
total deterministic projection of canonical source semantics with every DEV-AI-026 pin, content-addressed outputs and
PASS projection-totality and declared-equivalence results. AI/tool authorship and a legacy GENERATED_* catalogue class
are insufficient.

Source, registry, DesignEpoch, environment, toolchain, generator/profile, comparator or qualifying-input change makes
the projection STALE and requires regeneration/requalification. Byte differences are permitted only when the declared
CIR_EQUIVALENT or SEMANTIC_EQUIVALENT comparator verifies them within its contract.

PASS and ArtifactOriginClass are derived outcomes, not independently assignable claims. GENERATED requires nonempty,
fresh pins/evidence; PASS totality, hermeticity, hidden-input audit, replay and criterion-specific comparison; and a
registered comparator profile. For BYTE_IDENTICAL, exact payload bytes and length are compared; content hashes address
the payload but do not replace exact comparison unless the declared collision assumption is accepted.

CANONICAL BYTE-REPRODUCTION BOUNDARY

For one fixed InputRoot, CanonicalGenerationProfile, canonical generator binary, HermeticExecutionReceipt and
CanonicalSerializationProfile, every accepted BYTE_IDENTICAL replay must return the same canonical bytes or non-PASS.
AI agents may invoke or independently verify that generator. Arbitrary AI synthesis is outside this theorem and remains
SOURCE/MIXED. Diverse independent verifiers are preserved; they need not synthesize identical implementations.

ELISION AND DERIVED VIEW

Only BYTE_IDENTICAL-qualified GENERATED expansion with a PASS ElisionSafetyCertificate may be omitted from
authoritative H/S/T/THA/A. Unique semantics, rights, invariants,
authority, manually curated content, stochastic content and mixed fragments remain source. A hand-edited generated
expansion becomes MIXED/STALE. DERIVED_VIEW material is visibly non-authoritative and records its material-use reason.

INVARIANT REFERENCES
  DEV-AI-001..DEV-AI-034; existing DEV-001..DEV-003 and CPL-001..CPL-008 remain additive and controlling.

CONFORMANCE TEST REFERENCES
  TEST-DEV-AI-001..TEST-DEV-AI-025. Actual execution results remain Machine Package receipts and are PENDING.

FUNCTIONS

@SC|FormalizationOutput|-|requirement_instances:Set<RequirementInstance>,receipt:FormalizationReceipt

IngestNormativeInstrument(source, acquisition_context, access_authority)
  -> Result<ExternalNormativeInstrument, GardenConformanceDisposition>

DecomposeAndFormalize(instrument_ref, profile_ref)
  -> FormalizationOutput

The total output always contains the decomposed requirement set and its FormalizationReceipt. COMPLETE or PARTIAL is
admissible for subsequent mapping only when every used requirement is at least PARTIALLY_FORMALIZED and all declared
human-review obligations are satisfied. HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED, AMBIGUOUS, UNKNOWN, BLOCKED, STALE or MALFORMED blocks
automated verification of the affected requirement; an empty or partial set never hides the receipt or missing scope.

AssessInstrumentApplicability(instrument_ref, target_binding, context)
  -> InstrumentApplicabilityProfile

AssessAgainstNormativeInstrument(
  target_binding,
  instrument_refs,
  context,
  evidence_refs,
  assurance_profile
) -> GardenConformanceAssessmentReceipt

PrepareCertificationEvidence(assessment_receipt_ref, requested_scope, external_authority_ref)
  -> Result<EvidenceRef, GardenConformanceDisposition>

RecordExternalCertification(external_certificate, authority_and_authenticity_evidence, target_snapshot)
  -> ExternalCertificationBinding

The legacy name certify_against_standard, if exposed for compatibility, is an alias for the internal assessment above
and cannot create ExternalCertificationBinding or official certification. PrepareCertificationEvidence only packages
evidence for an external authority; RecordExternalCertification only authenticates and records that authority's act.

RESULT-CONSTRUCTOR INVARIANTS

FormalizationReceipt.result is derived from per_requirement_status, coverage, semantic-preservation, ambiguity and
human-review closure. InstrumentApplicabilityProfile.result is derived from its complete per-requirement map and exact
scope. UNKNOWN/INCONCLUSIVE/BLOCKED/STALE/MALFORMED input cannot be reassigned to a stronger result.

FormalizationOutput.requirement_instances has exactly the same requirement identity set as
FormalizationReceipt.requirement_refs, and each RequirementInstance.formalization_status equals the receipt's
per_requirement_status value at the receipt's instrument-source, knowledge and DesignEpoch snapshot. Later drift never
rewrites that historical result; it activates invalidators and makes dependent current use STALE until reassessed.
Missing, duplicate or mismatched membership/status is MALFORMED. InstrumentApplicabilityProfile.result is
NOT_APPLICABLE iff every in-scope requirement is NOT_APPLICABLE; any UNKNOWN/INCONCLUSIVE/BLOCKED/STALE/MALFORMED
member propagates a non-stronger aggregate and can never be hidden as NOT_APPLICABLE.

GardenConformanceAssessmentReceipt.per_requirement_results has exactly one entry for every requirement in the bound
applicability profiles, and each value equals the corresponding NormativeRequirementBinding.status. Every forward
control reference in a NormativeRequirementBinding is covered by its reverse_control_to_requirement_index_refs and
resolves back to that binding and RequirementInstance. Missing, duplicate, mismatched or one-way traceability blocks
CONFORMANT. These equalities apply at the receipt's target/knowledge/DesignEpoch/time snapshot; later drift preserves
history and invalidates current use rather than rewriting the receipt.

GardenConformanceAssessmentReceipt.certification_workflow_status is derived from the external workflow evidence.
NOT_SOUGHT/EVIDENCE_READY/SUBMITTED/UNDER_EXTERNAL_REVIEW require no external binding; DECIDED requires a fresh
external_certification_binding_ref. Workflow state is not an external certification status.

CONFORMANT is derived only when the instrument/applicability/scope roots are fresh, every applicable mandatory
requirement is SATISFIED, all mandatory proof/evidence/test/cross-check obligations PASS, conflicts are resolved by an
authorized strategy and no UNKNOWN/BLOCKED/STALE mandatory item remains. A remediation plan is not satisfaction.

ExternalCertificationStatus.VERIFIED_CURRENT is derived only when authenticity_verification_result = VERIFIED,
certifier authority/accreditation evidence is current, the certificate signature/hash and certified target/scope match,
the validity interval is current, referenced instruments are current and no suspension, revocation, supersession,
scope, environment, evidence, target or DesignEpoch invalidator applies. Otherwise the applicable non-current or
unknown status is retained. RecordExternalCertification records an external act; it cannot issue that act.

NormativeConflictResolutionRecord.status = RESOLVED only when selected_strategy != UNRESOLVED, unresolved_residuals is
empty and every authority/scope prerequisite is current. HUMAN_AUTHORITY_DECISION or AUTHORIZED_WAIVER_OR_EXCEPTION
requires a present, authentic and scope-authorized authorized_decision_or_waiver_ref; otherwise status is UNRESOLVED or
BLOCKED. A status field cannot override these derived conditions.

OperationalAdmissibilityResult comes from the separately applicable AAP/policy/safety/authority gate. Official
certification comes only from a current authenticated ExternalCertificationBinding. Neither is inferred from Garden's
assessment result, and an internal Garden ConformanceCertificate is not an external certificate.

  STD-001..STD-016; existing CMP-001..CMP-005 remain active and additive.

CONFORMANCE TEST REFERENCES
  TEST-STD-001..TEST-STD-010. Execution and external-certification evidence remain receipt-bound and PENDING.

FUNCTION CONTRACTS

import_engineering_design(
  source,
  acquisition_context,
  access_authority,
  import_profile,
  assurance_profile
) -> Result<EngineeringDesignImportReceipt, EngineeringImportDisposition>

Preconditions:
  Source acquisition and processing are authorized under EngineeringDesignSourceAccessPolicy; input bytes/objects are
  isolated as untrusted; resource/termination/security limits exist.
Postconditions:
  Returns exactly one receipt binding the immutable source root, format version, chunk/reference closure, geometry
  imports, semantic map, loss/ambiguity/unsupported findings, toolchain and provenance. COMPLETE is derivable only when
  all required source spans/dependencies and required semantic classes are covered without unresolved mandatory loss.
Failure:
  Partial or failed import remains auditable and cannot be passed to assessment as complete. No source mutation,
  ownership claim, permission, correctness, safety or certification is created.

assess_engineering_design(
  import_receipt_ref,
  assessment_scope,
  objective_and_requirement_refs,
  context,
  assurance_profile
) -> EngineeringDesignAssessment

Preconditions:
  The receipt and all used source/semantic-map/dependency roots are fresh for the declared scope. Any partial import is
  explicitly admitted with a non-stronger assessment ceiling and unresolved obligations.
Postconditions:
  Emits a total typed assessment, safety-case reference, per-domain findings, gaps, uncertainty, counterevidence and
  aggregate result. ACCEPTABLE/CONDITIONALLY_ACCEPTABLE never follows from missing mandatory evidence or UNKNOWN safety.
Failure:
  Inapplicable/unsupported checks remain NOT_APPLICABLE/UNKNOWN with rationale rather than silently disappearing.

improve_engineering_design(
  assessment_ref,
  improvement_objective,
  search_and_termination_contract,
  resource_budget,
  assurance_profile
) -> EngineeringImprovementCycleResult

Preconditions:
  Baseline design, assessment, authority and source-access constraints are current. Search is bounded. Current design
  and the evolving PhysicalBaselineTrajectory for DO_NOTHING are mandatory comparators where applicable.
Postconditions:
  Every candidate has an exact delta, affected closure, predicted tradeoffs, validation plan and validation receipt or
  explicit non-execution reason. Acceptance requires ordinary hard gates; the function cannot deploy or certify.
Failure:
  Exhaustion, oscillation, lack of evidence or non-dominance produces the applicable non-success result or DO_NOTHING;
  it is never relabeled as verified improvement.

map_external_engineering_format(
  source_artifact_ref,
  format_version_identifier_ref,
  adapter_profile_ref,
  mapping_scope,
  assurance_profile
) -> Result<EngineeringDesignSemanticMap, EngineeringSemanticMappingDisposition>

validate_engineering_semantic_mapping(
  semantic_map_ref,
  source_root,
  validation_profile,
  assurance_profile
) -> EngineeringSemanticMappingValidationResult

Preconditions:
  Format and adapter entries are exact, current and authorized; unsupported/extension constructs are explicit.
Postconditions:
  Mapping binds bidirectional source spans and typed targets, records loss/ambiguity and returns coverage plus
  round-trip/differential results where claimed. Validation never upgrades PARTIAL, UNKNOWN or unsupported mapping to
  exact equivalence.
Failure:
  Unknown version, unqualified adapter, unresolved reference, namespace collision, unit/frame mismatch or stale
  specification returns a typed non-success result and preserves source bytes.

EngineeringSemanticMappingDisposition =
  EXACT | APPROXIMATE | PARTIAL | UNSUPPORTED | AMBIGUOUS | CONFLICT | UNKNOWN | BLOCKED | STALE | MALFORMED.

@SC|EngineeringSemanticMappingValidationResult|-|semantic_map_ref,source_and_adapter_roots,per_construct_coverage_and_status,reverse_traceability_result,round_trip_and_differential_results,loss_ambiguity_conflict_and_unknown_findings,aggregate_result:EngineeringSemanticMappingDisposition,invalidators,provenance

RESULT-CONSTRUCTOR RULES

EngineeringDesignImportReceipt.result is derived from access, format identification, source-reference closure, required
chunk parsing, geometry/semantic coverage, loss, ambiguity, security and resource outcomes. An asserted result field
cannot override its constituent findings.

EngineeringDesignAssessment.aggregate_result is the greatest non-stronger result consistent with every mandatory
finding and safety-case obligation. UNSAFE, UNKNOWN or BLOCKED mandatory safety evidence cannot aggregate to ACCEPTABLE.

EngineeringDesignValidationReceipt.aggregate_result is derived from the complete per-obligation map for the exact
candidate and baseline roots. Later design, environment, model, requirement, format, evidence or DesignEpoch drift
preserves historical receipt bytes but makes current reuse STALE.

EngineeringImprovementCycleResult.result = ACCEPTED/PARTIALLY_ACCEPTED only when the exact selected delta has the
required validation, rights/access, proof, safety, authority and monitoring closure. An unvalidated benefit projection
is not independent Evidence and cannot satisfy its own acceptance criterion.

  Adds ordered degradation states, entry/exit/resource thresholds, sheddable/non-sheddable functions,

  invalidators. Only pre-ratified transitions are permitted; resource restoration alone does not restore authority.

  Adds timescale, temporal horizon, operating regime, environment, task/domain, state-space region, hardware/runtime
  context, authority ceiling, validity evidence, promotion conditions and invalidators. Out-of-domain/horizon use
  enters ApprovalPlan + DesignAuthorityDecision + GTF/DesignEpoch admission; architecture-changing promotion
  remains DesignEpoch-governed.

MINIMUM-RISK RULE
Minimum-risk is a profile-selected disposition, not a universal controller type. The active safety case may select
bounded continuation, isolation, handoff, safe hold, controlled shutdown or another qualified domain response.
Universal blind-halt semantics are forbidden.

STATUS AND PRECEDENCE
  Status: RATIFIED ADDITIVE human-source hardening profile.
  GSL: v45.1 unchanged; no new Core Object, primitive, top-level engine or architecture category.
  Existing semantics preserved: HSA, K0P/Safety, Process/GTF, AAP, Knowledge/Evidence, Proof, Dependency, Compliance,
  GSL-PHYSICS, Bridge, Compile, Security, Law/Governance and domain safety cases remain authoritative owners.
  Machine proof, empirical qualification, fallback-controller qualification and implementation evidence: PENDING.

@SC|SafeControlHandoffReceipt|SCHEMA-8E127067C8|receipt_id,containment_contract_ref:Ref<CriticalContinuityContainmentContract>,detected_event_and_evidence_refs,suspect_closure_root,completed_revocations_and_enforcement_attestations,fenced_or_rejected_command_refs,fallback_controller_identity_version_and_qualification_root,independence_evidence_and_result,transferred_state_root_interface_and_integrity_checks,physical_continuity_envelope_and_observed_state,shutdown_vs_continuity_decision_and_safety_case_ref,monitoring_unknowns_failures_and_escalations,current_phase:CriticalContinuityPhase,result:ContinuityHandoffDisposition,authority_design_epoch_environment_time_and_provenance

contain_critical_continuity(
  event_ref,
  containment_contract_ref,
  current_physical_state,
  assurance_profile
) -> SafeControlHandoffReceipt

Required priority:
  AGENCY/AUTHORITY REVOCATION → INDEPENDENT SAFE CONTROL HANDOFF → MINIMUM SAFE CONTINUITY → QUALIFIED RECOVERY.
The function first revokes/fences suspect authority and actuation capability at independent enforcement points. It then
uses only a separately qualified fallback controller whose current execution does not depend on the suspect AI's
mutable cognition, goals, credentials or authority. Minimum continuity and shutdown are compared using the exact domain
safety case. Determinism alone, shared training lineage, nominally separate credentials or majority agreement cannot
establish safety/independence. Recovery requires root-cause closure, repair, revalidation and fresh authorization.

project_physical_do_nothing(
  current_state,
  system_context,
  evaluation_horizon,
  model_and_uncertainty_profile,
  monitoring_profile
) -> PhysicalBaselineTrajectory

DO_NOTHING means the evaluated decision maker initiates no new intervention; endogenous/environmental dynamics continue.
The result cannot be VALID without the required trajectory, horizon, uncertainty and monitoring/handoff semantics for
the domain/risk profile. A frozen-state/zero-derivative baseline is admitted only when independently justified by the
physical model and evidence, never by the token DO_NOTHING itself.

  quantum_model_ref,
  classical_model_ref,

) -> QuantumClassicalCorrespondenceReceipt

No universal Lindblad/GKSL requirement exists. A GKSL/Lindblad model is used only when its declared open-system,
complete-positivity, trace-preservation and Markovian/semigroup approximations are justified for the target regime.
Out-of-regime non-Markovian, strong-coupling, structured-environment, short-time or other cases activate the required
higher-fidelity model or return a non-stronger result.

@SC|CounterfactualInfluenceProvenance|SCHEMA-C5A2F190CF|provenance_id,counterfactual_hypothesis_or_prediction_ref,decision_trace_ref,decision_maker_authority_context_and_time,intervention_ref_and_assignment_mechanism,target_population_system_and_selection_process,pre_intervention_state_and_baseline_refs,observation_refs:NonEmptySet<Ref<ObservationRecord>>,evidence_refs:Set<EvidenceRef>,per_evidence_production_mode:Map<EvidenceRef,EvidenceProductionMode>,causal_ancestry_and_temporal_order,confounder_selection_feedback_shared_source_and_dependence_graph,prediction_to_evidence_nonindependence_edges,independent_control_comparator_or_external_grounding_refs,claim_and_knowledge_derivation_refs,limitations_invalidators_and_provenance

record_counterfactual_influence(
  counterfactual_ref,
  decision_trace_ref,
  intervention_ref,
  observation_refs,
  causal_context
) -> CounterfactualInfluenceProvenance

COUNTERFACTUAL-INFLUENCE INTEGRITY BINDING

For assurance profiles requiring tamper-evident provenance, a canonical content root is derived over the applicable
CounterfactualInfluenceProvenance content, referenced decision/intervention/observation identities and declared
dependence edges. The root is bound through an accepted registered provenance mechanism using the existing
ExternalProvenanceBinding.

The binding covers, as applicable:
  exact CounterfactualInfluenceProvenance content root;
  source observation identities/content roots;
  decision and intervention identities;
  temporal/causal ordering information;
  evidence-production classifications;
  dependence/non-independence edges;
  independent-control or external-grounding references;
  scheme/version/trust roots;
  signer/attestation identity;
  validation environment;
  invalidators.

No CounterfactualTaint artifact is introduced. Counterfactual influence is represented by typed provenance and
dependence, not by a contaminated/clean ontology. Integrity of a provenance binding does not prove truth, independence,
causality, authority or admissibility.

Subsequent genuine observations remain empirical. Their production mode is observational, interventional, endogenous,
mixed or unknown. The counterfactual/prediction may explain why evidence was produced but cannot count as independent
support for itself. Dependence, confounding, selection and feedback prevent false independence promotion.

valid scoped exception. Reaching a target date does not create eligibility. Historical identity, provenance and required
retention survive removal from active use.

InvariantRefs: LIFE-DEP-001..003; DOC-OWN-001..012; existing DLC-*, DEP-* and ARCH-EVO-* remain controlling.
TestRefs: TEST-DEP-001..003.

GARDEN LANGUAGE SERVER PROFILE — DETAILED TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION
SemanticAnchor: [T-LSP]

Owners: Capability.Platform.Development and Engine.Compile over the pinned GardenCore.Semantics.GSL and registry roots.

LSPSemanticResolutionDisposition =
  RESOLVED | PARTIAL | AMBIGUOUS | UNKNOWN | BLOCKED | STALE | UNSUPPORTED | TIMEOUT | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | MALFORMED.

@SC|GardenLanguageServerProfile|SCHEMA-C0FB597E8F|profile_id,gsl_grammar_schema_registry_and_design_epoch_roots,supported_document_and_protocol_versions,parser_recovery_and_incremental_analysis_policy,qualified_identifier_alias_type_effect_scope_and_authority_resolution,anchor_reference_dependency_and_backlink_resolution,diagnostics_completion_hover_navigation_reference_and_rename_capabilities,formatting_code_action_and_edit_safety_policy,proof_conformance_compile_and_registry_action_bindings,workspace_trust_privacy_secret_network_and_plugin_policy,cancellation_progress_resource_latency_and_degraded_mode_contracts,cache_invalidation_version_negotiation_and_telemetry_policy,implementation_root_qualification_and_conformance_refs,invalidators,provenance

resolve_lsp_semantics(
  source_workspace_snapshot,
  cursor_or_range,
  language_server_profile_ref,
  context
) -> Result<LSPSemanticResolution, LSPSemanticResolutionDisposition>

@SC|LSPSemanticResolution|-|source_location_and_snapshot_root,parsed_syntax_and_recovery_findings,resolved_or_candidate_canonical_identifiers,type_effect_scope_authority_and_reference_results,diagnostics_and_safe_edit_candidates,dependency_and_invalidation_refs,result:LSPSemanticResolutionDisposition,provenance

The server preserves ambiguity and stale state. Completion, quick-fix, formatting or navigation output is advisory and
cannot compile, prove, authorize, merge, deploy or silently change canonical meaning.

PROOF / TEST SEMANTIC NAVIGATION

The existing diagnostics/navigation and proof/conformance action bindings collectively support source-bound discovery
of the canonical definition, valid references, owner, SchemaID where applicable, invariant bindings, conformance-test
bindings, proof-obligation bindings, existing proof/qualification receipt references, dependency/invalidation closure
and current DesignEpoch/source roots.

A CodeLens-style presentation may be used. If the client/protocol lacks CodeLens, equivalent hover,
related-information or navigation output is valid. Every displayed proof/test relationship binds the exact current
source and registry roots. A stale cached relationship is recomputed before it is presented as current.

A code action may qualify a bare alias automatically only when resolution has exactly one valid current canonical
target. Otherwise the result remains AMBIGUOUS and no automatic semantic edit is performed.

STDMappingFixtureDisposition =

instantiate_std_mapping_fixture(

BRIDGE / TYPED REPRESENTATION & OPERATOR ALGEBRA — DETAILED TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION
SemanticAnchor: [T-BRIDGE]

STATUS
  RATIFIED — ADDITIVE.
  Basis: current registered presentation contract.
  GSL: v45.1 unchanged.
  Architecture delta: adds Algebra.Bridge inside the existing Universal Algebras category.
  No new top-level category or engine.

PURPOSE
Bridge provides typed correspondence between Garden semantic objects/processes and computational vector/matrix/tensor/
operator representations. It supports efficient analysis, compilation and hardware realization while preserving:
  representation != semantic identity;
  approximate/lossy representation != exactness;
  similarity != truth;
  matrix reachability != causality or authoritative dependency closure;
  neural computation receipt != evidence of truth;
  fixed-point existence != convergence != stability != termination;
  optimization != admissibility.

CANONICAL LAYERS
  Semantic State
    → Computational Representation
    → Hardware / Execution Realization.

The ordinary Process Algebra states what semantic transformation is intended. Bridge states what mathematical/operator
representation may validly implement or analyze that transformation. Compile/Runtime determines the actual machine path.

  error_bound: Option<ErrorBound>
  method_ref: Option<Ref<TransformFunction>>
  evidence_refs: List<Ref<Evidence>>
  proof_refs: List<Ref<ProofTrace>>
  validity_domain: Scope
  provenance: Provenance
}

EXACT CORRESPONDENCE / COMMUTING DIAGRAM

For semantic operation O_S: T → U and represented operator O_R: R_T → R_U:

  decode_U(O_R(encode_T(x))) ≡ O_S(x)

for all x inside the declared validity domain and preserved semantic closure.

For a pure representation round trip:

  decode(encode(x)) ≡ x.

Approximate execution replaces ≡ with an explicitly bounded approximation relation and records LostProperties where
applicable. EXACT is admitted only when ProjectionCorrespondenceProof covers the composed encode/operate/decode path.

CANONICAL RELATION-OPERATOR PROJECTIONS
dependsOn, supports, contradicts, causes, governs and other relations may expose context-bound operator projections.
Every projection is context/projection/proof bound. Matrix reachability does not become causality, semantic equivalence
or authoritative dependency closure.

PROVEN_NONTERMINATING blocks/rejects a job only when the enclosing contract requires termination. A declared persistent
service instead follows its liveness/productivity/stoppability/per-step resource obligations.

UPWARD EPISTEMIC PATH

  hardware / execution result
    → RepresentationResult
    → RepresentationProjectionReceipt / OptimizationReceipt where applicable
    → typed semantic result
    → applicable epistemic ingress / validation
    → Observation | ClaimCandidate | computational receipt
    → Evidence / Knowledge only through their existing validation and promotion paths.

RECURSIVE-ANALYSIS METHOD RULE
Garden may combine spectral/operator analysis, ranking functions, well-founded measures, resource recurrences, abstract
interpretation, model checking, simulation, symbolic methods and other valid methods. Multiple independent methods are
required only where the applicable assurance profile requires them or where one method remains
inconclusive/approximate/non-decisive. A complete valid proof may discharge the obligation alone where the applicable
assurance policy permits.

evaluate_physical_telemetry_order(
  measurements: List<Measurement<T>>,
  temporal_bindings: Map<MeasurementRef, TelemetryTemporalBinding>,

  Authority: read/validation authority for the declared telemetry scope
  Failures: malformed_clock_binding | ambiguous_order | bound_exceeded | resource_unknown | stale_profile

assimilate_physical_telemetry(
  twin: PhysicalTwin,
  measurements: List<Measurement<T>>,
  profile: DataAssimilationProfile,

  Effects: updates_twin_projection; writes_audit; may_activate_prevalidated_fallback;
           physical_effect_only_if_separately_authorized
  Authority: profile-bound twin-update authority; actuation remains separately safety/authority gated
  Failures: non_identifiable | non_observable | order_unknown | calibration_invalid | model_invalid |
            constraint_breach | timeout | resource_unknown | stale | blocked

The function pair preserves immutable raw telemetry and historical assimilation results. Latest arrival is never
silently equated with latest physical state. Late correction never rewrites past evidence in place; it creates a linked
successor state and invalidates affected dependents. A deterministic ordering algorithm is not safety evidence.

  legacy ratification identifiers PHY-027..030 -> canonical PHYS-027..030.
The mapping is exact and profile-scoped. Bare PHY-* is retired for certified machine-facing artifacts.

2. HIERARCHY AND FIVE-FILE INTEGRATION

GARDEN
  Human Sovereignty Core: UNCHANGED
