  Universal Algebras
    Algebra.Bridge: CPI-P06 specialisation
  Meta-engines
    Engine.Compile: CPI-P01, CPI-P03
    Engine.Reason: CPI-P02, CPI-P05, CPI-P09
    Engine.Research: CPI-P07
    Engine.Orchestrate / AAP: CPI-P11, CPI-P13
    Engine.Evolve: CPI-P12
    Engine.Compliance: CPI-P14
    Engine.Compare / Engine.Proof / Engine.Dependency / Engine.Transition:
      reused cooperating owners, not duplicated
  Shared Capabilities
    Capability.Knowledge.Curation / GSL-KR profile: CPI-P04
    Capability.Knowledge + native observation interfaces: CPI-P08
    Capability.Compute.Runtime: CPI-P10
    Development, EventService, Security, Safety, Privacy, Infrastructure and
    Authority.Law: existing cooperating owners
  Domain / Theory / Context systems: existing typed contracts and operating scopes
  Profiles / Reference Implementations: register selected CPI-P01..CPI-P14 only after admission
  Instances / Deployments: no activation granted

Identifiers CPI-Pxx and CPI:: records below are patch-local, not new Core Objects or Component 51. The reviewed tree has no operative CASI declaration; this patch therefore requires no CASI migration. Where another accepted branch already contains CASI, reconcile these profiles with it rather than duplicating adapters or claiming that one version is automatically authoritative.

Five-file ownership of the proposed insertions:
HUMAN [H-CPI]: concise purpose, user-visible limits, no-global-certification wording, rights boundary; refer to existing [H-AI-DEV].
SYSTEM [S-CPI]: interaction and authority flow; reference [S-AI-DEV], [S-KNOWLEDGE], [S-EVOLVE], [S-COMPILE], [S-PROFILES].
TECHNICAL [T-CPI]: algorithmic obligations, profile contracts, qualification and failure semantics; reuse the existing anchors listed for each profile.
THEORIES [RA-CPI]: explanatory research placement and prior-work mapping only; CPI is an implementation profile bundle, not a newly ratified foundational theory. Preserve [RA-GSL-KR], [RA-GSL-AR], [RA-TRAIN], [RA-GAIA-TITANIUM], [RA-GAIA-DIAMOND].
ANNEXURE [A-CPI]: sole normative home of new profile records, local contract definitions, invariant/test catalogue, and future global ID bindings. Other files reference these definitions rather than duplicating them.

No existing architecture owner, invariant, test, definition, or rights statement is deleted. A merge must identify the existing schema corresponding to each field group; extend or reuse it where possible. Only genuinely missing record types receive newly allocated global SchemaIDs after collision checks. Until that binding passes, the draft is not compiler-admissible.

3. RECURSIVE CONSTRUCTION CONTRACT AND FAILURE SEMANTICS

3.1 Qualify three different kinds of work
DERIVATION: a fixed semantic source and qualified deterministic projection determine the output. Generate it with exact declared pins.
DESIGN: several permitted implementations satisfy the contract. Search, compare, retain the choice and its qualification; do not claim the chosen details were forced by the source.
DISCOVERY: a material fact, preference, domain assumption, semantic choice, safety bound, or rights basis is missing. Obtain evidence, an authorised decision, or a limited fallback. Writing more text cannot substitute for the missing input.

3.2 Loop
Pinned intent/source -> typed obligation bundle -> bounded decomposition -> candidate generation -> independent checking/counterexamples -> composition review -> exact candidate admission -> separate deployment authorisation -> observation -> dependency-aware revalidation.

At every cycle, write-scope, resources, freshness, privacy, evidence status, and existing authority constraints persist. A child is not allowed to refresh an exhausted budget. The existing automatic repair-depth limit of three remains unchanged where its profile applies. Novel research can be proposed as a new bounded campaign; it cannot silently bypass the previous campaign's rejection.

3.3 What can be established
For a fixed seed that satisfies property P under assumptions A, if every accepted transformation is checked to preserve P and A, induction gives preservation of P along the accepted finite chain. That says nothing by itself about whether P fully expresses the intended purpose, whether A matches the environment, or whether the search finds a passing candidate.

For a finite candidate set, a complete terminating checker, fair enumeration, adequate resources, and a valid member in that set, exhaustive search eventually finds a valid member. Practical AI search often lacks one or more of these assumptions. Probabilistic generation, correlated tests, changing environments, or infeasible contracts cannot be declared convergent merely because the loop repeats. [R01, R18]

3.4 Explicit outcomes
Continue only under existing admitted transition rules. Preserve SUCCESS_WITH_SCOPE, REJECTED, INCONCLUSIVE, RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, STALE, ESCALATED, and HALTED through the relevant canonical result type; these descriptions are not replacements for existing enums. No stable error count, consensus, or filled-out record may substitute for the required evidence.

3.5 Where construction can fail
Wrong source-to-meaning mapping; an unmeasured world variable; a mistaken trusted premise; generator and reviewer sharing one error; missing composition assumptions; hidden state or concurrency; adversarial evidence; stale permissions or models; unaffordable validation; unresolved legal rights; irreversible effects; or search that makes no progress. Existing mechanisms can detect or contain some such cases. Their existence is not a proof that every case is detectable, avoidable, or repairable.

4. SUBORDINATE PROFILE SPECIFICATIONS

All methods below are newly drafted requirements. Conceptual sources do not grant code-import permission. Each profile starts DISABLED / UNQUALIFIED until the declared source, schema, rights, implementation, evaluation and integration obligations pass.

CPI-P01 — Contract-Directed Recursive Construction
Primary owner: Engine.Compile
Cooperating owners: Capability.Platform.Development, Engine.Reason, Engine.Proof, Engine.Evolve
Existing anchors: [T-AI-DEV] [T-IMPLEMENT]
Conceptual references: R01

Delta and non-duplication: Specialise existing AI-development work packages with a semantic-choice ledger, measurable obligations, counterexample retention, and a finite construction frontier. This is not a replacement for the existing specification compiler or an additional compiler engine.

Required operating method: Pin the source, DesignEpoch, registries, trusted checkers, and execution boundary. Decompose a requirement into packages whose inputs, outputs, assumptions, effects, and acceptance conditions are explicit. Classify each missing detail as mechanically derived, implementation choice, unresolved interpretation, empirical unknown, rights question, or required human preference. Only the first two may be filled without a new source decision, and implementation choices must remain inside the permitted contract. Generate candidate implementations; compare them with an independently maintained semantic oracle or specification; retain counterexamples; retry only within the inherited TerminationContract. Child packages share the parent resource ledger. Do not let decomposition reset attempts or invent a default for an unresolved semantic choice. A successful child does not admit its parent until composition and integration obligations pass.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  BuildCandidate(work_package_ref, CPI.ObligationBundle, parent_checkpoint_ref) -> CPI.ConstructionCandidate and an existing SpecificationCompilerReceipt or bounded non-PASS outcome. Effects: write candidate workspace and development receipts only; no canonical commit or deployment.

CPI-P02 — Context Preservation and Correction Propagation
Primary owner: Engine.Reason
Cooperating owners: Engine.Compare, Engine.Dependency, Capability.Knowledge
Existing anchors: [T-CAPABILITIES] [T-KNOWLEDGE]
Conceptual references: G06

Delta and non-duplication: Operationalise the existing prompt/interpretation/comparison records. The GAC synthesis is explanatory support, not a new universal law or an extra top-level engine.

Required operating method: Represent the requested scope, objectives, material distinctions, evidence, assumptions, exclusions, and intended deliverable before substantial work. Preserve competing interpretations where the input does not decide between them. For a material correction, classify whether it changes a value, a source, a model, a goal, or scope. Traverse explicit dependency links; prioritise re-evaluation by consequence and deadline. Mark unreviewed affected conclusions pending or stale. Reachability in a stored graph is not assumed NP-hard; rechecking the affected claims can nevertheless exhaust a budget. Seek another observation when two situations cannot be distinguished using the available evidence. Never demand exhaustive representation of irrelevant detail.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  ReviewSituation(scope_record_ref, change_set, dependency_snapshot_ref, budget_ref) -> CPI.ObligationBundle and existing ReasoningTrace / ComparisonJudgment records. Effects: derived analysis and revalidation notices only.

CPI-P03 — Qualified Procedure Learning and Compilation
Primary owner: Engine.Compile
Cooperating owners: Engine.Reason, Capability.Knowledge.Curation, Engine.Dependency, Engine.Compare
Existing anchors: [T-AI-DEV] [T-KNOWLEDGE]
Conceptual references: R02

Delta and non-duplication: Bind learning from successful work to explicit applicability and reusable execution. Reuse existing generation, proof, cache, and invalidation contracts rather than creating a second knowledge-admission system.

Required operating method: Extract a proposed method from a successful trace or independently specified solution. Keep the source task, assumptions, exceptional cases, evidence, and output semantics. Generalise only after tests or a stated proof establish the proposed scope; one successful episode is insufficient for a universal rule. Qualify a cheap applicability predicate with APPLICABLE, NOT_APPLICABLE, and UNKNOWN outcomes. Compile or implement the routine without importing external source code. Monitor each use and invalidate on dependency, environment, authority, or verifier changes. Compare fresh reasoning and reuse at matched quality and risk, accounting for discovery, qualification, checks, fallback, repair, and amortisation. Statistical qualification must retain its distributional limits. A seed does not turn unobserved external state into deterministic input.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  QualifyProcedure(candidate_ref, applicability_spec, evaluation_plan_ref) -> CPI.QualifiedProcedure or a nonqualified candidate. Effects: candidate records; publication to an active knowledge view requires normal admission.

CPI-P04 — Bounded Knowledge Retrieval and Maintenance
Primary owner: Capability.Knowledge.Curation
Cooperating owners: Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Dependency, Engine.Search, HumanCore.Privacy
Existing anchors: [T-KNOWLEDGE] [RA-GSL-KR]
Conceptual references: R02

Delta and non-duplication: Add a maintenance policy to the existing GSL-KR knowledge profile; do not replace claim semantics, duplicate the ledger, or assert that every stored item is verified truth.

Required operating method: Separate active qualified procedures, evidence, disputed claims, hypotheses, archived history, and quarantined material through existing epistemic views. Retrieve using relevance, provenance, cost, and applicable validity instead of frequency alone. Cache results only with their dependency and snapshot bindings. Propose abstraction of multiple methods only after preserving material exceptions and testing the proposed domain. Mark an archive move, supersession, correction, logical invalidation, and privacy deletion as distinct operations. Rare but high-consequence emergency knowledge must not be discarded solely for low usage. Keep change lineage subject to data-minimisation and deletion duties; an append-only design does not justify retaining every private payload forever. Query bounds return a frontier and incompleteness, not a closed-world assertion that missing facts are false.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  MaintainKnowledge(snapshot_ref, budget_ref, retention_policy_ref) -> CPI.KnowledgeMaintenancePlan plus existing revalidation records. Effects: stage maintenance; commit only through knowledge/transition gates.

CPI-P05 — Resource-Bounded Revisable Inference
Primary owner: Engine.Reason
Cooperating owners: Engine.Proof, Engine.Dependency, Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Orchestrate
Existing anchors: [T-CAPABILITIES] [T-KNOWLEDGE]
Conceptual references: R03

Delta and non-duplication: Supply a budgeted inference operating policy inside the existing Reason machinery, not a new universal confidence calculus copied from another project.

Required operating method: Order pending inferences by consequence, expected decision improvement, cost, and urgency. Record logical premises separately from source trust and empirical confidence. Preserve contradictions and competing hypotheses when resolution is unavailable. Use typed probabilistic, interval, ordinal, or proof outcomes with declared semantics; do not convert all of them into one unqualified score. A bounded search may return a provisional answer with its unresolved frontier. Every promotion must follow the existing evidence policy. Repetition by other agents does not create independent support. A high-priority instruction can remain factually false. Deeper inference is selected when expected value or a mandatory obligation justifies it; mere trace length is not quality.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  InferWithinBudget(query_ref, knowledge_snapshot_ref, budget_ref) -> existing InferenceResult and ReasoningTrace with remaining_obligations_ref. Effects: analysis only until separately admitted.

CPI-P06 — Hybrid Representation and Checked Semantic Bridges
Primary owner: Algebra.Bridge
Cooperating owners: Engine.Reason, Engine.Compile, Engine.Proof, Capability.Compute.Runtime
Existing anchors: [T-BRIDGE] [T-IMPLEMENT]
Conceptual references: R04, R05, R06, R17

Delta and non-duplication: Unify interfaces where appropriate without equating symbolic rules, graph inference, neural representations, or proofs. Existing Bridge ownership remains authoritative.

Required operating method: Specify a typed representation mapping with source and target meanings, units, frame, approximation class, permitted operations, error bounds, and round-trip or refinement obligations. Permit opaque latent state as a referenced artifact, not as an unexplained replacement for required semantic evidence. Keep a narrow checking kernel separately pinned from proposer code. Prefer independent checker implementations for critical translations where feasible; identical model prompts do not establish independence. Switch between inexpensive qualified routines and fuller deliberation based on applicability and consequence. A fast path may not skip a mandatory guard. Any approximation affects the assurance statement explicitly.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  CheckBridge(source_contract_ref, mapping_ref, target_artifact_ref, qualification_ref) -> an existing translation/proof receipt plus CPI.ObligationBundle. Effects: qualification records only.

CPI-P07 — Predictive World-Model Learning
Primary owner: Engine.Research
Cooperating owners: Engine.Reason, Capability.Knowledge, Algebra.Bridge, Engine.Compare
Existing anchors: [T-CAPABILITIES] [T-BRIDGE]
Conceptual references: R07, R08

Delta and non-duplication: Add a concrete qualification interface for learned predictive models. This does not import a neural architecture, training corpus, checkpoint, or unpublished recipe.

Required operating method: Bind state and observation representations, actions, prediction horizon, data provenance, operating distribution, uncertainty, calibration, and drift tests. Learn candidate predictive models from authorised observations or lawfully created synthetic environments. Keep actual observations distinct from imagined trajectories. Evaluate held-out dynamics and control consequences against a simple comparator. Record data leakage checks and failure regions. Updating a model invalidates dependent rollout claims and any policy qualification that relied on its old dynamics. Prediction error and model confidence remain separate. Generated scenes cannot serve as external evidence that the world behaved as predicted.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  EvaluatePredictiveModel(model_artifact_ref, CPI.PredictiveInterface, CPI.EvaluationPlan) -> model evaluation receipts and revised obligations. Effects: sandbox model trials; no autonomous physical deployment.

CPI-P08 — Sensorimotor Grounding and Active Observation
Primary owner: Capability.Knowledge
Cooperating owners: Capability.Compute.NativeInterface, Capability.Trust.Safety, Engine.Research, Algebra.Bridge
Existing anchors: [T-KNOWLEDGE] [T-BRIDGE]
Conceptual references: R09

Delta and non-duplication: Specify how embodied observations and active measurements enter the existing epistemic pipeline. No new physical kernel or replacement for TRAIN/GAIA is introduced.

Required operating method: Link observations to sensor identity, calibration, time, units, spatial frame, occlusion, and operational limits. Represent object identity hypotheses separately from raw sensor events. Propose information-gathering actions with explicit costs, permissions, and physical constraints. Where observations disagree or identity is ambiguous, reduce the scope of dependent conclusions and seek discriminating evidence when feasible. A signed observation proves a source binding, not that the sensor is calibrated or the interpretation is correct. Use sandbox or simulator work before any physical action; real experiments require the applicable pre-existing safety and authority path.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  GroundObservation(observation_ref, environment_ref, sensor_profile_ref) -> grounded claim candidates and ambiguity obligations. Effects: knowledge candidates; active observations are separate authorised ACTION proposals.

CPI-P09 — Bounded Planning and Information-Gathering
Primary owner: Engine.Reason
Cooperating owners: Engine.Research, Engine.Compare, Engine.Orchestrate, Capability.Trust.Safety
Existing anchors: [T-CAPABILITIES]
Conceptual references: R10, R11

Delta and non-duplication: Implement action/search budget selection through existing decision machinery; do not add an infallible optimiser or duplicate the policy engine.

Required operating method: Represent plan alternatives with predictions, uncertainty, constraints, cost, time, dependencies, and information value. Allocate look-ahead by its expected effect on the decision, with explicit bounds and anytime outputs. Include observe, clarify, delay where safe, delegate, and stop as possible actions, but evaluate the effects of inaction too. Distinguish uncertainty from risk estimates and preserve hard constraints outside preference optimisation. Abort cannot be assumed safe in a moving physical system. When the same observations fit incompatible worlds, request distinguishing information or choose a qualified robust response rather than manufacture certainty.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  PlanBounded(goal_ref, state_snapshot_ref, CPI.PredictiveInterface?, budget_ref) -> proposed action plan, alternatives, and residual obligations. Effects: proposals only.

CPI-P10 — Durable Execution and Effect Reconciliation
Primary owner: Capability.Compute.Runtime
Cooperating owners: Engine.Orchestrate, Capability.State.EventService, Engine.Transition, Capability.Trust.Security
Existing anchors: [T-AI-DEV] [RA-GSL-AR]
Conceptual references: R12, R13

Delta and non-duplication: Close the identified persistent-supervisor gap with explicit job identity, journal, budget ownership, crash recovery, and external-effect reconciliation. This is a runtime specialisation, not a second event ledger.

Required operating method: Bind each job to a stable identifier, principal, approved inputs, DesignEpoch, resource account, phase, and effect ledger. Journal intended effects before dispatch where the protocol permits; record acknowledged effects. Resume with a new fenced lease and current authorisation. Use idempotency only where the destination actually supports it. A crash between an external effect and receipt must become an uncertain outcome requiring reconciliation, not an automatic retry. A checkpoint is not authority to bypass revocation. Account for cumulative work across restarts, replicas, and children. Protect journals from candidate writes. Do not promise exactly-once effects across arbitrary external systems.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  AdvanceJob(CPI.DurableJobRecord, authorised_transition, runtime_observation) -> next CPI.DurableJobRecord and existing action/effect receipts. Effects: only the individually authorised step.

CPI-P11 — Evidence-Aware Multi-Agent Coordination
Primary owner: Engine.Orchestrate
Cooperating owners: Engine.Compare, Engine.Reason, Capability.Trust.Security, Capability.Knowledge
Existing anchors: [T-CAPABILITIES] [T-AI-DEV]
Conceptual references: R13

Delta and non-duplication: Specialise existing multi-agent development roles with correlation-aware evidence accounting, least-privilege message handling, and explicitly bounded communication.

Required operating method: Assign agents scoped roles, write sets, tool access, budgets, and parent identities. Send task context and provenance as typed data rather than interpreting arbitrary peer messages as superior instructions. Record shared models, evidence, toolchains, and organisational dependencies when assessing reviewer independence. Separate proposer, evaluator, and release authority. Use diverse methods or observations where available; otherwise disclose common-mode risk. Do not infer truth from message count or expand permissions through agent agreements. Attribute failures and preserve dissenting findings without letting a single agent suppress them from the review record.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  CoordinateWork(work_package_set, role_bindings, shared_budget_ref) -> scoped task results and comparison/evidence records. Effects: bounded delegation only.

CPI-P12 — Protected Automated Engineering and Evolution
Primary owner: Engine.Evolve
Cooperating owners: Capability.Platform.Development, Engine.Compile, Engine.Research, Engine.Proof
Existing anchors: [T-EVOLVE] [T-AI-DEV]
Conceptual references: R14, R15

Delta and non-duplication: Combine candidate generation, program search, experiments, and negative-result preservation under the existing frozen-evaluator and bounded evolution controls. No evaluator self-promotion is added.

Required operating method: Generate diverse candidate repairs or algorithms within an explicit write set. Maintain immutable evaluator versions, held-out tasks, baseline results, and cost/risk objectives. Run each candidate in an isolated workspace. Preserve failed trials, contamination findings, and raw outputs. Compare against current Garden and a simple baseline, not only a deliberately weak system. A passing score is insufficient when another contract fails. Refresh held-out evaluation independently to control adaptation to repeated tests. Checker changes follow the baseline external-certification path. Merge and deployment remain separate authorised transitions.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  EvolveCandidate(work_package_ref, CPI.EvaluationPlan, population_budget_ref) -> CPI.ConstructionCandidate set and existing CIConformancePipelineReceipt records. Effects: candidates and experimental results only.

CPI-P13 — Backend Portfolio and Resource Efficiency
Primary owner: Engine.Orchestrate
Cooperating owners: Engine.Reason, Engine.Compare, Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware, Capability.Compute.Runtime
Existing anchors: [T-CAPABILITIES] [T-AI-DEV]
Conceptual references: R16

Delta and non-duplication: Compare reasoning backends, fast procedures, symbolic solvers, and learned models as replaceable mechanisms. No vendor model, checkpoint, training set, distillation output, or API entitlement is imported.

Required operating method: Bind each available mechanism to task eligibility, resource costs, quality/risk evidence, data permissions, retained state, and hardware limits. Select the smallest sufficient configuration using AAP rather than activating every subsystem. Treat imitation/distillation as a separately proposed process requiring rights to all teacher outputs and training data; absent those rights it is excluded. Preserve privacy between unrelated users and task domains. Measure end-to-end cost, latency, memory, energy, failure and fallback at matched utility. For a workload fraction f moved to a path with relative cost r and whole-workload overhead h, the illustrative compute saving is f(1-r)-h under the stated accounting assumptions. This formula is not an observed saving, does not account for everything by itself, and does not imply equivalent chip-purchase or electricity reductions.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  SelectBackend(task_ref, qualified_mechanism_records, CPI.EvaluationPlan, budget_ref) -> selected mechanism and rationale, or explicit unavailability. Effects: selection; invocation uses existing runtime authorisation.

CPI-P14 — Rights-Preserving Implementation Admission
Primary owner: Engine.Compliance
Cooperating owners: Capability.Authority.Law, Capability.Platform.Development, Capability.Knowledge.Curation, Capability.Trust.Security
Existing anchors: [T-COMPLIANCE-STD] [T-AI-DEV]
Conceptual references: L01, L02, L03, L04, L05

Delta and non-duplication: Specialise the existing licence/IP admission checks for this user-directed NO_THIRD_PARTY_IMPORT posture. This is not legal certification or a new ownership regime.

Required operating method: Classify material as a public conceptual reference, newly drafted requirement, Garden baseline material, third-party expressive material, executable code, model weights, data, or unknown provenance. For this patch admit no external code, weights, datasets, substantial copied prose, assets, or translated implementations. Even permissive-licence components are excluded from the import set under the zero-obligation instruction. Keep conceptual references for provenance without claiming ownership of the underlying public ideas. Preserve existing rights-holder fields and any previously valid obligations; this policy cannot erase pre-existing rights. Do not publish, upstream contributions, sign agreements, add patent grants, choose a Garden licence, or release protected designs. Evaluate patent freedom-to-operate separately for actual implementations and jurisdictions. An unresolved specific legal hurdle causes exclusion or hold, never circumvention or a renamed copy.

Function contract (specification notation, not executable GSL):
  AssessAdmission(material_description, proposed_use, existing_rights_ref) -> CPI.RightsBoundaryRecord. Effects: recorded policy disposition only; no legal right is created.


SemanticAnchor: [T-CPI-RQ]
Use the twelve owner-bound operational refinements in [A-CPI-RQ]. Reference algorithm symbols and type extensions are defined there rather than duplicated. Existing assurance status is never promoted by this candidate assembly. Exact composition unit checks, positive fencing and immutable acknowledgements are mandatory. The bounded reference and conditional proofs accompany this candidate; they do not implement every listed cognitive algorithm.

END CPI v1.1 CANDIDATE ADDITION


========================================================================================================================
[T-CDDT] CROSS-DOMAIN DISCOVERY & TRANSFER v1.1 — TECHNICAL PROFILE
========================================================================================================================
Execution binds logical cycle identity, operating-day configuration, profile, inventory/knowledge roots, source/access scope,
resource policy, model/method versions, randomness/clock inputs where relevant, operation keys and checkpoints. Finalized cycle results are immutable; additional work creates linked continuation/revision. Bounded loops/checkpoints are mandatory; resource exhaustion returns partial/deferred, never completeness.

Transfer analysis assesses relevant entities, relations, states, operations, interfaces, scales, units, observables, assumptions and constraints; each element is preserved/adapted/unsupported/incompatible. Correspondence class is exact mapping under assumptions | qualified approximation | shared mechanism | analogy awaiting mapping. Source-domain success or terminology cannot establish target-domain validity.

Novelty/familiarity are independent scoped assessments:
garden_familiarity = PREVIOUSLY_RECORDED | NOT_LOCATED_IN_BOUND_GARDEN_SCOPE | UNKNOWN.
Per target application novelty = UNSCREENED | EQUIVALENT_PRIOR_WORK_FOUND | RELATED_WORK_FOUND_DELTA_UNRESOLVED |
PROPOSED_DELTA_NOT_LOCATED_IN_SEARCH_SCOPE.
Unsuccessful search never emits unqualified NEW_TO_WORLD.

ClaimValidationPlan binds target claim, assumptions, baseline, outcomes, failure/stop criterion, evidence, resource limit and review condition.
Causal claims use the existing C0-C4 ladder and retain identification assumptions/uncertainty. Metamorphic testing, counterexample-guided refinement,
decision-focused evidence acquisition, causal transportability, inverse reconstruction/simulation are selectable strategies, not mandatory new theories.

Metrology:
- define prediction target/horizon/domain before outcome;
- retain supported/refuted/inconclusive/not-evaluated outcomes separately from workflow disposition;
- only eligible evidence updates a versioned compatibility/scheduling model;
- freeze pre-outcome features to prevent leakage;
- retain provenance/dependence clusters to avoid duplicate evidence;
- evaluate updates against current and simpler baselines using held-out/time-ordered/domain-separated evidence;
- protected exploration and mandatory revalidation survive low scores;
- rollback changes active model but preserves history.

CDDT may guide research scheduling; it never bypasses evidence, safety, privacy, authority, DesignEpoch or adoption gates.


========================================================================================================================
[T-OCF] OPERATIONAL CLOSURE FOUNDATION — IMPLEMENTATION CONTRACT
========================================================================================================================
Primary owners: Engine.Compile, Engine.Reason, Engine.Dependency, Engine.Compare, Engine.Proof, Engine.Research,
Engine.Evolve, Engine.Orchestrate, Engine.Transition, Engine.Audit, Engine.Compliance, Capability.Knowledge.Curation/GSL-KR.
Existing Safety/Security/Privacy/Authority/TRAIN/GAIA/GESTALT/APAO/GNR/Reliability-OS boundaries remain controlling.
No second compiler, repository, evolution engine, or safety authority is created.

Closure algorithm:
1 MAP — extract semantics, inputs/outputs, state, effects, assumptions, resources, authority, privacy, safety, evidence, failure behavior, update behavior, composition and deployment conditions.
2 MATERIALITY — retain any distinction whose plausible variation can alter truth status, safety, cost, authority, consequential output or admissibility; consequential unknowns remain unresolved.
3 CLASSIFY — DERIVATION | DESIGN | DISCOVERY | BLOCKED.
4 CLOSE — execute the corresponding qualified path; never fill an unresolved semantic/empirical choice by prose default.
5 COMPOSE — revalidate units, frames, timing, concurrency, state ownership, feedback, shared resources, common-mode errors, authority/privacy/safety propagation and external effects.
6 QUALIFY — bind source/environment/verifier/tests/evidence/assumptions/DesignEpoch/rights/validity/invalidators.
7 OBSERVE — collect results, failures, costs, drift, applicability failures, correction effort and effect integrity.
8 REOPEN — invalidators mark affected receipts stale/revalidation-required before consequential reuse.
9 LEARN — store typed GSL-KR fault/fix/counterexample lineage; abstraction requires exception-preserving qualification.
10 RECURSE — audit the repair, interfaces, changed checker/evaluator boundary and new assumptions.

[T-OCF-BOOTSTRAP] AGI-ORIENTED RECURSIVE CONSTRUCTION ORDER
Stage 0 trusted seed: minimal GSL semantics, immutable source roots, protected checker boundary, persistent job/resource identity, basic GSL-KR claim storage.
Stage 1 minimal executable GSL kernel sufficient to represent obligations, claims, dependencies, tests and receipts.
Stage 2 closure auditor: source → ClosureManifest/GapRecord.
Stage 3 runtime builder: CPI recursive construction of DERIVABLE/DESIGNABLE obligations in sandbox.
Stage 4 knowledge ingestion: provenance/epistemic separation.
Stage 5 cognitive portfolio: qualify concrete reasoning/planning/learning mechanisms under replaceable interfaces.
Stage 6 predictive/world-model and skill learning with applicability/drift handling.
Stage 7 bounded multi-agent/evolution with protected evaluation.
Stage 8 physical/domain simulation, experiment and pilots; empirical failure returns to GSL-KR.
Stage 9 federation of qualified knowledge/software without hidden global authority.

Highest-priority residual implementation frontier:
F1 minimal executable GSL compiler/semantic kernel;
F2 automated source→ClosureManifest obligation extraction;
F3 general GSL-KR ingestion/entity/evidence pipeline;
F4 concrete cognitive mechanism portfolio/routing policy;
F5 concrete world-model learning;
F6 concrete skill/procedure induction;
F7 automated compositional-assurance checker;
F8 trusted persistent runtime/distributed storage;
F9 independent proof/check infrastructure;
F10 domain empirical validation harnesses;
F11 federation/update protocol;
F12 TRAIN/GAIA hardware/timing validation where used.

OCF does not claim universal ontology completeness, decidability, guaranteed search convergence, reversibility of physical effects, perfect validators, completed AGI, universal superiority, or elimination of unknown unknowns.


========================================================================================================================
[T-MSC] MINIMUM STABLE CORE — TECHNICAL PRESERVATION CONTRACT
========================================================================================================================
MSC is a release/profile designation, not a new component.

A successor mechanism is Garden-compatible only if, for its declared scope:
- GSL semantic distinctions required by the task remain recoverable;
- CLAIM status is not silently upgraded through storage, compression, consensus or model confidence;
- provenance/dependencies/DesignEpoch lineage remain reconstructible;
- authority, privacy, safety and consent are checked independently of cognitive confidence;
- protected roots are not self-certified by the candidate path they judge;
- unresolved obligations remain explicit under OCF;
- recursive construction obeys CPI parent scope/budget/checker inheritance;
- resource exhaustion, uncertainty and unsupported constructs fail honestly;
- consequential reuse rechecks applicability and live authority;
- external effects are accounted for through the existing action/effect/runtime contracts.

MSC does not require any particular LLM, database, planner, theorem prover, world model, scientific theory or hardware vendor.

========================================================================================================================
[T-TML] THEORY & MODULE LIFECYCLE — OPERATIONAL CONTRACT
========================================================================================================================
Primary owner: Engine.Evolve.
Co-owners: Engine.Research, Engine.Compare, Engine.Proof, Engine.Dependency, Engine.Compile, Engine.Audit,
Capability.Knowledge.Curation/GSL-KR, Engine.Compliance.

The lifecycle applies to theories, algorithms, profiles, backends, compiler profiles and domain models unless a stronger frozen-root rule applies.
Every version binds:
- exact predecessor(s);
- scope/applicability;
- claim set or functional contract;
- evidence/proof/test references;
- unresolved anomalies;
- failure/invalidator conditions;
- benchmark/economic profile where material;
- dependencies/toolchain/environment;
- migration/rollback behavior;
- rights/provenance;
- status and supersession relationship.

A CURRENT_BEST state means “best admitted under the declared comparison/evidence set”, never global optimality.
A successor may narrow scope while improving truthfulness. Supersession does not erase predecessor evidence or field-valid regimes.

For empirical theories, the lifecycle SHOULD retain:
claim/prediction → cheapest or highest-value discriminating observation/experiment → result → dependency-aware update.
When no safe/authorised observation is available, keep the frontier open.

========================================================================================================================
[T-V151-UPGRADES] NEW SUBORDINATE PROFILES
========================================================================================================================

V151-P01 — External Deterministic Action Gate
Owner: Capability.Compute.Runtime / GNR.
Co-owners: Compliance, Authority, Security, Safety, GAIA Diamond/Titanium where applicable.
Every consequential tool/device/external-system operation carries an explicit capability/action contract. The gate evaluates principal,
delegation scope, live revocation/expiry, target/resource limits, data policy, action class, and mandatory safety policy independently from
the cognitive proposer. An unsupported capability is unavailable, not implicitly allowed. Gate failure produces a typed denial; the AI may
propose a lawful alternative but cannot rewrite the gate through ordinary task content.

V151-P02 — Agent Execution Privilege Rings
Owner: Capability.Trust.Security / GNR / Reliability OS.
R0 protected roots; R1 qualified system services; R2 admitted bounded agents; R3 generated/experimental code; R4 untrusted content.
Cross-ring calls use explicit typed interfaces/capabilities. Candidate/generated code begins no more privileged than R3 unless separately
qualified. R4 data is never executable authority. Privilege escalation requires a separately authorised transition and evidence.

V151-P03 — Outcome-Based Knowledge & Memory Evaluation
Owner: Capability.Knowledge.Curation / GSL-KR.
Co-owners: Compare, Research, Reason, Audit.
Evaluate a memory/knowledge mechanism on matched tasks using task success, consistency/calibration, stale-error rate, correction burden,
latency, compute/memory/energy where measurable, privacy cost and total operating cost. Retrieval precision/recall alone cannot establish
benefit. Compare against no-memory/full-context/simple-cache baselines where relevant.

V151-P04 — Causal World-Model Extension
Owner: Engine.Research.
Co-owners: Reason, Knowledge, Compare, Proof, CPI PredictiveWorldModel.
Predictive models may emit causal hypotheses only with explicit graph/structural assumptions and identification status. Preserve
OBSERVATIONAL_ASSOCIATION, CAUSAL_HYPOTHESIS, IDENTIFIED_UNDER_ASSUMPTIONS, INTERVENTION_SUPPORTED and REFUTED/INCONCLUSIVE distinctions.
do/intervention claims require appropriate evidence; simulation does not become causal proof. Transport to a new domain requires an explicit
transportability/applicability analysis.

V151-P05 — Temporal Hypergraph Knowledge Projection
Owner: Capability.Knowledge.Curation / Algebra.Bridge.
Complex events/relations may be projected from ordinary GSL EVENT/THING/AGENCY/TIME/CONTEXT/CLAIM objects into typed temporal hyperedges
with role-labelled participants and provenance. The projection is derived/indexing structure, not an eleventh core object and not a separate
truth store. Round-trip or loss declarations are required.

V151-P06 — Neuro-Symbolic Constraint Bridge
Owner: Algebra.Bridge.
Co-owners: Reason, Research, Proof, Safety.
Learned/continuous models may consume differentiable/soft constraints and hard symbolic constraints through a typed bridge. Hard
safety/authority constraints remain hard gates even if a learned objective assigns them low probability or high cost. Approximate constraint
semantics, gradients and numerical tolerances must be declared; learned satisfaction is not formal proof.

V151-P07 — Persistent Embodied State–Action Loop
Owner: Capability.Knowledge + Engine.Research + GNR.
Maintain persistent object/entity/state hypotheses across perception cycles with time/frame/sensor provenance. Bind predicted transitions to
actions and later observations; preserve identity ambiguity/occlusion; qualify learned skills for the operating distribution. Physical actions
still pass existing TRAIN/GAIA/Safety/Authority paths.

V151-P08 — Recursive Self-Improvement Metrology
Owner: Engine.Evolve / Audit.
Measure per campaign: autonomous work fraction, human intervention categories, generated candidates, accepted changes, defects introduced,
defects detected before deployment, post-admission regressions, evaluator modifications attempted/rejected, compute/time/energy/cost where
measurable, retained utility delta, rollback frequency and frontier reduction. Higher autonomy is not itself success; utility and assurance at
matched cost remain controlling.

V151-P09 — Reliability Transaction / Progressive-Deployment Hardening
Owner: Reliability OS / Runtime / Transition.
Where effects span multiple services or steps, use a declared transactional/compensation graph rather than assuming atomicity. Keep kill/contain
controls outside ordinary agent write scope; use health/SLO/error-budget signals, canary/progressive activation and adversarial/chaos tests where
appropriate to the domain. Failure injection is authorised/sandboxed and cannot bypass safety/rights. Existing circuit-breaker/rollback duties remain.

No profile above imports any external implementation. External systems that motivated comparison are provenance references only.

========================================================================================================================
v15.1 EXTERNAL-DESIGN LEARNING / RIGHTS BOUNDARY
========================================================================================================================
Garden may learn from publicly described architectures, algorithms, benchmarks and safety patterns through CDDT/Research/TML.
Under the current no-import posture, v15.1 imports no external code, weights, datasets, substantial prose, diagrams or service dependency.
External ideas are treated as prior-art/provenance and independently restated as Garden requirements. Any future direct import requires a
separate rights/admission review and does not alter ownership of independently created Garden material or pre-existing Garden rights.
No patent validity, patentability or freedom-to-operate conclusion is created by this design document.


========================================================================================================================
[T-CMUR] CROSS-MODEL UPGRADE REVIEW — OPERATIONAL CONTRACT
========================================================================================================================
Primary owner: Engine.Evolve.
Co-owners: Engine.Orchestrate, Engine.Compare, Engine.Reason, Engine.Proof, Engine.Audit, Engine.Dependency,
Engine.Research, Engine.Compliance, Capability.Knowledge.Curation/GSL-KR.

CMUR applies to every AUTOMATIC admission of a theory, module, algorithm, compiler/profile, safety rule, runtime component,
knowledge-maintenance rule, or foundational-design change. Manual proposals may be drafted without CMUR, but automatic promotion may not.

Minimum ordinary automatic-admission quorum:
- at least 3 distinct qualified model identities/checkpoints;
- at least 5 distinct review roles;
- at least one adversarial/skeptical reviewer;
- at least one independent recomputation/checking role;
- at least one implementation/integration/regression role.
Where available, prefer different model families/providers/toolchains. Exact independence is rarely knowable; therefore record observed
independence factors and common-mode risks rather than claiming perfect independence.

Foundational/critical profile:
For MSC/OCF/CPI protected semantics; HumanCore/HSA; safety/authority/privacy/security roots; compiler semantics; irreversible physical action;
or high-consequence deployment, automatic admission requires a stronger configured quorum. Recommended starting profile:
- 5 or more distinct qualified model identities;
- 7 or more roles;
- multiple model families and at least 2 independently administered/provider paths where available;
- at least one non-LLM checker/prover/test path where the claim type permits;
- no unresolved CRITICAL finding.
If this profile cannot be satisfied, automatic admission is blocked; candidate status may remain.

Independence dimensions recorded per reviewer:
model/provider/family/checkpoint; prompt/role; context visibility; source set; retrieval provider; toolchain; code executor; verifier;
training-lineage information if publicly known; organisation/operator; randomness/seed where relevant.
Unknown independence is recorded UNKNOWN, never assumed independent.

Blind-first review:
Where practical, reviewers inspect the frozen candidate independently before seeing peer conclusions, reducing conformity cascades.
A second synthesis round may expose all findings for rebuttal and correction.

Review semantics:
- each finding is typed: BLOCKER | MATERIAL | MINOR | QUESTION | SUPPORT;
- each finding cites exact candidate section/artifact and evidence/counterexample;
- SUPPORT is not evidence of truth by itself;
- majority vote cannot close BLOCKER/mandatory obligations;
- shared-source/model findings are clustered for independence accounting;
- contradictory reviews generate a Compare/Reason obligation rather than averaging away the contradiction.

Correction semantics:
Any material candidate change after review invalidates affected review receipts. Re-review scope is dependency-aware but cannot omit
changed semantics, changed tests/checkers, or changed external-effect/authority paths.

CMUR is one admission layer, not a new truth engine. It never substitutes for empirical observation, formal proof, physical testing,
licence/rights clearance, protected authority, or safety certification when those are otherwise required.

GARDEN v15.3 — FULL SUCCESSOR ADDENDUM

Predecessor: Garden v15.2 FULL (2026-09-11)
Release intent: no-loss successor integrating cross-layer integrity/causality/continuity hardening.
GSL: v45.1 unchanged. Core topology, Human Sovereignty, authority hierarchy and existing protected roots unchanged.

[T-CLIC] CROSS-LAYER INTEGRITY, CAUSALITY & CONTINUITY — IMPLEMENTATION CONTRACT

CLIC-P01 Intent-to-Effect Semantic Conservation
Owner: Engine.Dependency with Algebra.Bridge; cooperating Reason/Compile/Proof/Orchestrate/Knowledge/Runtime/Diamond/Titanium.
Method: bind bounded source intent, interpretation choices, material semantics (units, frames, time, identity, scope, allowed effects, epistemic state and authority), transformation checks, qualified approximation and observed effect. A missing consequential semantic link remains unresolved; local syntax/hash/embedding similarity is not enough.

CLIC-P02 Causal Intervention & Counterfactual Qualification
Owner: Engine.Reason; cooperating Research/Compare/Knowledge/CPI-P07/P09.
Method: state estimand, requested/supported causal level, confounders, mechanism assumptions, source/target population/environment, evidence and identification basis. Reuse C0..C4. Prediction does not imply intervention effect; intervention evidence does not automatically identify individual counterfactuals; transportability is separate.

CLIC-P03 Attention, Salience & Information-Value Governance
Owner: Engine.Reason under AAP/Orchestrate resource allocation.
Method: prioritize consequential unresolved questions using relevance, anomaly, decision sensitivity, expected information value where meaningful, cost, deadlines and neglected coverage. Preserve mandatory monitoring and an explicit unexamined frontier. Salience never upgrades truth or authority.

CLIC-P04 Identity, Commitment & Responsibility Continuity
Owner: Engine.Transition / Capability.State.EventService; cooperating VSM/CPI-P10/Orchestrate/Dependency/HumanCore.
Method: distinguish task, state, authority, responsibility, privacy and commitment continuity. Restart cannot erase accepted duties; copy cannot multiply delegation; fork/merge must allocate or preserve unresolved responsibilities; local lifecycle labels map to existing UPA/Transition semantics rather than creating a new universal state machine.

CLIC-P05 Emergence, Common-Cause Failure, Reversibility & Option Preservation
Owner: Engine.Research / Reliability OS; cooperating Reason/Compare/Dependency/CPI-P09/P11/P12/safety systems.
Method: assess interaction topology, feedback, correlated evidence/model/tool/operator/infrastructure dependencies, synchronization, nonlinear thresholds, affected parties, reversal/compensation feasibility, systemic reach and future-option loss. Local safety does not imply system safety. Reversibility is a conditional planning preference, not an absolute rule; urgency, inaction risk and hard obligations remain considered.

Five local function specifications pending canonical function binding/reuse:
CheckIntentChain; QualifyCausalClaim; AllocateAttention; ReviewContinuity; AssessInteractionOptions.
No new engine, authority root or GSL primitive is created.

Admission precedence:
- existing Human Sovereignty/K0P/Safety/Authority controls remain superior;
- OCF and CPI closure rules remain controlling;
- CMUR remains mandatory for automatic upgrade admission;
- CDDT owns cross-domain discovery, not CLIC;
- CASI remains deferred and unactivated.

====================================================================================================
GARDEN v15.4 — ALGEBRA, CAUSAL LEVERAGE & RESIDUAL INTEGRITY ADDENDUM
====================================================================================================

SemanticAnchor: [T-ALG-FRM]
ALGEBRA FORMALIZATION — v15.4

Purpose:
Make the existing AlgebraSignature instances operationally unambiguous without introducing a new algebra engine.
REG-ALGEBRA-001 remains the canonical instance registry. SCHEMA-C6B28E5F7D (AlgebraSignature) and
SCHEMA-D8E1601C54 (AlgebraInstanceRegistry) remain the owner schemas. No duplicate AlgebraOperand/AlgebraOperator
schema is required: the registry entry for each operator SHALL carry the following normalized fields:

  algebra_ref;
  operator_name;
  operand_types[];
  result_type;
  preconditions[];
  declared_laws[];
  declared_non_laws[];
  error_behavior;
  unknown_behavior;
  authority_effect;
  epistemic_effect;
  determinism: DETERMINISTIC | STOCHASTIC_WITH_DECLARED_RANDOMNESS | ENVIRONMENT_DEPENDENT;
  proof_obligations[];
  version;
  provenance.

A law not declared for the active operator/version SHALL NOT be relied upon. Lack of a declared law means
NOT_ESTABLISHED_FOR_USE, not a mathematical proof that the law is impossible.

PROCESS instance — operands: Process, Transition, TerminationContract.
Operators:
  SEQUENCE, PARALLEL, BRANCH, COMPOSE, RETRY, ROLLBACK, COMPENSATE, RECONSTRUCT, ITERATE, RECURSE, TERMINATE,
  DO_NOTHING.
Existing [T-PROCESS] semantics remain authoritative. In particular, ROLLBACK is not a claim of physical reversal and
PARALLEL is not universally commutative at the effect level.

POLICY instance — operands: PolicyArtifact, PolicyDecision, ObligationSet.
Operators:
  AND_D, OR_D, NOT_D, RESOLVE, PRECEDENCE, EXCEPTION, OBLIGATION_UNION.
Policy composition preserves applicability, authority scope, exceptions, unresolved conflict and owner-qualified result
status. Policy algebra cannot create authority absent the substantive authority source.

DECISION instance — operands: typed DecisionAlternative / Effect / Result values and a declared DecisionContext.
Operators:
  SELECT, WEIGHTED_SELECT, RANKED_SELECT, PARETO_SELECT.
SELECT requires a declared choice rule over admissible candidates. WEIGHTED_SELECT may scalarize only dimensions for
which the DecisionContext supplies a justified commensurability/weight model; hard rights/safety/authority constraints
remain outside preference weighting. RANKED_SELECT preserves ties/incomparability where the ordering is partial.
PARETO_SELECT returns the nondominated frontier unless another authorized rule selects within it.

CONFORMANCE instance — operands: RequirementSet, ApplicabilitySet, ObligationSet, EvidenceSet, ProofSet, CoverageSet,
StandardMapping.
Operators:
  REQUIREMENT_UNION, REQUIREMENT_INTERSECTION, SCOPE_PROJECT, MAP_STANDARD, PROPAGATE_OBLIGATION, BIND_EVIDENCE,
  BIND_PROOF, INVALIDATE_BY_DEPENDENCY, COVERAGE, GAP, DIFF, COMPOSE_CONFORMANCE.
Coverage over a declared finite requirement set does not imply completeness of the universe. BIND_EVIDENCE and
BIND_PROOF preserve the distinction between empirical support and formal entailment.

EVIDENCE instance — operands: Evidence, EvidenceSet, DependenceModel.
Operators:
  CORROBORATE, CONTRADICT, CONDITION, ASSESS_DEPENDENCE.
Existing [T-EVIDENCE-ALGEBRA] semantics remain controlling. Repetition, votes, compression or shared-source copies do
not manufacture independence.

BRIDGE instance remains governed by its existing detailed operator table and REP-* invariants. v15.4 does not duplicate
it.

CROSS-ALGEBRA COMPOSITION INTERFACES

  APPLY_POLICY(policy_or_policy_set, context) -> PolicyDecision
  APPLY_DECISION(decision_context, alternatives) -> DecisionResult
  VERIFY_CONFORMANCE(target, requirement_set, context) -> ConformanceResult
  APPLY_EVIDENCE(evidence_set, dependence_model, context) -> EvidenceComposition

These are typed composition interfaces between existing algebras, not new top-level engines. Each invocation preserves
source algebra status, authority, uncertainty, provenance and non-PASS terminals.

SemanticAnchor: [T-GCL]
GENERALIZED CAUSAL LEVERAGE — v15.4

Status:
RATIFIED as a bounded analytical/theory profile. GCL is not a new physical force, not a universal scalar metric and not
an authority source.

Core principle:
A small informational intervention can redirect much larger pre-existing flows through ordinary causal pathways.
Examples include a short control message changing a machine schedule, a policy notice redirecting institutional action,
or a warning changing human behavior. The leverage resides in the causal organization of the system, not in information
creating energy or momentum from nothing.

Canonical assessment form:

  GCL(I, B, M, E) = Delta_effect_vector[ do(I) versus do(B) ] / Size(I | E)

where:
  I = declared informational intervention;
  B = counterfactual baseline/reference intervention;
  M = declared causal model / identification basis;
  E = exact encoding or measurement basis for intervention size.

The numerator is a typed vector. Example dimensions may include energy redirected, material flow, computational work,
financial/resource commitment, agent/action count, institutional actions, spatial reach or domain-specific effects.
Dimensions with incompatible units SHALL NOT be silently added into a universal scalar.

CausalLeverageVector = ordered finite set of:
  {dimension_id, quantity_or_count, unit_or_semantics, sign/direction, interval_or_distribution?, uncertainty,
   evidence_refs[], causal_attribution_status}.

TransmissionChannel = PHYSICAL | COMPUTATIONAL | INFORMATIONAL | ECONOMIC | SOCIAL | INSTITUTIONAL |
OWNER_QUALIFIED_OTHER.
The channel labels describe pathways; they are not forces and do not create ontology beyond existing typed relations.

Schema reference:
  SCHEMA-A000718817 — CausalLeverageAssessment.

@SC|CausalLeverageAssessment|-|assessment_id,information_intervention_ref,encoding_basis,intervention_size,counterfactual_baseline_ref,causal_model_ref,causal_support_level:C0 | C1 | C2 | C3 | C4,transmission_channels[],transmission_path_refs[],effect_vector,scalarization_ref?,affected_scope,latency,duration,reversibility_class,rights_impact_ref?,safety_impact_ref?,uncertainty,status:ESTIMATED | OBSERVED_WITH_ATTRIBUTION | MODEL_ONLY | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN,knowledge_snapshot,environment_commitment?,DesignEpoch,provenance

Rules:
- causal_support_level uses the current Garden/CDDT/CLIC meanings: C0 Correlation, C1 Temporal, C2 Mechanistic,
  C3 Intervention, C4 Counterfactual.
- MODEL_ONLY / ESTIMATED outputs may be useful for planning but do not become observed causal facts.
- an OBSERVED_WITH_ATTRIBUTION claim requires the applicable identification/evidence obligations; mere temporal
  sequence or prediction accuracy is insufficient.
- scalarization_ref is optional and valid only when a domain-qualified mapping justifies combining the vector for a
  specific decision. The vector remains retained.
- leverage magnitude is separate from benefit, harm, legitimacy, authority and preference.
- missing effect dimensions are UNKNOWN/bounded, not zero by default.

Function specification:
  AssessCausalLeverage(intervention_ref, baseline_ref, causal_model_ref, encoding_basis, scope, budget_ref)
    -> CausalLeverageAssessment | bounded non-PASS result.
  Effects: analysis/knowledge candidate only; no execution authority.

Primary owners:
  Engine.Reason; Engine.Compare; Engine.Dependency; Engine.Research; Capability.Knowledge.
Cooperating owners:
  CLIC-P02; CLIC-P05; CDDT causal qualification; SRT; AAP; HumanCore; Engine.Policy; applicable Domain.Applied.*.

SemanticAnchor: [T-EPI-GOV-V154]
EVIDENCE / GOVERNANCE RECONCILIATION

v15.4 does not add the old numeric EpistemicVeto threshold. Instead the controlling rule is structural:

- a vote/consensus/majority cannot change Claim/Evidence/Proof status;
- a materially relevant counterexample remains present until resolved, scoped out with evidence, invalidated, or otherwise
  disposed under ordinary epistemic rules;
- governance may legitimately choose an action under uncertainty where existing rights/safety/authority policy permits,
  but the decision receipt SHALL state the unresolved material evidence and the basis for proceeding;
- one supported dissent can be more important than many correlated unsupported votes; source count is not epistemic
  weight by itself.

This is a consolidation of existing K-INT-008, GOV-010, CMUR-007/010/019, RPM-007, Evidence Algebra and CLIC controls,
not a new authority source.

SemanticAnchor: [T-V154-RECOVERY]
HISTORICAL PATCH RECOVERY / NON-DUPLICATION

The following older proposals are satisfied by current v15.4 mechanisms and SHALL NOT be reintroduced as duplicate
owners: DesignMirror; ConstitutionalEvent; OntologicalDebt; FutureStressAssessment; BeliefTimeline/HistoricalEpistemicState;
GlobalInvariantConsistencyReceipt; rejection/failure memory; adaptive/loss-aware summarisation.

The old AgencyLevel Tool->Assistant->Delegate->Sovereign ladder is informative history only. Current Garden represents
capability, autonomy, delegation, resource scope, physical control and legitimate authority separately; no single ladder
may silently substitute for those dimensions.

Hardware/device attestation MAY be mandatory under an applicable assurance profile. Absence of attestation does not
universally mean 'not Evidence'; evidence quality instead follows source/provenance/calibration/measurement and policy
requirements. A valid attestation binds device/software state under its assumptions but does not itself prove the world
state was correctly measured.

No universal fixed-age purge interval is introduced. [A.56] remains controlling.



====================================================================================================
GARDEN v15.5 — RELEASE INTEGRITY, GCL ARITHMETIC & CLIC BINDING REPAIR
====================================================================================================

SemanticAnchor: [T-V155]

SemanticAnchor: [T-GCL-V155]
GCL v15.5 — TYPED ANALYTICAL COMPUTATION CONTRACT

The v15.4 formula
  GCL(I,B,M,E) = Delta_effect_vector[do(I) versus do(B)] / Size(I|E)
is retained only as historical explanatory shorthand. It is NOT the canonical executable contract after v15.5.

Canonical computation separates four operations:

A. MeasureInterventionEncoding(intervention_ref, encoding_profile_ref, scope_ref)
   -> TypedVector | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN

   The TypedVector records every material intervention-measure coordinate with explicit semantics, unit/basis, boundary,
   uncertainty and provenance. Examples may include UTF-8 bytes, GSL AST nodes, speech duration or channel resource use.
   Multiple coordinates are not silently commensurable.

B. ComputeTypedEffectDelta(intervention_effect_ref, baseline_effect_ref, alignment_ref)
   -> TypedVector | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN

   Difference is coordinate-wise only after dimension/unit/semantic/frame alignment. Missing coordinates remain UNKNOWN
   or explicitly out-of-scope; they are not inserted as zero. Uncertainty and causal-attribution status propagate.

C. NormalizeInterventionMeasure(intervention_measure_ref, norm_ref, scope_ref)
   -> BoundedScalar | NOT_APPLICABLE | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN

   norm_ref resolves a qualified NormDefinition or equivalent existing measurement contract. It states included
   coordinates, units/reference scales, weighting/normalization semantics, validity scope, zero handling, uncertainty and
   provenance. Normalization is optional. No universal "information size" exists.

D. ComputeNormalizedCausalLeverage(effect_delta_ref, normalized_intervention_size_ref, arithmetic_context_ref)
   -> TypedVector | NOT_APPLICABLE | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN

   Each output component retains its effect dimension and the exact denominator semantics. A zero/non-positive denominator
   where division is undefined, UNKNOWN denominator, incompatible basis, or missing required mapping yields a non-PASS
   result rather than infinity or an invented value. Cross-basis comparisons require a qualified conversion/mapping.

AssessCausalLeverage(intervention_ref, baseline_ref, causal_model_ref, encoding_profile_ref, scope_ref, budget_ref)
   -> CausalLeverageAssessment | bounded non-PASS result

orchestrates these operations. It may return an unnormalized assessment containing intervention_measure_vector and
counterfactual_effect_delta_vector when normalization is unavailable or unjustified.

CausalLeverageAssessment v15.5 interpretation/amendment:
  - information_intervention_ref: unchanged;
  - counterfactual_baseline_ref: unchanged;
  - encoding_basis: legacy compatibility view of encoding_profile_ref;
  - intervention_size: legacy compatibility view; may be populated only when a qualified scalar measure exists;
  - intervention_measure_vector: TypedVector (current preferred representation);
  - intervention_norm_ref?: NormDefinition or equivalent qualified measurement rule;
  - normalized_intervention_size?: BoundedScalar;
  - counterfactual_effect_delta_vector: TypedVector (current preferred effect-delta representation);
  - normalized_leverage_vector?: TypedVector;
  - arithmetic_function_contract_refs[]: exact active FunctionContracts/versions;
  - effect_vector remains a compatibility projection of counterfactual_effect_delta_vector;
  - all existing causal/evidence/rights/safety/status fields remain controlling.

ANALYTICAL COMPUTATION / ALGEBRA BOUNDARY

An admitted Garden algebra is a registered normative composition structure. A typed analytical computation is not an
algebra merely because it performs arithmetic. Analytical functions SHALL be machine-resolvable through REG-FUNCTION-001,
types/units/frames, applicable Bridge contracts and TheoryEquationContract where equation-level claims are made. If an
analytical operation defines or changes normative composition semantics, it must be admitted under REG-ALGEBRA-001.

This distinction does not create a loophole: untyped or status-laundering analytical arithmetic remains inadmissible.

SemanticAnchor: [T-CLIC-BIND-V155]
CLIC v15.5 — CANONICAL FUNCTION AND PROFILE BINDING

REG-CLIC-001 identifies CLIC-P01..P05 as profile/projection identifiers, not SchemaIDs.

Canonical FunctionContract bindings:

1. CheckIntentChain
   Owner: Engine.Dependency. Cooperating: Algebra.Bridge, Engine.Compare, Engine.Reason.
   Inputs: chain_ref, obligation_bundle_ref, current_context_ref.
   Outputs: ComparisonResult plus ReasoningTrace reference; unresolved semantic obligations remain explicit.
   Effects: analysis records only. Authority: none beyond read/evaluation permissions.

2. QualifyCausalClaim
   Owner: Engine.Reason. Cooperating: Engine.Research, Engine.Compare, Capability.Knowledge.
   Inputs: claim_ref, model_ref, evidence_refs, target_scope_ref.
   Outputs: InferenceResult plus ReasoningTrace, including supported causal level and identification frontier.
   Effects: analysis/knowledge candidate only; does not authorize intervention.

3. AllocateAttention
   Owner: Engine.Reason. Cooperating: Engine.Orchestrate/AAP, Engine.Research, Capability.Knowledge.
   Inputs: inventory_snapshot_ref, mandatory_duties_ref, budget_ref.
   Outputs: ProcessPlan plus ReasoningTrace, preserving mandatory duties and unexamined frontier.
   Effects: planning only; observations/actions require ordinary permission.

4. ReviewContinuity
   Owner: Engine.Transition. Cooperating: Capability.State.EventService, Engine.Dependency, HumanCore.
   Inputs: transition_ref, commitment_refs, authority_refs, privacy_refs.
   Outputs: TransitionContract plus ComparisonResult; applicable SafeControlHandoffReceipt may be referenced for physical
            continuity but is not required for purely informational transitions.
   Effects: proposes/binds transition semantics only; commit follows existing Transition authority.

5. AssessInteractionOptions
   Owner: Engine.Research. Cooperating: Reliability OS, Engine.Reason, Engine.Compare, Engine.Dependency.
   Inputs: candidate_plan_refs, system_boundary_ref, evidence_refs, budget_ref.
   Outputs: ComparisonResult plus InferenceResult, preserving dependence/common-cause/reversibility/option uncertainty.
   Effects: analysis only; no release or deployment authority.

All five contracts inherit existing FunctionContract requirements for types, closed effect vector, result algebra,
authority, consent where applicable, failure modes, dependencies, invalidators, resources, TerminationContract,
DesignEpoch/environment/knowledge bindings, provenance and explanation trace.

CLIC PROFILE MATERIALIZATION

CLIC-P01..P05 are views/profiles over existing canonical records. They SHALL NOT be treated as globally allocated schema
identifiers. REG-CLIC-001 maps each profile to host record families. If implementation needs persistent fields not
representable by the mapped hosts, the design returns NEEDS_SCHEMA_ADMISSION and allocates a new SchemaID only through the
normal collision/equivalence process; no local profile identifier may be promoted implicitly.

SemanticAnchor: [T-REF-CLOSE-V155]
RELEASE CLOSURE EXECUTION

The v15.5 source build executes the current ReferenceClosureReceipt process across the five current source files. The
scanner version, parsing rules, file hashes, counts and unresolved list are published as an auxiliary release artifact.
PASS is bounded to structural reference/registry closure under those declared rules. It does not certify semantics,
behavior, physical implementation or deployment.
